**Deliverable:** a slot or field marked optional does not block finalize when absent, while required ones still do.

**Grouped scope:**
- **`optional: bool`** on both `Slot` and `Field` (serde default false, skip-serialize-on-false to keep existing schema goldens byte-stable, mirroring `singleton`); `check_slot_present` / `is_author_required` skip the requiredness check when set, at the simple + repeatable enforcement arms.

**Proves:** an absent optional field/slot finalizes **clean**; an absent required field/slot still **blocks**; an absent optional field renders with **no stray fields-block line** (byte-stable absent form). Built against [changelog.md](../design/changelog.md) → engine work #3.

## Increment 4 — doc-level `set: on-create` + `default:` materialization (adr-scoped, isolated)

**Deliverable:** a doc-level (header/simple-section) `set: on-create` date and a `default:` value are materialized at create — honoring `adr`'s long-broken `date` (set-on-create) + `status: proposed` (default) promises. **Isolated** so its front-matter-test churn lands alone (review finding S2).

**Grouped scope:**
- **CLI-side `on_create_doc_fields(schema)`** at create walks header/simple fields for `type: date, set: on-create` (stamp `today_iso`) and `default:` (stamp the value), seeding the instance before render — the **engine stays clock-free** (the CLI owns the clock, exactly as the proven item-level path does).
- **Absorb the front-matter-test churn** — the many tests pinning empty front-matter on `create` update in this increment, in isolation.

**Proves:** a freshly-created `adr` carries today's `date` + `status: proposed`; an `adr` create round-trips with materialized header; the churn is quarantined from the engine-recursion work. Built against [changelog.md](../design/changelog.md) → engine work #4.

## Increment 5 — the `changelog` doctype + `record-change` workflow + `single-task` fold-in (the acceptance)

**Deliverable:** a new project authors a managed `changelog` singleton through jigc end-to-end — the cold-create → warm-append byte-stable proof (flow 24). Consumes increments 1–3 (the schema can't load without `Leaf::Repeatable`, uses the multi-word section + optional field).

**Grouped scope:**
- **`changelog` schema** (dev pack, `singleton`, `location: changelog/`, edge-free) — `## Unreleased Changes` (single-level repeatable change-groups: `category` enum + `notes` slot) + `## Releases` (two-level: release item = version title + item-level `date` set-on-create + optional `link` field + nested `changes` change-groups). Authored *with* its driver (earn-from-driver).
- **`record-change` workflow** (dev pack, `creates-task: true, selectable: false`, `allows-create: [{type: changelog, as: changelog}]`) + its steps — idempotent-create the singleton → `add-item` a release + nested change-groups → `set-slot` notes → finalize promotes.
- **`single-task` fold-in** — add `{type: changelog, as: change}` to `single-task`'s `allows-create` (beside `{type: adr, as: decision}`), pack-local; **regression watch** that the `single-task` ADR-create + superseding-decision acceptance still pass.
- **Flow 24 acceptance** — spiked against the rebuilt binary (the multi-level authoring path is net-new); the nested-leaf finding address confirmed at the spike (B1/S1).

**Proves:** worked-examples [flow 24](../design/worked-examples.md#24-changelog-authoring--cold-create--warm-append-a-multi-level-singleton-doctype-expansion) — `jigc start --workflow record-change` mints a task (off-router), cold-creates the `changelog` singleton with a nested-group release, finalize promotes byte-stable; a second run warm-re-creates (copy-in preserves the prior release), appends a new release, re-promotes byte-stable with both present; a half-authored nested entry blocks, an absent optional `link` finalizes clean, the multi-word section round-trips, and the `single-task` fold-in appends an unreleased entry through the create-gate. The Flow-A-only bound holds (an existing `CHANGELOG.md` still routes `needs-reconcile`). Built against [changelog.md](../design/changelog.md).

**Outcome (2026-06-14).** All five increments built by the `milestone-build` harness, each **independently validated clean (0 fix rounds)**. The four engine lifts landed as designed (`Leaf::Repeatable` recursive parse/render/validate + parent-scoped path locator; multi-word section-id parser fix; `optional:` flag on `Slot`+`Field`; doc-level `set: on-create`+`default:` honoring `adr`'s broken `date`/`status`); the `changelog` doctype + `record-change` driver + `single-task` fold-in ship. **Milestone-completion audit (code-review + e2e): e2e overall-pass** — all six flow-24 bars + four lifts proven through the rebuilt binary. **2 findings auto-fixed** (not deferred): a HIGH scope-honesty defect — the nested-address grammar was inconsistent so the shipped `author-change.yaml` step directed the agent to an address the binary rejected (the test masked it) — fixed by converging on the settled section-qualified form (`03bc4b8`); and 2× LOW incomplete `on_create` materialization (`ab0828e`). **1 newly-found pre-existing finding logged** (not an M22 regression; its own focused fix): an empty-slot+field-group item renders byte-unstably in the minted-but-unfilled intermediate (never a committed state — a required slot blocks finalize) — scheduled in [decisions-pending.md](decisions-pending.md). Gate green (fmt/clippy/476 tests/build). Commits `b1293b8…ab0828e`. **Honest bound holds:** Flow-A only — an existing `CHANGELOG.md` still routes `needs-reconcile` until auto-migration (G1). [DECISIONS.md](../DECISIONS.md) → M22 completion.

## M23 · auto-migration (G1) — rewrite a foreign `CHANGELOG.md` to conformant shape and adopt it — changelog-first

**Created 2026-06-14**, the second of the two milestones the paired post-M21 deferral was **split** into (G2 shipped as M22). Adds the **transform** arm to existing-project ingestion: a non-conformant foreign doc is **rewritten into conformant shape + adopted**, where M9/M21's [detect-and-route](../design/project-setup.md) only classifies + routes. Serves **Flow B** (an existing project). Baseline verified by 3 `capability-auditor`s + 4 `gap-detector`s exercising a **HEAD-built** binary (the pinned binary is *not* HEAD — it carries a later composed methodology pack; re-pin at build); survived an independent pre-decompose design review (**2 BLOCKING + 3 SHOULD + 3 CONSIDER, all baked** — B1 retire-transaction rollback, B2 enum-check-shape + flow-25 address, S1 path-collision guard, S2 finalize-`--approve` gate, S3 diff source). **The load-bearing decision (settled before planning): Framing A — the determinism boundary stays INTACT.** The **LLM rewrites** the foreign prose through the existing write verbs; the **CLI strict-parses + conformance-gates + adopts iff conformant** (the strict parser is the *sole* structural authority; **no CLI fuzzy heading-mapping is built**; the [VISION boundary table](../VISION.md#the-determinism-boundary) is unchanged). The charter's original "relaxes the boundary / fuzzy-mapping machinery" framing is **superseded** ([DECISIONS.md](../DECISIONS.md) → 2026-06-14 M23 planning). **Proves:** an existing non-conformant foreign `CHANGELOG.md` (today `Unmanaged` at root) is migrated through `jigc migrate CHANGELOG.md --as changelog` → the LLM rewrites it to canonical shape through the CLI → a human review gate shows the fidelity diff → on `--approve`, finalize writes `changelog/changelog.md`, retires the foreign original, and adopts — byte-stable, with the reds (a non-conformant category blocks, a human-reject is byte-safe) (worked-examples flow 25). **Changelog only** (**M24** hardens this changelog reference; the location-bearing doctypes `adr`/`spec`/`prd`/`arch-doc` + code-inferred docs generalize at **M25**). **Honest bounds:** migration fidelity is human-judged not CLI-verified; KaC preamble / per-release summary / `[Unreleased]` compare-link are accepted drops; version-slug collisions block (accept-and-block). **The binary changes** (a new `migrate` verb + a source seam + an enum-on-id-from validate lift + a `FinalizePlan` retirement field + a finalize `--approve` gate), so it must be **rebuilt + re-pinned** (`cargo install --path crates/cli` + copy to both bin dirs) before any exercise. Goal: unblock the **Flow-B existing-project live test** against a real foreign-doc corpus. Design home: [design/auto-migration.md](../design/auto-migration.md). Decomposition below.

## Milestone 23 — auto-migration (G1, changelog-first): decomposition

**Planned + settled 2026-06-14** ([DECISIONS.md](../DECISIONS.md) → 2026-06-14 M23 planning; design [auto-migration.md](../design/auto-migration.md)). **Four increments, risk-first + linear:** the headline net-new **migrate spine** first (verb + source seam + migration workflow, staged-only — fail-fast on whether the LLM-rewrite-through-CLI shape composes and yields a conformant staged doc), then the small independent **enum-on-id-from** conformance-rigor lift (the "adopt iff conformant" guarantee the adopt gate leans on), then the riskiest **commit boundary** (the review gate + the first byte-destructive retire + adopt, transactional), then the **flow-25 end-to-end acceptance + the measured corpus run** that consumes all three. Every gate carries the forward-review **red obligation** (fire-and-block / round-trip on real input before green — the vacuously-green-managed-doc failure class) and spikes the **cold/empty** state (an empty or single-release foreign file). **Byte-stability confirmation (review C2):** Increment 1 confirms the migration author-sequence never re-parses a minted-empty *slotted* item (the changelog change-group is slot-only, release is field-only — neither is the slot+field-group shape the M22-logged finding lives in, so it's a confirm-not-fix; if a re-parse-between-verbs path appears, halt and route to the logged fix).

## Increment 1 — the `migrate` spine: verb + CLI-owned source seam + migration workflow (staged-only)

**Deliverable:** `jigc migrate <path> --as changelog` reaches a root `Unmanaged` foreign `CHANGELOG.md`, mints an **off-router** migration task (the `record-change` shape: `creates-task: true`, `selectable: false`, `allows-create: [{type: changelog, as: changelog}]`), stages the foreign bytes, surfaces them into the composed `migrate-changelog` workflow via a **CLI-owned source seam** (a read-only context placeholder, syntactically distinct from a managed-doc `{{@…}}` deref), and the LLM authors the canonical `changelog` through the existing `doc create` / `add-item` / `set-slot` / `set-field` spine. Ends at a **staged, conformant** changelog in the task working area — no commit/retire/adopt yet.

**Grouped scope:** the `migrate` CLI verb + dispatch (`crates/cli/src/cli.rs` + a new `migrate.rs`); the off-router migration task minting + foreign-bytes staging into the task; the source seam (the read-path placeholder + its resolution in `compose.rs`/`data_value.rs`, carrying raw foreign bytes as read-only context); the `migrate-changelog` workflow + its author step (dev-pack, mirrors `record-change`/`author-change`); the historical-`date` authoring path (`set-field …/date` overwrites the on-create stamp — verified at planning).

**Proves (red on real input):** `jigc migrate <real foreign CHANGELOG.md> --as changelog` over a `git init` temp repo mints the task and composes with the **foreign content present** in the emitted workflow (the seam resolves); driving the author spine produces a staged `changelog/changelog.md` that **round-trips byte-stable** (`render(parse(staged)) == staged`), with a multi-release foreign file authored from scratch in one task (cold/empty spike: a single-release and an empty-`[Unreleased]` foreign file both author clean). Built against [auto-migration.md](../design/auto-migration.md) → The mechanism (verb / source seam / migration workflow).

## Increment 2 — enforce the `enum` on `id-from` fields (conformance rigor)

**Deliverable:** the strict conformance gate rejects a change-group whose `category` (an `enum`-typed `id-from`) is outside the enum, so "adopted iff conformant" is real for the one enum the changelog has. A **new** validate code path reads the id-from value off the parsed item **heading** (not `item.fields`) and, when the field declares an `enum`, checks membership by **re-slugging** the heading value against the slug-form enum members — `Fixed`→`fixed` passes, `Performance`→`performance` is rejected; no coercion.

**Grouped scope:** the new id-from enum check in `crates/engine/src/validate.rs` (`check_item_leaves`); pin the emitted finding code (expected `schema-conformance.field-value-conformant`) + the slug-cased address, and update [worked-examples.md](../design/worked-examples.md) flow-25 RED 1 to the pinned shape. **No blast radius:** a non-`enum` `id-from` stays exempt — every other shipped doctype's `id-from` is a `title`/`key` string (verified at planning), so none newly fails (the regression watch asserts this).

**Proves (red on real input):** a foreign `Performance` change-group **blocks** at conformance naming its slug-cased address; a `Fixed`/`Added` change-group **passes** (re-slug membership); the existing doctypes (`adr`/`spec`/`prd`/`arch-doc`/methodology) conformance is **unchanged** (regression watch). Built against [auto-migration.md](../design/auto-migration.md) → Engine/validation work #1.

## Increment 3 — the commit boundary: review gate + retire + adopt (the first destructive op)

**Deliverable:** on a migration task, `jigc task finalize <id>` *without* `--approve` renders the **fidelity diff** ({staged source-seam bytes} vs {the task working-area rendered doc}) and **exits non-zero, committing nothing**; `finalize <id> --approve` executes the transaction: write the canonical doc → **retire the foreign original** → `git` commit → adopt, **all-or-nothing**. Rejection (no `--approve`) is byte-safe — the foreign original is untouched, nothing adopted.

**Grouped scope:** the net-new finalize `--approve` gate + the fidelity-diff render (a finalize-time gate, **not** the compose-time `checkpoint` step kind); the retire step as a `FinalizePlan.retirements` field (`crates/engine/src/finalize.rs`) executed **inside the commit closure** so `git add --all` stages the deletion (`crates/cli/src/task.rs` → `try_execute_finalize_plan`); **extend `rollback_promotions`** to restore each retired path on commit failure (so an approved-but-failed commit never leaves the foreign file deleted with no commit — review B1); the **path-collision guard** (foreign path == canonical managed path → skip retire, rewrite in place — review S1, handles the in-`changelog/` squatter); the adopt-after-write (the existing register-only `index.absorb_doc` + file-state hash).

**Proves (red on real input):** `--approve` on a conformant migration writes `changelog/changelog.md` byte-stable, the foreign original is **gone**, a follow-up `ingest` reports it `adopted`; finalize **without** `--approve` blocks (non-zero, no commit, foreign file intact); a **human-reject** (discard) leaves the foreign original untouched and the edge index unchanged; a **commit-failure-after-approve** (seeded hook reject) **rolls back** the retire (foreign file restored). Cold/empty spike: migrating a single-release file commits + retires clean. Built against [auto-migration.md](../design/auto-migration.md) → The review gate / Retire-the-foreign-original.

## Increment 4 — flow-25 end-to-end acceptance + the measured corpus run (the marquee)

**Deliverable:** the full migrate → review → `--approve` → adopt path proven end-to-end against the **rebuilt** binary (worked-examples flow 25), plus the **migration-quality measure** run over a small real corpus.

**Grouped scope:** a real-binary e2e (`CARGO_BIN_EXE_jigc` over `git init` temp repos) covering the happy path + both reds (the enum-block from inc 2, the byte-safe human-reject from inc 3); the **measured corpus** — 3–5 real public `CHANGELOG.md` files of varying conformance (clean KaC, loosely-structured, non-KaC-categories, multi-release) — scored on the three metrics (round-trip conformance · fidelity-acceptance at the review gate · content-preservation spot-check), recorded alongside the run (the [measurement.md](../design/measurement.md) posture, protocol-counted not engine-emitted).

**Proves:** worked-examples [flow 25](../design/worked-examples.md#25-changelog-migration--rewrite-a-foreign-changelogmd-to-conformant-shape-and-adopt-it-auto-migration-g1) — the transform arm exists under Framing A; strict-parse + adopt iff conformant; the enum is enforced on the id-from; historical dates survive; the review gate is byte-safe both ways; the honest bounds hold (changelog-only, accepted drops, slug-collision blocks). The measured baseline is the milestone's done-bar (unblocking the Flow-B live test). Built against [auto-migration.md](../design/auto-migration.md) → Acceptance + Migration-quality measure.

**Outcome (2026-06-15).** All four increments built (the harness halted once on a transient API 529 mid-inc-2 — not a build failure; on continuation, inc-1's missed pack-inventory bump [`migrate-changelog`+`author-migration`] was fixed `487a7d9`, the orphaned inc-2 T1 enum work committed `8ab9d7e`, then a `skipThrough:2` re-launch built inc-3+inc-4+audit). The migrate spine ships end-to-end: `{{source}}` seam → `jigc migrate <path> --as changelog` → `migrate-changelog` workflow + `author-migration` step → finalize `--approve` review gate → transactional retire + adopt, one commit. **Milestone-completion audit: e2e 9/10 + all six flow-25 acceptance bars pass; code-review's 3 retire-path data-loss findings auto-fixed (`a9731ce`); gate independently re-verified green.** The **1 e2e FAIL** — the in-location squatter end-to-end (a non-conformant file already at `changelog/changelog.md` → `write.wrong-shape`; not an acceptance bar, fails cleanly, no data loss) — is **deferred to M24 + honest-bounded** (the human's call, 2026-06-15): the primary root-`CHANGELOG.md` case (the live Flow-B demand) works fully; over-claiming docs corrected, deferral logged with an M24 trigger ([decisions-pending.md](decisions-pending.md) → in-location-squatter migration). Gate green. Commits `2990ea5…a9731ce`. **Honest bound:** changelog-only; fidelity human-judged; KaC preamble/summary/`[Unreleased]`-link are accepted drops; the in-location squatter deferred. The Flow-B existing-project live test is **unblocked**. Binary rebuilt + re-pinned. [DECISIONS.md](../DECISIONS.md) → 2026-06-15 M23 completion.

## M24 · harden changelog migration to a correct reference — fix the M23 smoke-test friction BEFORE generalizing — ✅ shipped (2026-06-16)

**Created 2026-06-16.** A manual smoke test ran the M23 migrate spine on two real, dateless repos (throwaway clones of `project-delta` — 127 lines, heading-categorised — and `project-gamma` — 607 lines, inline `feat:`/`fix:` prefixes). It **proved the spine works end-to-end at scale** but surfaced a cluster of friction that is **mostly general migration/authoring mechanism**, not changelog-specific — so the human reset the sequence (2026-06-16): **M24 hardens** changelog migration to a correct reference, **then M25 generalizes** the corrected pattern to `adr`/`spec`/`prd`, **then M26 = arch-doc** (code-anchors + the deferred empty-slot+field-group byte-stability fix). Fixing once here avoids N fixes across four doctypes. Baseline verified by 3 `capability-auditor`s + 4 `gap-detector`s exercising the re-pinned binary (two findings came back **sharper than the recorded design**: the in-location squatter fails at the `state.rs` idempotent-create copy-in — a Frankenstein doc — not the write verb as the docs claimed; and the `git add --all` sweep is unbounded without a task change-manifest). **The four human-gated forks settled (all recommended path):** the batch path = **declarative whole-doc** `jigc doc author --from` (Path A — chain the existing splice primitives, *not* an `Instance`/render path that bypasses the create-gate + squatter seam); `remove-item` incl. a new **nested** `remove_nested_item`; write-time validation = id-from-enum **+** the item-field parity gap via **one shared helper**; the in-location squatter **fixed now** (not deferred). Recommended-and-proceeded: CLI migration-mode **date-suppression** (no schema edit), **auto-provisioned formulaic commit** (fields via default/on-create; summary/body via a bounded slot-fill rule), a **heuristic advisory** fidelity summary, **bounded migration-only** `git add` narrowing, `migrate-<doctype>` task name. **No new schemas.** Survived an independent pre-decompose review (2 BLOCKING + 3 SHOULD + 4 CONSIDER, all baked). **The binary changes** (a new `doc author` verb + a write-time-validation lift + `remove_nested_item` + migration-mode discriminator threading + a finalize-path commit/diff/git-add lift), so it must be **rebuilt + re-pinned** before any exercise. Acceptance = the **FULL** project-delta + project-gamma live migration, measured (round-trip conformance / fidelity-acceptance / content-preservation / **agent-call count**) — worked-examples flow 26. Design home: [auto-migration.md](../design/auto-migration.md) → Hardening (M24). Decomposition below.

## Milestone 24 — harden changelog migration (the M23 smoke-test friction): decomposition

**Planned + settled 2026-06-16** ([DECISIONS.md](../DECISIONS.md) → 2026-06-16 M24 planning; design [auto-migration.md](../design/auto-migration.md) → Hardening, [write-commands.md](../design/write-commands.md) → Batch authoring). **Seven increments, risk-first + linear:** the headline net-new **declarative batch** first (fail-fast on the milestone's largest mechanism + the verbosity-at-scale answer the acceptance rides), then the two doctype-general authoring lifts the migration leans on (**write-time validation**, then **remove-item**), then the migration-mode behaviours — **mint-side** (date + naming), the **isolated high-blast squatter** fix, the **finalize-path** cluster — and finally the **guidance + full measured live migration** that consumes them all. Every gate carries the forward-review **red obligation** (fire-and-block / round-trip on real input before green) and spikes the **cold/empty** state. **No new schemas.** **Byte-stability (decompose note):** the declarative batch is the chain-of-splice-primitives path (parse-per-leaf, persist once — byte-identical to the M23-proven per-verb chain minus the no-op intermediate persists), and `remove_nested_item` only splices a block to empty; neither re-renders a minted-empty slotted item, and the changelog has no slot+field-group item — so the M22-logged empty-slot+field-group instability is **confirmed not fired** (re-keyed to M26).

## Increment 1 — `jigc doc author <doctype> --from <payload>`: declarative whole-doc batch authoring (the headline risk)

**Deliverable:** a new **doctype-general** CLI verb that authors a whole instance from one structured payload — applying `create` + N `add-item` + `set-slot`/`set-field` over a **single in-memory buffer**, persisting **once**, atomically. Collapses a 50-release migration from hundreds of round-trips to one. Proven on the changelog at multi-release scale.

**Grouped scope:**
- **The payload format** — a structured input (the resolved "form-marker" open) the CLI parses into an ordered leaf-write sequence; slot values carried as prose, scalar fields inline; the agent authors the payload (owns prose + which-content-goes-where), the CLI places every leaf (boundary intact).
- **The `doc author` verb + dispatch** (`crates/cli/src/doc.rs` + `cli.rs`).
- **The apply engine — Path A (review B1, pinned):** `create` runs through the existing `create_gated` path (so the **create-gate is enforced** and the squatter seam is shared), then each `add-item`/`set-*` **splice primitive is chained over the running buffer with no persist between leaves**; persist once at the end. It does **not** build an `Instance` and `render` (that would bypass byte-preservation + the create-gate/squatter seams).
- **Atomicity** — any leaf error discards the buffer; persist only on full success (rollback = drop the in-memory buffer).

**Proves (red on real input):** a multi-release **dated** changelog authored from one payload round-trips byte-stable **and is byte-identical to the equivalent per-leaf verb chain**; a structurally-malformed payload is **rejected whole** (nothing persisted); the **create-gate still blocks** an un-allowed doctype through the batch; the existing per-leaf verbs are unchanged (regression watch). Cold/empty spike: a single-release and an empty payload both behave. Built against [write-commands.md](../design/write-commands.md) → Batch authoring; [auto-migration.md](../design/auto-migration.md) → Hardening #1.

## Increment 2 — write-time value validation: one shared id-from-enum helper + the item-field parity gap

**Deliverable:** a bad value is rejected **at the write verb**, not deferred to finalize — for `add-item` id-from enums and item/nested-item field values — so a long authoring run (or a batch) fails fast at the point of the mistake.

**Grouped scope:**
- **One shared `slug + enum-membership` helper** called by **both** the `add-item` write path and finalize's `check_id_from_enum` (`crates/engine/src/validate.rs`) — not a mirrored copy (review S3; drift risk as M25 copies it to four doctypes). The id-from value lives in the item *heading*, so the helper re-slugs and checks membership.
- **Close the item-field parity gap** — wire the existing `check_value` into `set_item_field_or_insert` / `set_nested_item_field_or_insert` (`crates/engine/src/write.rs`) so a malformed item/nested `date`/enum is rejected at write time (the logged 2026-06-07 gap, `doc.rs:932`).
- The batch (inc 1) inherits mid-batch rejection.

**Proves (red on real input):** `add-item --title "Improvements"` (∉ enum) **blocks at write** naming the slug-cased address; a malformed item `date` **blocks at write**; the emitted finding **code is identical** to finalize's (the address leaf-suffix may differ by call site); a batch payload carrying a bad category is rejected; the other shipped doctypes' conformance is **unchanged** (regression watch). Built against [write-commands.md](../design/write-commands.md) → Two check times; [auto-migration.md](../design/auto-migration.md) → Hardening #3.

## Increment 3 — `jigc doc remove-item <addr>` (top-level + nested `remove_nested_item`)

**Deliverable:** a mis-authored item — a top-level release **or** a nested change-group — can be removed without discarding the whole task (a general recovery verb).

**Grouped scope:**
- **Engine `remove_nested_item`** — the parent-scoped id-chain locator (the dual of `add_nested_item`); the existing `remove_item` is top-level-only and cannot reach a nested change-group (the smoke test's own wrong-category example).
- **CLI `remove-item` verb + dispatch** resolving both `#section/item` and the nested `#section/item/child/leaf`-class address; byte-stable splice-to-empty.

**Proves (red on real input):** removing a **top-level** release and a **nested** change-group each round-trips byte-stable; the nested address resolves to the intended item (never a same-anchor sibling); a mis-named target routes a clean block (no wrong-item delete). Built against [write-commands.md](../design/write-commands.md); graduates the `remove-item` deferral ([decisions-pending.md](decisions-pending.md) → add-item family).

## Increment 4 — migration-mode mint: dateless date-suppression + `migrate-<doctype>` task name

**Deliverable:** a migration over a **dateless** source renders releases with **no date** (not the migration day), and the migration task is named `migrate-<doctype>` (no collision with `--task`/the doctype id).

**Grouped scope:**
- **The migration-mode discriminator** — read `read_source_path(task_dir)` at the mint/add-item seam; `on_create_item_fields` (`crates/cli/src/doc.rs`) **suppresses** the `set: on-create` date stamp in migration mode (authoring still stamps today; an explicit payload date still writes). **No schema change** (an absent `set: on-create` date already finalizes clean).
- **Task naming** — the empty-intent mint (`crates/cli/src/start.rs`) slugs the task id to `migrate-<doctype>`, not the bare doctype name.

**Proves (red on real input):** a migration batch/`add-item` of a dateless release renders **no date line** and finalizes clean; an authoring (`record-change`) release still stamps **today**; an explicit date in the payload still writes; the task id is `migrate-changelog` and a second migration of the same doctype does not serial-collide on the doctype name. Built against [auto-migration.md](../design/auto-migration.md) → Hardening #6/#9b; [changelog.md](../design/changelog.md) → date posture.

## Increment 5 — in-location squatter: seed the working area blank over an occupied canonical path (isolated, high-blast)

**Deliverable:** `jigc migrate` on a non-conformant file **already at** the canonical managed path authors **end-to-end** (no Frankenstein doc), while non-migration create-or-update still copies the committed doc in. **Isolated** — it touches the load-bearing `state::create` copy-in (the M16 clobber-fix), so its careful red-test lands alone.

**Grouped scope:**
- **Extend the `source-path == promote-destination` discriminator from the retire side to the create side** — at `state::create` step 4 (`crates/engine/src/state.rs`), **seed blank** (skip the copy-in) when a migration task's recorded `source-path` canonically equals the committed singleton's canonical path. **The discriminator is the migration source-path match, never singleton-ness** (else it resurrects the M16 clobber-fix the copy-in exists for).
- **Share one path-normalization routine** across the create-side and retire-side guards (lift the finalize-private `lexical_normalize` to a shared home — review C1) so seed-blank and retire-skip never disagree.

**Proves (red on real input):** migrating an in-location squatter **seeds blank, authors, finalizes byte-stable** (the M23 e2e FAIL now passes); a non-migration `doc create` on a committed singleton **still copies-in** (the M16 clobber-fix intact — regression watch); the create-side seed-blank and retire-side skip agree on the same path normalization. Built against [auto-migration.md](../design/auto-migration.md) → Path-collision guard / Hardening #8; [DECISIONS.md](../DECISIONS.md) (B1 create-gate, C1).

## Increment 6 — migration finalize-path hardening: commit auto-provision + git-add narrowing + structural fidelity summary

**Deliverable:** a migration finalizes **without** the agent authoring a commit doc, commits **only** the migration's own changes (never unrelated user WIP), and the review gate shows a **release-level** fidelity summary.

**Grouped scope:**
- **Auto-provision the migration commit** — `type`/`scope` via the **existing** doc-level `default:`/`set: on-create` field materialization (no boundary crossing); `summary`/`body` via a CLI templated fill governed by the **bounded slot-fill rule** (content is a pure deterministic function of source path + doctype, never judgment — review S2).
- **Narrow the migration finalize `git add`** to **{promote + retire + jigc's own tracked config (`.jigc/config/`, `.jigc/.gitignore`)}** — never arbitrary user WIP; non-migration tasks keep `git add --all` (the general dirty-tree redesign stays deferred). The jigc-config layer is included because it is git-tracked and `setup` never commits (review B2).
- **Structural fidelity summary at the review gate** (`crates/cli/src/render.rs`) — parse the canonical rewrite for release ids; **heuristic version-scan** of the foreign side; render "source releases absent from the rewrite: …" as an **advisory, fuzzy-labeled** aid that feeds **no** structural decision (review C4; Framing A intact).

**Proves (red on real input):** a migration finalizes clean with **no manual commit authoring** (the formulaic `docs(changelog): …` message lands); an unrelated untracked file is **NOT** in the migration commit while `.jigc/config/` **IS**; the review gate without `--approve` shows the release-delta summary; the summary feeds nothing structural. Built against [auto-migration.md](../design/auto-migration.md) → Hardening #4/#5/#9a, The review gate; [DECISIONS.md](../DECISIONS.md) (B2, S2, C4).

## Increment 7 — the marquee: category map+merge guidance + the full project-delta+project-gamma live migration, measured

**Deliverable:** the `author-migration` guidance instructs category map+**merge** onto the enum (and infer-from-prefix when headingless) and drives the **declarative batch**; the **FULL** project-delta + project-gamma live migration runs end-to-end on throwaway clones, **measured** on the four facts — worked-examples flow 26 (the milestone's done-bar).

**Grouped scope:**
- **Rewrite `crates/cli/pack/steps/author-migration.yaml` guidance** — map foreign categories onto the `category` enum **and merge many-to-one** (two foreign categories → one member can't be two groups; the enum is the id-from, so they collide), infer the member from `feat:`/`fix:` prefixes when a foreign file has **no** category headings, and author via `jigc doc author --from` (the batch).
- **Less-terse `setup`/`ingest` output** (#9c).
- **The real-binary flow-26 e2e** (happy path + the hardening reds — write-time enum block, `remove-item` recovery, dateless no-date, squatter end-to-end, auto-commit, structural summary) + **the measured run** over the two named repos (round-trip conformance / fidelity-acceptance / content-preservation / **agent-call count**), recorded ([measurement.md](../design/measurement.md) posture). **Rebuild + re-pin** the binary as the prerequisite.

**Proves:** worked-examples [flow 26](../design/worked-examples.md#26-changelog-migration-hardened--the-full-real-repo-live-migration-through-the-batch-path-m24) — both real dateless changelogs migrate fully via one batch payload each; dateless releases render no date; the write-time enum reject, `remove-item` recovery, auto-provisioned commit, structural release-delta summary, and in-location squatter are all exercised; the **agent-call count** is recorded and is a large cut vs per-leaf; **no new schemas**; Framing A intact. Built against [worked-examples.md](../design/worked-examples.md) → flow 26; [auto-migration.md](../design/auto-migration.md) → Migration-quality measure.

**Outcome (2026-06-16).** All seven increments built — the build harness halted **twice, neither a design failure**: (1) at Inc-3 T2 on a genuine planning gap (the pre-existing top-level `remove_item` left a dangling EOF blank line on the *last* item — the planner's "reuse the verified `remove_item`" assumption missed it wasn't byte-stable for the last item; resolved by an inserted engine-hardening commit mirroring `remove_nested_item`'s edge, `6fce01e`, then T2 via a `build-executor`, `d9b167e`); (2) an orchestration plumbing halt (a stringified-`args` dropped `skipThrough`, so the resume tried to re-plan already-built Inc 1 — the `build-planner` correctly refused; fixed in the workflow script, no repo change). The **declarative batch** (`jigc doc author --from`, Path A — chain the splice primitives over one buffer, persist once) ships and is **byte-identical to the per-leaf chain**; write-time validation (one shared id-from-enum helper + the item-field parity gap); `remove-item` incl. nested; migration-mode date-suppression; the in-location squatter blank-seed (M16 clobber-fix intact); auto-provisioned commit + migration-scoped `git add` + the advisory structural fidelity summary; `migrate-<doctype>` naming; map+merge guidance. **No new schemas.** **Milestone-completion audit: e2e overall-PASS** (all 7 increments + flow-26 through the rebuilt binary; determinism + non-changelog regression clean) **+ code-review found no blocking defects.** The **measured done-bar** (the full live migration, [completions/artifacts/M24/migration-quality-measure.md](../completions/artifacts/M24/migration-quality-measure.md)): **project-delta** 13 releases / 20 change-groups, **project-gamma** 80 releases / 127 change-groups, each authored in **ONE** batch call, byte-stable, retired+adopted, content fully preserved (53/53 and 433/433 bullets) — the **agent-call count cut from 54→1 and 335→1** (the verbosity fix's payoff). **4 audit findings auto-fixed** (not deferred): a silent slot/field marker-mismatch misroute now rejects loudly (`73a16ac`), migration on-create suppression scoped to the date stamp only (`868642b`), the migration-stage pathspecs guarded on existence so the byte-destructive commit can't abort (`b189b52`), and the review-gate release-delta summary always renders (`(none)` when empty, `a60d4ef`). Gate green (fmt/clippy/**1162 tests**/build). Commits `73a16ac…a60d4ef` (built atop the Inc 1–5 commits `3b6a26d…4df5963`). Binary rebuilt + re-pinned (both bin dirs). **Honest bound:** changelog-only — M25 generalizes the corrected pattern to `adr`/`spec`/`prd`, M26 = arch-doc (+ the empty-slot+field-group byte-stability fix). [DECISIONS.md](../DECISIONS.md) → 2026-06-16 M24 completion.

## M25 · generalize the corrected migration pattern to the location-bearing doctypes adr/spec/prd

**Created 2026-06-17**, the third of the migration arc (M23 spine → M24 hardened the changelog reference → **M25 generalizes** the corrected pattern to the **location-bearing, multi-instance** doctypes `adr`/`spec`/`prd`; **M26 = arch-doc** + the deferred empty-slot+field-group byte-stability fix). The `migrate` verb / source seam / review gate / retire transaction / declarative `doc author --from` batch are doctype-parameterized and **proven on the changelog singleton**; M25's work is the **shape differences**, baseline-verified by 3 `capability-auditor`s + 4 `gap-detector`s **exercising** the HEAD-built binary (not reasoning by analogy — the M3 trap). **Framing A intact** (LLM rewrites foreign prose through the write verbs; the CLI strict-parses + conformance-gates + adopts iff conformant; no CLI fuzzy heading-mapping). Survived an independent pre-decompose design review (**1 BLOCKING + 4 SHOULD + 4 CONSIDER, all baked or consciously dispositioned** — B1 prd-greenfield-driver scope, S1 finalize-promote clobber guard, S2 two-call-site ref-shape codes, S3 slug-grammar comma-footgun close, S4 out-of-set-supersedes drop). **The four human-gated forks settled** ([DECISIONS.md](../DECISIONS.md) → 2026-06-17 M25 planning): migration model = **one-file-one-task, sequential** (per-file task-id; NOT a dir sweep); **prd repeatable-requirements** (no engine code, but rewrites the greenfield Flow-1 driver too); **write-time ref-shape check + widen `adr.supersedes` to `0..*`**; acceptance corpus = **real MADR ADR (with supersession) + real spec + synthesized-labeled prd**. **Two handover claims corrected by exercise:** a *dateless* foreign adr fabricates today (the doc-level on-create materializer takes no migration flag — M24 scoped suppression item-level only); the M24 in-location-squatter fix is singleton-gated and doesn't apply. **The binary changes** (per-instance migration task-id; three `migrate-{adr,spec,prd}` workflows + per-doctype author-migration steps; a write-time ref-shape check; doc-level date-suppression threading; the `adr.supersedes` 0..* widen + prd repeatable-requirements schema deltas + the Flow-1 greenfield rewrite; the finalize-promote clobber guard), so it must be **rebuilt + re-pinned** (both bin dirs) before any live exercise. Acceptance = worked-examples flow 27, measured (round-trip conformance · fidelity-acceptance · content-preservation · agent-call count). **No code-inferred docs / whole-repo inference** (M25+/north-star, out). Design home: [auto-migration.md](../design/auto-migration.md) → Generalizing to adr/spec/prd (M25). Decomposition below.

## Milestone 25 — generalize migration (adr/spec/prd): decomposition

**Planned + settled 2026-06-17** ([DECISIONS.md](../DECISIONS.md) → 2026-06-17 M25 planning; design [auto-migration.md](../design/auto-migration.md) → Generalizing to adr/spec/prd, [worked-examples.md](../design/worked-examples.md) → flow 27). **Seven increments, risk-first + linear:** the headline unverified-reuse risks first — the **migration spine generalizes to one multi-instance doctype** (per-instance task-id + the `migrate-adr` workflow), then **edge migration** (the ref-shape check + `supersedes` 0..* + ordering/drop, the path changelog never exercised) — then the migration-mode correctness fixes (**doc-level date-suppression**, the **finalize-promote clobber guard**), then the **prd schema + greenfield-driver rewrite**, then the **remaining two doctype workflows**, and finally the **real measured corpus** that consumes them all. Every gate carries the forward-review **red obligation** (fire-and-block / round-trip on real input before green) and spikes the **cold/empty** state. **Byte-stability (decompose note):** the M22-logged empty-slot+field-group instability is **confirmed not fired** on adr/spec/prd (re-checked at M25 planning — spec's slot+field `criteria` item carries no on-create field to materialize at mint, so it round-trips byte-stable; stays keyed to M26). **Pre-existing bound accepted:** migrated titles render as the slug in the H1 (the title→H1 round-trip fix is out of scope — wide golden blast radius).

## Increment 1 — per-instance migration task-id + the `migrate-adr` workflow (the spine generalizes, headline risk)

**Deliverable:** a foreign ADR migrates end-to-end through `jigc migrate <path> --as adr` — minting a **per-file** migration task (so a corpus of N ADRs migrates sequentially, not blocked by the singleton task-id collision) and a per-title-slug managed doc at `decisions/`, retired + adopted, byte-stable. Edge-free at this increment.

**Grouped scope:**
- **Per-instance migration task-id** — derive the mint id-source from the source file (`migrate-<doctype>-<slug(stem)>`) in `crates/cli/src/start.rs` (the fixed `migrate-<doctype>` at `:114` is the singleton blocker). **(I)-pick at pickup:** uniqueness for same-stem-different-dir files (path-aware slug vs mint-suffix loop — `mint_task` hard-rejects, never suffixes). Blast radius is contained (source-path/retire/squatter read the task *dir*; `{{task.id}}` + `commit:<id>` slug follow).
- **The `migrate-adr.yaml` workflow** (the `migrate-changelog` shape: source seam → author step → migrate-finalize) + a **doctype-specific `author-migration` guidance step for adr** (Status/Context/Decision/Consequences heading map; foreign-status→3-enum mapping). The shipped changelog author step is changelog-shaped, **not** a reusable shell.

**Proves (red on real input):** a 2nd `jigc migrate <other>.md --as adr` **no longer collides** (per-file task-id); a real foreign ADR migrates → per-title slug at `decisions/`, foreign original retired, adopted, byte-stable; a foreign status outside the enum is **mapped** (or blocks at the write-time enum check). Cold spike: migrate into an empty `decisions/`. Built against [auto-migration.md](../design/auto-migration.md) → Migration model + Per-doctype workflows.

## Increment 2 — edge migration: write-time ref-shape check + `adr.supersedes` 0..* + ordering/drop guidance

**Deliverable:** a set of cross-referencing foreign ADRs migrates with its `supersedes` edges wired + resolved at finalize, and a malformed ref is caught **at the write verb** (not a misleading finalize dangle) — the path changelog (edge-free) never exercised.

**Grouped scope:**
- **Write-time ref-shape check** in the shared `check_value` (`crates/engine/src/write.rs`): a ref value must be `<type>:<slug>` with `type` matching the field's `to:` AND `<slug>` matching the slug grammar (each element of a list ref). **Two call sites** (review S2): `set_field_validated` → `write.malformed-value`; the conformance gate (`validate.rs`) → `schema-conformance.field-value-conformant`. Validating the slug body **catches the unbracketed comma form** (review S3), closing that footgun at write.
- **Widen `adr.supersedes` `0..1`→`0..*`** (inverse `superseded-by` likewise) — cardinality is unenforced today (a bracket-list already authors clean); re-pin churn: `schema.rs:636`, the adr snapshot, `validate_envelope.rs:457`.
- **adr guidance: the dependency-ordering contract** (migrate + finalize a supersession *target* before the ADR that supersedes it — the forward-ref resolves against the committed store) + **the out-of-set drop** (a `supersedes` whose target won't be migrated is dropped to prose, not a forever-dangling ref — review S4).

**Proves (red on real input):** a bare slug / wrong-type / comma-form `supersedes` **blocks at write** as `write.malformed-value`; B-`supersedes`-A **resolves** when A is committed first, **blocks** (`ref-resolves` / `field-value-conformant`) when the target dangles; a 2-element bracket-list authors + validates clean; out-of-set targets drop to prose; other ref-bearing doctypes (`arch-doc.cites`) are unbroken (regression). Built against [auto-migration.md](../design/auto-migration.md) → Edge migration + Write-time ref-shape check.

## Increment 3 — doc-level date-suppression (the dateless-adr false-history fix)

**Deliverable:** a *dateless* foreign ADR migrates with **no date** rendered, not the migration day fabricated as false history — the doc-level twin of M24's item-level fix.

**Grouped scope:**
- Thread the migration discriminator into `on_create_doc_fields` (`crates/cli/src/doc.rs:765`, today flagless) so a `type: date, set: on-create` **header** field skips the today-stamp in migration mode (mirror the proven item-level `on_create_item_fields(…, migration)`). Both call sites (`run_create`, `run_author`) pass the flag.

**Proves (red on real input):** a dateless adr migration renders **no date line** (no fabricated today); an **explicit** payload date still survives (overwritable on-create, unchanged); a non-migration `doc create adr` still stamps today (regression); spec/prd (no date field) unaffected. Built against [auto-migration.md](../design/auto-migration.md) → Doc-level date-suppression; [DECISIONS.md](../DECISIONS.md) → M25 fork-C recommend-and-proceed.

## Increment 4 — finalize-promote clobber guard (no silent data loss)

**Deliverable:** a migration (or any create) whose promote destination already holds a committed managed doc **blocks at finalize-promote** instead of silently clobbering it — covering the in-location squatter *and* a title-slug collision across the corpus.

**Grouped scope:**
- A finalize-promote guard (review S1): block when a **create-provenance** doc's promote destination already holds a committed managed doc — **regardless of conformance or migration-ness** (the data-loss case for a colliding corpus is conformant-over-conformant). The non-singleton `create` does not check the committed store at the mint site (`state.rs` step 3 working-area-only; step 4 `&& schema.singleton`), so this is the right seam.

**Proves (red on real input):** a 2nd ADR whose title slugs to an already-committed slug **blocks at finalize-promote** (no clobber); an in-location squatter at the canonical path blocks; a normal first-time migration promote is unaffected (regression). Bounded: the squatter case is off-canonical-foreign-path-bounded. Built against [auto-migration.md](../design/auto-migration.md) → Honest bounds (the clobber guard).

## Increment 5 — prd repeatable-requirements + the greenfield Flow-1 rewrite

**Deliverable:** `prd`'s `requirements` becomes a **repeatable section** (per-requirement `title` field + `statement` slot), and the existing project-setup **greenfield** authoring is rewritten to author requirements as per-requirement items — so both migration *and* greenfield produce individually-addressable requirements.

**Grouped scope:**
- **Schema:** `prd.yaml` `requirements` slot → repeatable (mirroring `spec.criteria` minus the code-anchor); `vision`/`context` stay fixed slots; correct the stale schema comment.
- **Greenfield driver rewrite (review B1):** `crates/cli/pack/steps/author-prd.yaml` (the `<<author: {{task.brief#requirements}}>>` slot directive → per-requirement item authoring) + `develop-idea.yaml` if it references the slot.
- **Blast radius:** `crates/cli/tests/flow11_new_project_arc.rs` (the Flow-1 acceptance) + the prd render golden.

**Proves (red on real input):** the prd schema round-trips byte-stable (repeatable requirements, no empty-slot+field-group instability — no on-create field at mint); **flow11 greenfield authors repeatable requirements** and finalizes clean; a multi-requirement prd authored via `doc author --from` (`items:`) round-trips. Built against [auto-migration.md](../design/auto-migration.md) → prd; [decisions-pending.md](decisions-pending.md) → prd repeatable (graduated).

## Increment 6 — `migrate-spec` + `migrate-prd` workflows + guidance steps

**Deliverable:** the remaining two doctype migration paths — a foreign spec migrates to `specs/` (criteria, optional `maps-to-test` clean); a foreign prd migrates to `prds/` (repeatable requirements via items).

**Grouped scope:**
- `migrate-spec.yaml` + a spec author-migration guidance step (goal/context slots + criteria extraction; `maps-to-test` is optional — a migrated spec with no test anchors finalizes clean).
- `migrate-prd.yaml` + a prd author-migration guidance step (vision/context slots + the repeatable `requirements` items, post-inc-5 schema).

**Proves (red on real input):** a real-but-idiosyncratic foreign spec migrates end-to-end (criteria without `maps-to-test` finalize clean); a synthesized foreign prd migrates with per-requirement items; both per-file task-id'd, retired + adopted, byte-stable. Built against [auto-migration.md](../design/auto-migration.md) → Per-doctype workflows + Honest bounds (spec, prd).

## Increment 7 — the real measured corpus (acceptance, flow 27)

**Deliverable:** the milestone done-bar — a **real foreign corpus** migrated end-to-end and measured: a real **MADR/Nygard ADR** set (with supersession chains, exercising the edge-wiring + ordering), a **real-but-idiosyncratic spec**, and a **synthesized-and-labeled prd**, on throwaway clones.

**Grouped scope:**
- The live migration of the sourced corpus through the rebuilt + re-pinned binary; measured on the four facts (round-trip conformance · fidelity-acceptance · content-preservation · **agent-call count**), recorded alongside the run ([completions/artifacts/M25/](../completions/artifacts/M25/)), the prd arm **labeled synthetic**.
- The reds exercised on real input: write-time ref-shape reject, the ordering-contract dangle block, the dateless-date suppression, the finalize-promote clobber guard, the out-of-set supersedes drop, a human-reject byte-safe.

**Proves:** worked-examples [flow 27](../design/worked-examples.md#27-migration-generalized--a-cross-referencing-adr-corpus--a-spec--a-prd-one-file-at-a-time-m25) — a cross-referencing ADR corpus + a spec + a prd migrate one file at a time, edges wired across the set, fidelity measured; the honest bounds (accepted-drops, slug-H1, synthesized prd, off-canonical squatter) hold; Framing A intact. Built against [worked-examples.md](../design/worked-examples.md) → flow 27; [auto-migration.md](../design/auto-migration.md) → Migration-quality measure.

**Outcome (2026-06-17).** All seven increments built. The harness halted **once on a genuine decomposition gap** (Inc-5 T1): flipping `prd.requirements` slot → repeatable breaks `flow11` + the form-D compose test in the same motion, so the schema flip has no green full-gate commit boundary in isolation — the locked design had already declared it one inseparable change. **Re-sequenced** (collapse T1–T3 into one atomic gate-green task, T4 separate — [DECISIONS.md](../DECISIONS.md) → M25 Inc 5 RE-SEQUENCED), driven to completion via `build-executor`s + an independent `increment-validator` PASS, then resumed `skipThrough:5`. The build's own fix-loop also caught a guidance bug at Inc 7 (a dated source's date dropped, `50d825c`). **What shipped:** the per-instance migration task-id (`migrate-<doctype>-<slug(path)>`); three `migrate-{adr,spec,prd}` workflows + per-doctype author-migration guidance steps; the write-time ref-shape check (both `check_value` call sites) + `adr.supersedes` widened `0..*`; doc-level date-suppression (dateless adr no longer fabricates today; explicit dates survive); the finalize-promote clobber guard; prd repeatable-requirements + the greenfield Flow-1 driver rewrite. **Milestone-completion audit: e2e overall-PASS** (all 7 increments + flow-27 through the rebuilt binary; determinism byte-identical across two runs; human-reject byte-safe) **+ code-review found one LOW finding, auto-fixed** (not deferred): a source path that slugs to empty collapsed the per-file task-id back to the singleton `migrate-<doctype>` — fixed with a deterministic `blake3`-prefix fallback (`13aef14`, reproduction now mints distinct ids). **The measured done-bar** ([completions/artifacts/M25/migration-quality-measure.md](../completions/artifacts/M25/migration-quality-measure.md)): a **real MADR ADR set** (`log4brains`, 4 files, **two supersession chains**) + a **real idiosyncratic spec** (semver, 11 criteria) + a **synthesized-labeled prd** (4 requirements), each migrated **one file at a time** — **6/6 round-trip-conformant** (parse + conformance-gate + adopt, byte-stable, `ingest` idempotent), **6/6 fidelity-accepted**, content preserved within the accepted-drops bound, the supersession edges resolved across the set via the committed-store ordering contract, and the **authoring agent-call count cut 62 leaf-writes → 6 batch calls**. Gate green (fmt/clippy/**~510 engine + all cli targets**/build). Commits `4dd20ea…13aef14`. Binary rebuilt + re-pinned (both bin dirs). **Honest bounds:** slug-as-H1 (title→H1 round-trip out of scope); adr accepted-drops (Considered Options / Decision Drivers / Pros-and-Cons); prd corpus synthetic; non-singleton in-location squatter off-canonical-path-bounded. **Next: M26 = arch-doc** (code-anchors + the deferred empty-slot+field-group byte-stability fix). [DECISIONS.md](../DECISIONS.md) → 2026-06-17 M25 completion.

## M26 · generalize the corrected migration pattern to arch-doc + the byte-stability fix

**Created 2026-06-18**, the **fourth and last** of the migration arc (M23 spine → M24 hardened the changelog reference → M25 generalized to `adr`/`spec`/`prd` → **M26 = arch-doc**, the last and hardest named doctype — its repeatable `components` carry **both a `description` slot and an `implemented-by` code anchor**, and those anchors must resolve against real code). Closing it makes all five named doctypes migratable and the named doctype graph complete for migration. **Scope:** M26 migrates a foreign architecture *document*; it does **not** infer one from the codebase (the north-star "code-inferred docs", explicitly out). The `arch-doc` schema + driving workflow shipped at M13; M26 adds only the migration *arm* + the long-deferred byte-stability fix.

**Baseline-verified by exercise** (3 `capability-auditor`s + 4 `gap-detector`s on the re-pinned binary sha `856f84fab4cc`; survived an independent pre-decompose review — 0 BLOCKING + 2 SHOULD + 5 CONSIDER, all baked). **The migration arm is pure pack-config — two yaml files** (`workflows/migrate-arch-doc.yaml` + `steps/author-migration-arch-doc.yaml`), **zero engine/CLI/schema change**: `migrate --as <doctype>` routes by string interpolation (no Rust enumeration), the schema is migration-ready as shipped, and the spine (verb / source seam / declarative batch / review gate / retire+adopt / clobber guard / write-time ref-shape check) is inherited and proven on four doctypes. **The genuinely-new work** is (a) the **byte-stability fix** — the empty-slot+field-group `render(parse(x))!=x` *does* fire through the per-leaf batch (correcting the M24/M25 "parse-once" note) but is masked from commit by `required-slot-present`, so it is a **#1-risk paydown decoupled from migration** (isolated, non-gating); (b) **doc↔code on a *migrated* doc** — the first migration to author a *resolving* code anchor (M25 omitted them), with the **mandated blocking red**; (c) the **guidance** mapping foreign prose onto arch-doc's required slots + the bracketed in-store-only `cites`. **Four human-gated forks settled** ([DECISIONS.md](../DECISIONS.md) → 2026-06-18 M26 planning): **C1** hybrid corpus (real foreign + synthesized-over-jigc-Rust), **C2** mandate ≥2 resolving anchors + the red, **C3** write-time code-anchor shape check NOT built (finalize-only, bound recorded), **C4** byte-fix = Increment 1, isolated, non-gating, direction B. **The binary changes** (the writer empty-slot render + the two embedded pack workflows), so it must be **rebuilt + re-pinned** (both bin dirs; `cargo clean -p cli` so `include_dir!` re-embeds the new workflow/step) before any live exercise. Acceptance = worked-examples flow 28, measured (round-trip conformance · fidelity-acceptance · content-preservation · agent-call count). Design home: [auto-migration.md](../design/auto-migration.md) → Generalizing to arch-doc (M26); the byte fix → [parsing.md](parsing.md) → Round-trip guarantees. Decomposition below.

## Milestone 26 — arch-doc migration + the empty-slot byte-stability fix: decomposition

**Planned + settled 2026-06-18** ([DECISIONS.md](../DECISIONS.md) → 2026-06-18 M26 planning; design [auto-migration.md](../design/auto-migration.md) → Generalizing to arch-doc, [worked-examples.md](../design/worked-examples.md) → flow 28). **Three increments, risk-first + linear:** the **isolated byte-stability fix first** (a #1-risk paydown on a clean substrate, non-gating — leads because it is cheap, isolated, and has render-golden blast radius best retired alone), then the **migration arm** (the two pack yaml files + the guidance, the milestone's substance), then the **measured hybrid acceptance** that consumes both. Every gate carries the forward-review **red obligation** (fire-and-block / round-trip on real input before green). **No schema/field-type change.**

### Increment 1 — empty-slot+field-group byte-stability fix (engine, isolated, non-gating)

**Deliverable:** the writer canonicalizes an empty present-slot (`slot: Some("")`) to render identically to an absent slot (`slot: None`) — direction B in `render_item_at` (`crates/engine/src/write.rs`): `let slot_empty = item.slot.as_deref().is_none_or(|s| s.trim().is_empty()); let slotless = slot_empty && item.slots.is_empty();`. So a minted-but-unfilled slot+field-group item (arch-doc's component shape) round-trips byte-stable, retiring the M22→M26-keyed `render(parse(x)) != x` #1-risk violation.

**Grouped scope:** the 1-line predicate change; the **2 engine fixture rewrites** (`write::nested_roundtrip::cold_fill_one_nested_leaf_at_a_time_is_byte_stable` + `write::nested_locator::top_level_field_write_after_nested_child_lands_in_parent_region` — both changelog nested change-group literals, the three-blank form → one-blank, review-verified as the *only* two that flip: 503 pass / exactly these 2 fail before the fixtures are updated); the **~8 stale item-path comments** in `write.rs` re-grepped + corrected (lines ~231, 1136, 2200-2223, 2504, 2658, 4055 — describing the old `\n\n\n`/`\n\n\n\n` empty-item form); the [parsing.md](parsing.md) → Round-trip guarantees home (already authored at planning).

**Proves (red on real input):** a new engine test renders a **minted-empty arch-doc component** (a `description` slot left empty + an `implemented-by` field) and asserts `render(parse(x)) == x` — RED before the predicate change (the minted one-blank vs reparsed three-blank divergence this session reproduced), GREEN after; genuinely-slotless items (a changelog release, `slot: None`, no slot leaf) stay byte-identical (masking-guard: the fix moves only `Some("")` items). Decoupled from migration — this increment touches no pack file and gates nothing downstream. design_refs: parsing.md → Round-trip guarantees → Empty-slot canonical form; DECISIONS 2026-06-18 fork C4.

### Increment 2 — migrate-arch-doc workflow + author-migration-arch-doc guidance (pack-only)

**Deliverable:** `jigc migrate <file> --as arch-doc` composes and authors end-to-end — a new `workflows/migrate-arch-doc.yaml` (the `migrate-<doctype>` shape: source seam → author step → migrate-finalize, `allows-create: [{type: arch-doc, as: arch-doc}]`) + a new `steps/author-migration-arch-doc.yaml` carrying the doctype-specific guidance. No engine/CLI/schema change.

**Grouped scope:** the guidance's six obligations ([auto-migration.md](../design/auto-migration.md) → The author-migration-arch-doc guidance) — the foreign→canonical **heading map** (Overview→`overview`; component/module sections→`components` items; decision refs→`cites`; fixed single-word section ids); **required-slot synthesis** for both `overview` *and* each component `description`; **`implemented-by` optional — anchor a concrete `path#symbol` verbatim, else omit**; **`cites` bracketed, in-store-only, drop-to-prose** otherwise; **committed-first ordering**; **slug-distinct component titles + no agent-authored commit** (the commit is auto-provisioned). `cargo clean -p cli` so `include_dir!` re-embeds the two new files.

**Proves (red on real input):** a smoke migration of the synthetic-Rust arch-doc fixture → the **committed doc** round-trips byte-stable (the intermediate-state proof is Inc 1's; this gate proves the *committed* doc) and finalizes with one resolving `implemented-by`; the **reds fire** — a dangling `cites` blocks at `ref-resolves`, an unfilled `description` blocks at `required-slot-present`, a vanished anchored symbol blocks at `doc-code.symbol-exists`; `migrate --as arch-doc` no longer errors `no workflow`. design_refs: auto-migration.md → Generalizing to arch-doc; the migrate-adr workflow/step as the copy template.

### Increment 3 — the measured hybrid acceptance (flow 28) + rebuild/re-pin

**Deliverable:** rebuild `jigc` + re-pin both bin dirs (record sha256), then the **hybrid corpus** migrated end-to-end and measured — worked-examples [flow 28](../design/worked-examples.md#28-migrations-last-doctype--a-foreign-architecture-doc-with-code-anchors-and-decision-citations-m26).

**Grouped scope:** the **real-foreign arm** (an arc42/C4/README-architecture doc, file-only anchors accepted, non-Rust OK — the in-the-wild fidelity claim); the **synthesized-over-jigc-Rust arm** (labeled synthetic) carrying the **C2 mandate** — first provision a committed `adr` (migrate-adr or create+finalize, the flow-16 precondition), then migrate an arch-doc with **≥2 components carrying real, independently-resolving `implemented-by` anchors** + a `cites → adr` over the committed adr; the **blocking red** — delete component A's anchored symbol (a **fixture/clone** target, never a live jigc dep) → finalize blocks naming `arch-doc:<slug>#components/<a-id>/implemented-by` while B stays valid; the four facts recorded under `completions/artifacts/M26/` (the [measurement.md](../design/measurement.md) recorded-alongside posture); the honest bounds carried in (slug-as-H1, file-only-for-non-Rust, C3 finalize-only, synthetic-arm labeled).

**Proves:** worked-examples [flow 28](../design/worked-examples.md#28-migrations-last-doctype--a-foreign-architecture-doc-with-code-anchors-and-decision-citations-m26) — a foreign architecture doc migrates with code anchors + decision citations; **doc↔code on a migrated doc** holds with per-item disambiguation; **`cites → adr`** resolves over a committed adr with committed-first ordering; the slot+field component is byte-stable on the Inc-1 substrate; Framing A intact. design_refs: auto-migration.md → Acceptance corpus + Migration-quality measure; worked-examples flow 28.

**Outcome (2026-06-18).** All three increments built + independently validated clean, **zero fix rounds** — the cut held exactly as planned (no decomposition gaps, unlike M25). **What shipped:** **(Inc 1)** the empty-slot+field-group byte-stability fix — `render_item_at` canonicalizes an empty present-slot (`Some("")`) to the one-blank slotless form, so a minted-but-unfilled slot+field component round-trips `render(parse(x)) == x`, retiring the M22→M26-keyed #1-risk violation (1-line predicate + the 2 changelog-fixture rebases + the new proving test + ~8 comment corrections; **direction B**, exactly the 2 fixtures flipped as the review predicted); **(Inc 2)** the **pack-only** migration arm — `migrate-arch-doc.yaml` + `author-migration-arch-doc.yaml` (the six guidance obligations: heading map, required-slot synthesis for `overview` *and* `description`, optional/drop-able `implemented-by`, bracketed in-store-only `cites`, committed-first ordering, slug-distinct titles + no agent-authored commit), zero engine/CLI/schema change; **(Inc 3)** the measured **hybrid** acceptance — `flow28_marquee` binary-proof + the recorded corpus. **Milestone-completion audit: e2e overall-PASS** (real-foreign + synthetic-Rust arms, the three finalize reds, the **C2 per-item-disambiguation red** naming A's item address while B stays valid, determinism byte-identical across two divergent runs — all through the rebuilt binary on the embedded pack + sibling probe, no env overrides) **+ code-review found no critical/high/medium**, only **2 LOW**, both auto-fixed (`7d41141`: a strengthened empty-slot masking guard that now genuinely distinguishes correct-from-broken + a `trim()`-intent comment; the predicate is correct and unchanged). **The measured done-bar** ([completions/artifacts/M26/migration-quality-measure.md](../completions/artifacts/M26/migration-quality-measure.md)): a **real foreign arch-doc** (`project-beta/project-beta` `ARCHITECTURE.md` — a ~700-line non-English architecture document, 4 components, file-only anchors) + a **synthesized-labeled** arch-doc over jigc's own Rust (2 components anchored to real `symbol-exists` over `parse.rs`/`write.rs` clones, `cites` over a committed adr migrated **first**) — **3/3 round-trip-conformant** (review-gate block → `--approve` → retire+adopt, `ingest` idempotent), both fidelity-accepted, content preserved within the accepted-drops bound, the **authoring agent-call count cut 14→1 and 9→1** (the batch payoff, on the arc's last doctype). Gate green (fmt/clippy/**508 engine + all cli targets**/build). Commits `62e6ded…e68f85f`. Binary rebuilt + re-pinned both bin dirs (`jigc` sha `4817ec32`, `doc-code` sibling `f56032c`). **Honest bounds:** slug-as-H1 (now keyed); file-only anchors for non-Rust targets (the Rust-only `doc-code` probe); the write-time code-anchor shape check stays finalize-only (C3); the synthetic arm is labeled synthetic. **The migration arc is complete** — all five named doctypes (changelog · adr · spec · prd · arch-doc) are migratable. [DECISIONS.md](../DECISIONS.md) → 2026-06-18 M26 completion.

## M27 · doc-code multi-language — generalize the code-anchor probe from Rust-only to six languages

**Created 2026-06-19.** Closes the **non-Rust silent-pass**: a `code-anchor` `#symbol` on a non-`.rs` file used to emit *no finding*, so a polyglot citation looked validated when it never was ([decisions-pending.md](decisions-pending.md):82, the M17-friction this graduates). M27 generalizes the `doc-code` probe (`crates/cli/probes/doc-code/`) from Rust-only to **six languages** — Rust + TypeScript/JavaScript/Python/PHP/bash — so `implemented-by`/`cites-code` anchors into those files genuinely resolve at finalize (block on a vanished symbol, pass when present). **Scope:** purely **probe-internal** — the baseline auditors verified the pack schema, the three code-anchor doctypes (`arch-doc.implemented-by` / `adr.cites-code` / `spec.maps-to-test`), the engine, and the wire contract are all language-blind, so M27 needs **zero** pack/engine/CLI/schema change; the grammar deps stay out of the engine/cli lock graph by the probe's detached-workspace invariant. CSS + docker-compose are **parked** (their addressable units — selectors, service keys — aren't `path#symbol` AST named items; a follow-on, [decisions-pending.md](decisions-pending.md)).

**Baseline-verified by exercise** (3 `capability-auditor`s + 4 `gap-detector`s, the latter spiking all five grammars against the probe's pinned `tree-sitter 0.26.9`; survived an independent pre-decompose review that spiked the grammars again — 5 table corrections baked, model held). **The reframe that drove planning:** the baseline's "`symbol-exists` is field-driven, so it ports for free" claim was **REFUTED** — the new grammars expose a `name` field on *non-declaration* nodes (bash `command`, TS `import_specifier`/`variable_declarator`, PHP `simple_parameter`, Python imports), so M10's any-`name`-node walk would **false-PASS a called-but-undefined symbol** (the exact rot the probe exists to catch). So the resolution **model itself changes** from field-driven to a **per-language node-kind allowlist** — the milestone's central work, a fourth seam beyond the three dispatch points (`is_rust_file` / `with_rust_root` / `main.rs:209`). **Five human-gated forks settled** ([DECISIONS.md](../DECISIONS.md) → 2026-06-19 M27 planning): **F1** all-declarations-any-nesting (visibility ignored) via the allowlist, with two value-position kinds (`variable_declarator`, `method_definition`) tightened to a declaration position; **F2** un-grammared extensions emit a non-blocking **`doc-code.unsupported-language` advisory** (distinct check-id, **un-keyed** — inventory stays 27, no golden re-pin); **F3** bash = `.sh`/`.bash` + **shebang-sniff** for extensionless scripts, functions-only; **F4** non-Rust `criterion-maps-to-test` blocks on a vanished symbol + advises the is-a-test half is unverified (Rust-only predicate); **F7** acceptance mandates the blocking-red on **TypeScript + Python** on a real polyglot repo, measured on the installed binary. **The binary changes** (5 grammar crates added to the probe → a larger embedded+extracted probe), so it must be **rebuilt + re-pinned** (both bin dirs) before any live exercise; the 150 MB `cargo_install` size guard is tightened to a release-realistic ceiling. Acceptance = a new worked-examples flow (mirror flow 13/16 on a non-Rust target), measured. Design home: [validation.md](../design/validation.md) → Multi-language resolution. Decomposition below.

## Milestone 27 — doc-code multi-language (Rust → six languages): decomposition

**Planned + settled 2026-06-19** ([DECISIONS.md](../DECISIONS.md) → 2026-06-19 M27 planning; design [validation.md](../design/validation.md) → Multi-language resolution). **Four increments, risk-first + linear:** the **resolution-model change + the dispatch generalization, proven on TypeScript** (the headline correctness risk — the allowlist mechanism + the value-position tightening + the hardest grammar, retired first on one language), then the **remaining four grammars** (JS/Python/PHP/bash + shebang), then the **`unsupported-language` advisory + the non-Rust `maps-to-test` floor** (the fallback for un-grammared files, depending on resolution existing), then the **measured TS+Python acceptance** that consumes all of it. Every gate carries the **red obligation** (a vanished symbol blocks naming the item address; a present symbol passes — proven before green). **No pack/engine/CLI/schema change** (probe-internal); the only engine-adjacent move is the probe re-declaring a `Severity::Advisory` variant the engine already accepts.

### Increment 1 — the resolution-model change + dispatch generalization, on TypeScript (the headline risk)

**Deliverable:** the probe resolves a TypeScript/TSX `path#symbol` against a **per-language node-kind allowlist** at any nesting. Generalize the seam: `is_rust_file(path)` → `grammar_for(path) -> Option<Language>` (an extension→grammar map; `.ts`/`.mts`/`.cts`→TypeScript, `.tsx`→**TSX** — a distinct grammar, the plain TS grammar errors on JSX), `with_rust_root` → grammar-parameterized, `main.rs:209` routes to the resolved grammar (an extension with no grammar yet keeps the M10 silent-skip — Inc 3 replaces that with the advisory). Replace `item_names`' field-driven `name`-walk with an allowlist dispatch keyed on the grammar.

**Grouped scope:** add `tree-sitter-typescript 0.23.2` to the probe `Cargo.toml`; the TS/TSX allowlist (`function_declaration`, `class_declaration`, `abstract_class_declaration`, `interface_declaration`, `type_alias_declaration`, `enum_declaration`; `method_definition` **class-body-member-only**; `variable_declarator` **module/export/top-level-only**); the Rust path preserved unchanged (still the field-driven walk, since only declarations carry `name` in Rust); the TS hostile-input panic-free fixture; **fix the stale root `Cargo.toml` `exclude`** (`crates/cli/pack/probes/doc-code` → `crates/cli/probes/doc-code`) since this increment edits the probe's `Cargo.toml`.

**Proves (red on real input):** a TS `implemented-by` (or `cites-code`) anchored to a real symbol blocks when the symbol vanishes, passes when present; the **negative cases hold** — a function-local `let` (`variable_declarator` in a `statement_block`), an object-literal method (`method_definition` outside a `class_body`), and a called-but-never-defined name (`import_specifier`/`command`-style over-match) all **do not** resolve; an exported **abstract class** **does** resolve (the false-block the review caught). design_refs: validation.md → Multi-language resolution (the dispatch + the TS row + the value-position tightening); DECISIONS 2026-06-19 forks F1.

### Increment 2 — the remaining four grammars: JavaScript, Python, PHP, bash (+ shebang dispatch)

**Deliverable:** `grammar_for` + the allowlist cover JS/JSX, Python, PHP, and bash; bash additionally dispatches via **shebang-sniff** for extensionless scripts.

**Grouped scope:** add `tree-sitter-{javascript 0.25.0, python 0.25.0, php 0.24.2, bash 0.25.1}` to the probe `Cargo.toml`; the four allowlists (JS: `function_declaration`/`class_declaration` + the two tightened value-position kinds; Python: `function_definition`/`class_definition`, nested defs at any nesting; PHP: `function_definition`/`class_declaration`/`method_declaration`/`interface_declaration`/`trait_declaration`/`enum_declaration`; bash: `function_definition` **only**); the dispatch extensions (`.js`/`.jsx`/`.mjs`/`.cjs`→JS, `.py`/`.pyi`→Python, `.php`/`.phtml`→PHP, `.sh`/`.bash`→bash); the **shebang sniff** (`#!…sh`) firing **only** when the extension maps to no grammar (extension always wins — pure file-bytes read, determinism intact); a hostile-input panic-free fixture per grammar.

**Proves (red on real input):** for each language, a real declared symbol resolves and a vanished one blocks, per its allowlist (PHP trait/interface/enum resolve — the review's false-block fix; a Python nested `def` resolves; a bash *called-but-undefined* function does **not** resolve, only a `function_definition`); an **extensionless** `#!/bin/bash` script dispatches to bash via shebang; a `.py` file with a bash shebang stays Python (extension wins). design_refs: validation.md → Multi-language resolution (the JS/Python/PHP/bash rows + dispatch + shebang precedence); DECISIONS 2026-06-19 forks F1/F3.

### Increment 3 — the `unsupported-language` advisory + the non-Rust `maps-to-test` floor

**Deliverable:** a `#symbol` anchor on a present file whose extension/shebang maps to **no shipped grammar** emits a non-blocking `doc-code.unsupported-language` advisory (replacing M10's silent `return None`); a non-Rust `criterion-maps-to-test` anchor blocks on a vanished symbol and advises on the is-a-test half.

**Grouped scope:** add the `Severity::Advisory` variant to the probe's re-declared `Severity` enum (engine already accepts `advisory` — no engine change) + an advisory `Finding` constructor; emit `doc-code.unsupported-language` from the `grammar_for(path) == None` path on a present file carrying a `#symbol`; wire the non-Rust `criterion-maps-to-test` **truth table** (symbol **absent** → one blocking `criterion-maps-to-test`, no advisory; symbol **present** → one `unsupported-language` advisory, symbol passes — never co-fire); confirm the un-keyed advisory flows through the engine post-pass keeping its emitted severity (the `file-state.baseline-adopt` precedent — verified at review against `result.rs`), so the inventory stays **27** with no `knobs.yaml`/golden change.

**Proves (red on real input):** a `#symbol` on a `.css` (or `compose.yaml`) file emits the advisory — **not** a silent pass, **not** a block; a non-Rust `maps-to-test` blocks on a vanished symbol and advises (one finding each, never both) on a present one; a store/finalize report carrying only `unsupported-language` advisories does not flip the exit code (advisory, not blocking); the inventory count is unchanged. design_refs: validation.md → Multi-language resolution (the advisory + the maps-to-test truth table) + MVP check inventory (un-keyed informational); DECISIONS 2026-06-19 forks F2/F4.

### Increment 4 — the measured TS+Python acceptance (flow 29) + size-guard + rebuild/re-pin

**Deliverable:** rebuild `jigc` + re-pin both bin dirs (record sha256), then a non-Rust doc↔code **blocking red** on a real polyglot repo, on **TypeScript and Python**, measured on the installed binary (embedded pack, sibling probe, no env overrides) — worked-examples **flow 29** (mirror flow 13/16 on a non-Rust target).

**Grouped scope:** author worked-examples flow 29 (an `arch-doc implemented-by` / `adr cites-code` anchored to a real TS symbol + a real Python symbol; finalize passes when present, blocks naming the item address when vanished — incl. the **per-item disambiguation** shape: delete component A's TS symbol, A blocks while B's Python symbol stays valid); **tighten the `cargo_install_probe.rs` size guard** from 150 MB to a release-realistic ceiling so future grammar bloat trips it; **rebuild + re-pin** (a larger embedded+extracted probe); the four measured facts recorded under `completions/artifacts/M27/`; **qualify the stale shipped-milestone prose** M27 makes false — worked-examples flow 15 ("would false-block on a TypeScript project") + flow 28's non-Rust honest-bound, `architecture-documentation.md:84` ("Rust-grammar-only"), `auto-migration.md:161/171` (the "silently passes" caveat) — each gets a "(at MXX; generalized at M27)" qualifier, not a silent rewrite.

**Proves:** flow 29 — a non-Rust `path#symbol` citation genuinely validates against reality: blocks on a vanished TS *and* a vanished Python symbol (naming the item address), passes when present, per-item disambiguation holds across two languages; the installed binary resolves the sibling probe with the larger grammar set within the size budget; the M17-friction "doc-code non-Rust silent symbol pass" is closed in the wild. design_refs: validation.md → Multi-language resolution; worked-examples flow 29; DECISIONS 2026-06-19 fork F7 + the measurement.md recorded-alongside posture.

## M28 · doc-code CSS — the first breadth target, proving the HD1 "addressable unit" generalization

**Created 2026-06-20.** The doctype-expansion track's first **breadth** target of the multi-language doc↔code north-star ([ideas/multi-language-doc-code.md](multi-language-doc-code.md) → HD1). M27 generalized the probe to six languages whose addressable symbols are AST *named items* (a `.name`-field read); CSS is the **most-different model** — no `.name`-field declarations, only **selectors** (`.btn`, `#header`), **`@keyframes` names**, and **custom properties** (`--color`).
