So shipping CSS is not "add a 7th grammar like M27 did" — it **forces and proves the HD1 keystone**: the probe's resolution model generalizes from *"read the `.name` field of declaration nodes"* to *"a per-node-kind **extractor** yielding a unit name."* The real deliverable is that abstraction, demonstrated on CSS; every later language (YAML keys, HTML ids) then rides the same extractor seam. **Scope: probe-internal only** — zero engine/CLI/pack-schema change (baseline-verified by exercise: the `code-anchor` field-type, the three code-anchor doctypes, the engine, and the wire contract are all language-blind; a `styles.css#card` string is structurally accepted everywhere — the only language constraint lives in the probe's `grammar_for`). tree-sitter-css stays out of the engine/cli lock graph (the probe's detached-workspace invariant). **CSS-only this milestone**; YAML/docker-compose stays deferred (the next breadth increment, same HD1 model). **is-a-test is N/A for CSS** (CSS has no tests) — out of scope.

**Baseline-verified by exercise** (3 `capability-auditor`s + 4 `gap-detector`s **spiking real tree-sitter-css ASTs** — `tree-sitter-css 0.23.2` compiled against the probe's pinned `tree-sitter 0.26.9`; survived an independent pre-decompose review that re-spiked the ASTs + verified anchor round-trip, no blocking findings — 4 precision fixes baked). **The two over-match forks the spike settled** (both the M27 false-*PASS* shape — a `name`-bearing non-declaration node falsely resolving a citation): **(1)** a pseudo-class (`:hover`/`:root`) parses as `pseudo_class_selector > class_name` (the *same* `class_name` kind a real `.class` uses) → the class extractor **parent-constrains** `class_name` to a `class_selector` child (the M27 `method_definition`→`class_body` pattern); **(2)** a custom property is **not** a node-kind — `--color` and `color` both parse as `declaration > property_name` → the extractor **gates `property_name` on `starts_with("--")`** (else every CSS property false-resolves). So the model is **node-kind + predicate**, not pure node-kind. **Forks settled** ([DECISIONS.md](../DECISIONS.md) → 2026-06-20 M28 planning): **F1** unit set = class / id / `@keyframes` / custom-property (exclude element/attribute/universal/pseudo); compound selectors contribute all class/id names via the existing walk; **F2** custom-property **keeps the `--`** (human-decided — the real CSS identifier, collision-safe; class/id/`@keyframes` need *no* stripping, the grammar already separates the sigil); **F3** the six grammars stay byte-identical (the 58 probe units the guard); **F4** acceptance via `arch-doc.implemented-by` a real CSS class (single anchor per component, mirroring flow 29); **F5** exact→blocking, no heuristic creep; **F6** pin `tree-sitter-css 0.23.2`. **Honest bounds the milestone originates** (HD1 is silent): element/attribute/universal/pseudo selectors intentionally unverified; `@import`ed sheets not followed (single-file parse); class `.x` / id `#x` collide under `path#name` (the `#kind:name` qualifier stays HD1-deferred). The binary is rebuilt + re-pinned (both bin dirs) before the live measure; CSS is the smallest grammar so the 90 MiB guard holds with margin (its "a seventh grammar blows it" comment is now false → updated). Acceptance = a CSS doc↔code **blocking-red** measured on the installed binary on the live PHP/TS/Vite repo (worked-examples flow 30, mirror flow 29/16 on a CSS target). Decomposition below.

## Milestone 28 — CSS symbol-exists (the HD1 addressable-unit generalization): decomposition

**Planned + settled 2026-06-20** ([DECISIONS.md](../DECISIONS.md) → 2026-06-20 M28 planning; design promoting into [validation.md](../design/validation.md) → Multi-language resolution). **Two increments, risk-first + linear:** the **extractor-model generalization + CSS resolution** (the keystone abstraction + the headline over-match correctness risk, retired first — the whole probe change), then the **measured acceptance + size-guard + rebuild/re-pin + doc fold-back** (the in-the-wild proof, consuming Inc 1). **No pack/engine/CLI/schema change** (probe-internal); the only engine-adjacent fact is that `code-anchor` values are language-blind opaque text (review-verified). Every gate carries the **red obligation** (a vanished CSS selector blocks naming the item address; a present one passes — proven before green).

### Increment 1 — the extractor-model generalization + CSS resolution (the keystone + headline risk)

**Deliverable:** the probe resolves a `.css#name` anchor against CSS **addressable units** — class / id / `@keyframes` / custom-property names — exact→blocking, while the six existing grammars stay **behaviorally byte-identical**. The resolution model generalizes from M10/M27's fused `child_by_field_name("name")` read to a per-grammar extractor dispatch.

**Grouped scope:** generalize `item_names` (`resolve.rs:221-238`) to dispatch a per-grammar extractor — the six grammars route to the existing `.name`-field read (one arm, unchanged); CSS routes to a new `css_item_names` of **four node-kind arms**: `class_selector`→`class_name` (**parent-constrained** to a `class_selector` child — closes the pseudo-class over-match), `id_selector`→`id_name`, `keyframes_statement`→`keyframes_name`, `declaration`→`property_name` **gated on `starts_with("--")`, kept verbatim** (closes the every-property over-match). `item_names`/`symbol_exists` signatures unchanged. Add `Grammar::Css` + its `language()` arm + `grammar_for` `.css`→`Css` + `tree-sitter-css 0.23.2` to the **detached** probe `Cargo.toml` (stays out of the engine/cli lock graph). Compound/chained/`@media`-nested selectors contribute all names via the existing full-tree walk (no walk change). A CSS hostile-input panic-free fixture. **Re-point the test-flip census** (review-corrected, two buckets): **(a) flips RED, assertion changes** — `probes/doc-code/src/main.rs:407` + `tests/doc_code_probe.rs:703` (a `.css#symbol` now resolves, not advises); **(b) stays green, exemplar stale, re-point deliberately** — `main.rs:436`, `main.rs:449`, `crates/engine/src/result.rs:972` — all to **`.pl`** (off-roadmap; not `.yaml` — YAML is the next breadth milestone).

**Proves (red on real input):** a CSS `implemented-by` (or `cites-code`) anchored to a real selector **blocks** when the selector vanishes, **passes** when present; the **over-match negatives hold** — a pseudo-class (`:hover`, `:root`), a plain CSS property (`color`, `display`), and an element/attribute/universal selector all **do not** resolve; a compound selector (`.card > .title`) contributes **both** names; a custom property resolves as `#--color` (`--` kept); a selector named only in a `/* comment */` does not resolve; the six existing grammars' **58 probe units stay green** (byte-identical). design_refs: ideas/multi-language-doc-code.md → HD1; validation.md → Multi-language resolution; DECISIONS 2026-06-20 M28 planning (the two over-match forks + F1/F2/F3/F6).

### Increment 2 — the measured CSS acceptance (flow 30) + size-guard + rebuild/re-pin + doc fold-back (the in-the-wild proof)

**Deliverable:** rebuild `jigc` + re-pin both bin dirs (record sha256), then a CSS doc↔code **blocking red** on a real CSS selector, measured on the **installed binary** (embedded pack, sibling probe, no env overrides) on the live PHP/TS/Vite repo — worked-examples **flow 30** (mirror flow 29/16 on a CSS target).

**Grouped scope:** author `flow30_acceptance.rs` (a CSS arm mirroring `flow29_acceptance.rs` — a component whose `arch-doc.implemented-by` anchors a real `styles.css#<class>`; finalize **blocks** naming the item address when the class is deleted, **passes** when present, incl. **per-item disambiguation** — A's CSS-class deletion blocks A while B's anchor stays valid; drives the real binary via the sibling probe, `JIGC_DOC_CODE_PROBE` removed) + worked-examples **flow 30**; **doc fold-back** (separate commit, the M16-T5 pattern) — promote HD1's addressable-unit model into `validation.md` → Multi-language resolution (**reframe** "read the `.name` field" → "extractor per node-kind", add a **CSS row** + an **extractor column**, **keep the `#multi-language-resolution-m27` anchor stable** — ~9 inbound links — via a subsection, not a rename; write the CSS-specific **honest bounds**) + **T3-qualify** (never rewrite) the falsified sites (`validation.md:178/:188/:215`, the `VISION.md` Open-questions multi-language index, `ideas/multi-language-doc-code.md` status line + tiered roadmap — CSS shipped, the doc stays for YAML/HD2/HD3/HD4); **update** the now-false `cargo_install_probe.rs:41` "a seventh grammar blows it" comment + re-run the 90 MiB guard; **rebuild + re-pin** both bin dirs (a larger embedded+extracted probe; record `jigc` + `doc-code` sha256 identical across `~/.local/bin` + `~/.cargo/bin`); record the measured facts under `completions/artifacts/M28/`.

**Proves:** flow 30 — a CSS `path#name` citation genuinely validates against reality on the *actually-shipped* binary: blocks on a vanished CSS selector (naming the item address), passes when present, per-item disambiguation holds; the installed binary resolves the sibling probe with the CSS grammar within the size budget; the CSS half of the CSS+docker-compose deferral is closed in the wild and the HD1 addressable-unit keystone is proven on the most-different model. design_refs: validation.md → Multi-language resolution; worked-examples flow 30; DECISIONS 2026-06-20 M28 planning (F4/F5 + the doc fold-back + the measurement.md recorded-alongside posture).

## M29 · doc-code YAML/docker-compose — the second breadth target, the first language to RIDE the HD1 seam

**Created 2026-06-20.** The doctype-expansion track's **second breadth** target of the multi-language doc↔code north-star ([ideas/multi-language-doc-code.md](multi-language-doc-code.md) → HD1; CSS was #1 at M28). The reframe is the **inverse of M28's**: M28 *forced and built* the HD1 keystone (the per-grammar extractor seam); M29 is the first language that **rides** the finished seam, so it is the proof the keystone *generalizes cheaply* — "support language N = one uniform move (define N's addressable units), not N special cases." YAML's addressable units = **mapping keys** (a docker-compose service `web` is a mapping key under `services:`); `path#name` = "any mapping key named `name`," over the same `code-anchor` field-type, the same `symbol-exists` check-id, **zero engine/CLI/pack-schema change** (probe-internal — baseline-verified by exercise: a `compose.yaml#web` value flows opaque through `field-types.yaml` → `check_code_anchor` write-validation → conformance → `target_surface` enumeration → probe `split_anchor`; the **only** extension-inspecting code is the probe's `grammar_for`). **is-a-test is N/A for YAML** (YAML has no tests) — out of scope.

**Baseline-verified by exercise** (3 `capability-auditor`s + 4 `gap-detector`s, the latter **spiking the real grammar** — `tree-sitter-yaml 0.7.2` compiled against the probe's pinned `tree-sitter 0.26.9`, dumping S-expressions of a real `compose.yaml` + a hostile `census.yaml`; survived an independent pre-decompose review that re-spiked the claims, **one should-fix baked** — the merge-key correction below — + two advisories baked): the per-grammar extractor dispatch seam is live and clean (`item_names` `resolve.rs:234-254` short-circuits `Grammar::Css → css_item_names`; a `Grammar::Yaml` arm slots in identically, both `match`es exhaustive so a missed arm can't ship); the detached-workspace invariant is **double-detached** → `tree-sitter-yaml` cannot enter the engine/cli lock graph; the embed/extract/re-pin spine is grammar-count-agnostic; the size guard (90 MiB ceiling, ~18.5 MiB headroom over the ~72 MB debug artifact) holds with margin — YAML's parser is **tiny** (~310 KB); the probe suite is **68 passing** at HEAD (`734feeb`).

**The over-match census — the keystone risk (where M28's energy went), spike-settled.** The verified citable-node rule: **admit a name iff `node.kind()` ∈ {`block_mapping_pair`, `flow_pair`}, take `child_by_field_name("key")`, strip a single pair of surrounding matched quotes.** The `[key]`-field constraint is the YAML analogue of CSS's `class_name`-under-`class_selector` parent-constraint — it **structurally excludes** every over-match negative (anchor `&web`, alias `*web`, tag `!!str web`, mapping value `WEB_HOST: web`, sequence item `- web`, `#`-comment text — all live under `[value]` or as sequence children, never `[key]`). **Two `unverified-reuse` busts — the extractor is NOT a pure `css_item_names` mirror:** **(B1)** flow mappings `{a: 1}` parse as `flow_pair`, **not** `block_mapping_pair` — a single-arm extractor false-blocks a valid anchor (under-match); **(B2)** the `[key]` field returns the raw span *including quotes* — a quoted key needs stripping (CSS's `css_child_text` is the wrong helper). **Merge-key correction (review-caught):** `<<: *anchor`'s `[key]` is the scalar `<<`, so the rule **admits `<<`** (only the aliased value is excluded) — harmless, left as-is, **no `<<`-negative test** (it would fail).

**Forks settled** ([DECISIONS.md](../DECISIONS.md) → 2026-06-20 M29 planning): **F1** unit set = mapping keys (block + flow), any nesting; exclude anchors/aliases/tags/values/sequence-items/comments (merge-key `<<` admitted-harmless). **F2** key naming = **strip surrounding matched quotes** (the deliberate opposite of M28's keep-the-`--`; internal escapes/decorated keys not normalized — an honest bound). **F3** extractor scope = one `Grammar::Yaml` arm + `yaml_item_names` (2 node-kinds + quote-strip); the seven grammars stay byte-identical (the **68** units the guard). **F4** acceptance = `arch-doc.implemented-by` a real compose service key (single anchor per component → per-item disambiguation); `adr.cites-code` works; `spec.maps-to-test` excluded (is-a-test path). **F5** severity = exact→blocking. **F6** version pin = `tree-sitter-yaml 0.7.2` (`tree-sitter-grammars` org, spiked-green). **F7** compose-blind breadth = **`.yaml` AND `.yml`**, extension-only (no filename special-casing) — resolves `#name` on **any** YAML (k8s/GHA/config), broader than docker-compose (`compose.yaml#web` = "a YAML key named `web`," not "a compose *service*"). **F8** acceptance delivery = the **M28 posture** (hermetic `flow31_acceptance.rs` + a representative measured artifact; the literal live-repo run is owner-driven hardening afterward, not a completion gate).

**Honest bounds the milestone ORIGINATES** (written into `validation.md`): **flat-namespace collision — sharper than CSS's** (generic keys `image`/`ports`/`build` recur under every service so `compose.yaml#image` resolves trivially-true; a service key is indistinguishable from a same-named key elsewhere — recorded explicitly, the M28 LOW lesson); **compose-schema-blind** (the name must not over-promise); block + flow both contribute; anchors/aliases/tags/values/seq-items excluded; **single-file parse** (no `extends:`/`!include`/cross-file; multi-doc `---` streams resolve all docs' keys); the **key-path `#services.web` / `#kind:name` qualifier stays deferred** (trigger: a real citation must disambiguate same-named keys at different paths). **G3 — `implemented-by` single-cardinality bound carries** (multi-service-per-component is enforced-blocked at `target_surface.rs:421`, deferred 0..* engine work, not an M29 knob). **Inventory — no literal count asserted** (M29 adds no check-id; the 27/24/20 doc drift predates M29). Decomposition below.

## Milestone 29 — YAML/docker-compose symbol-exists (the HD1 seam ridden): decomposition

**Planned + settled 2026-06-20** ([DECISIONS.md](../DECISIONS.md) → 2026-06-20 M29 planning; design promoting into [validation.md](../design/validation.md) → Multi-language resolution). **Two increments, risk-first + linear** (mirroring M28): the **YAML extractor + grammar wiring + activation + test-flip census** (the whole probe change + the headline B1/B2 + over-match correctness risk, retired first), then the **measured acceptance + size-guard + rebuild/re-pin + doc fold-back** (the in-the-wild proof, consuming Inc 1). **No pack/engine/CLI/schema change** (probe-internal). Every gate carries the **red obligation** (a vanished mapping key blocks naming the item address; a present one — block or flow, quoted or plain — passes — proven before green).

### Increment 1 — the YAML extractor + grammar wiring + extension activation + test-flip census (the keystone + headline B1/B2 risk)

**Deliverable:** the probe resolves a `.yaml`/`.yml#name` anchor against YAML **mapping keys** (block + flow, any nesting, quotes stripped) — exact→blocking, while the seven existing grammars stay **behaviorally byte-identical**.

**Grouped scope:** add `tree-sitter-yaml 0.7.2` to the **detached** probe `Cargo.toml` (stays out of the engine/cli lock graph); add `Grammar::Yaml` + its `language()` arm; add a new `yaml_item_names` extractor of **two node-kind arms** — `block_mapping_pair` and `flow_pair`, each reading `child_by_field_name("key")` then **stripping a single pair of surrounding matched quotes** (B1 + B2 — *not* a copy of `css_child_text`); `item_names`/`symbol_exists` signatures unchanged; keys at any nesting via the existing `find_named_item` walk (no walk change); add a YAML hostile-input panic-free fixture (`yaml_hostile_input_does_not_panic`). Add `grammar_for` **`.yaml`/`.yml` → `Grammar::Yaml`** (the extension activation — extension-only, no filename special-casing, F7). **Correct the doubly-stale `resolve.rs:6-8` & `:173-175` comments** ("M10 silent-skip / advisory is a later increment" — false since M27) + document the new `.yaml/.yml → Yaml` arm. **Re-point the test-flip census** (the activation flips it RED): `crates/cli/probes/doc-code/src/main.rs:407-419` (`un_grammared_file_with_symbol_emits_unsupported_language_advisory`) currently asserts `compose.yaml` + `services:\n  web:` + `web` → one `unsupported-language` advisory — now `web` *resolves*; re-point the un-grammared exemplar to the surviving `.pl`/Perl arm and assert present-key → 0 findings / vanished-key → one blocking `doc-code.symbol-exists`.

**Proves (red on real input):** a YAML `implemented-by` (or `cites-code`) anchored to a real mapping key **blocks** when the key vanishes, **passes** when present; **B1 holds** — a flow-mapping key (`{web: …}`) resolves (not false-blocked); **B2 holds** — a quoted key (`"web":`/`'web':`) resolves as `#web`; the **over-match negatives hold** — an anchor (`&web`), alias (`*web`), tag (`!!str web`), mapping value (`WEB_HOST: web`), and sequence item (`- web`) all **do not** resolve; a key at deep nesting (`services: web: build: context:`) resolves via the walk; YAML hostile input is panic-free; the seven existing grammars' **68 probe units stay green** (byte-identical). design_refs: ideas/multi-language-doc-code.md → HD1; validation.md → Multi-language resolution; DECISIONS 2026-06-20 M29 planning (the over-match census + B1/B2 + F1/F2/F3/F6/F7).

### Increment 2 — the measured YAML acceptance (flow 31) + size-guard + rebuild/re-pin + doc fold-back (the in-the-wild proof)

**Deliverable:** rebuild `jigc` + re-pin both bin dirs (record sha256), then a YAML doc↔code **blocking red** on a real compose service key, measured on the **installed binary** (embedded pack, sibling probe, no env overrides) on a representative `compose.yaml` — worked-examples **flow 31** (mirror flow 30 on a YAML target); the literal live PHP/TS/Vite + docker-compose run is owner-driven hardening afterward (F8, the M28 posture).

**Grouped scope:** author `flow31_acceptance.rs` (a YAML arm mirroring `flow30_acceptance.rs` — an `arch-doc` whose two `components` carry **single** `implemented-by` anchors at **real service keys** in a `compose.yaml`, e.g. `compose.yaml#web` / `compose.yaml#db`; finalize **blocks** naming the item address when a service key is deleted/renamed, **passes** when present, incl. **per-item disambiguation** — A's service-key deletion blocks A while B's anchor stays valid; drives the real binary via the sibling probe, `JIGC_DOC_CODE_PROBE` removed) + worked-examples **flow 31**; **doc fold-back** (separate commit, the M16-T5 pattern) — promote the YAML addressable-unit model into `validation.md` → Multi-language resolution (add a **YAML row** to the extractor table + a new **`#yaml-addressable-units-m29` subsection** after the CSS one, with the YAML honest bounds; **keep `#multi-language-resolution-m27` and `#css-addressable-units-m28` byte-stable** — add, never rename) + **T3-qualify** (never rewrite) the falsified sites (`validation.md:179/:189/:232/:165`, the `VISION.md` Open-questions index, `ideas/multi-language-doc-code.md:3,65` status + tiered roadmap — YAML shipped, the doc stays for JSON/TOML/HD2/HD3/HD4) + **graduate the whole** `decisions-pending.md:86` CSS+docker-compose entry (the compose/YAML half SHIPPED — no deferred half remaining); **update** the now-false `cargo_install_probe.rs:38-45` "seven-grammar" comment (eighth grammar) + re-run the 90 MiB guard (ceiling stays 90 MiB); **rebuild + re-pin** both bin dirs (a larger embedded+extracted probe; record `jigc` + `doc-code` sha256 identical across `~/.local/bin` + `~/.cargo/bin`); record the measured facts under `completions/artifacts/M29/`.

**Proves:** flow 31 — a YAML `path#name` citation genuinely validates against reality on the *actually-shipped* binary: blocks on a vanished compose service key (naming the item address), passes when present, per-item disambiguation holds; the installed binary resolves the sibling probe with the eighth grammar within the size budget; the docker-compose/YAML half of the CSS+docker-compose deferral is closed and the HD1 keystone is proven to generalize cheaply (the first language to ride the seam, not build it). design_refs: validation.md → Multi-language resolution; worked-examples flow 31; DECISIONS 2026-06-20 M29 planning (F4/F5/F8 + the doc fold-back + the measurement.md recorded-alongside posture).

## Milestone 30 — finalize change-set scoping (per-task narrowing): decomposition

**Planned + settled 2026-06-20** ([DECISIONS.md](../DECISIONS.md) → 2026-06-20 M30 planning; the worktree fan-out half split to **M31**, parked in [decisions-pending.md](decisions-pending.md) → M31). Retires the A1 production blocker: `jigc task finalize` (per-task) commits only the task's declared change-set — the **git index** + jigc's promoted/written docs + the first-commit `.jigc/config`+`.jigc/.gitignore` layer — leaving untracked/unstaged WIP out and surfacing it. The project-alpha-2.0 dogfood proved the hazard (unrelated WIP swept into a task commit, `git show --stat`). **Four increments, risk-first + linear:** the per-task narrowing keystone (the data-hazard fix, retired first), then the left-out manifest surface, then the index-validated citation gate, then the guidance + docs + measured acceptance. **The milestone squash path stays `Sweep`** (M31 owns it) — out of scope here. Every gate carries the **red obligation** (the narrowed commit must exclude unstaged/untracked WIP — proven before green). Baseline verified green at `83cb9e2` (118 test binaries); started from green.

### Increment 1 — the per-task `IndexHonoring` narrowing + `StagePolicy` + block-on-empty (the keystone + the dogfood red)

**Deliverable:** a per-task non-migration `finalize` commits exactly the **existing git index** + jigc's promoted docs + the first-commit config layer — never the ambient dirty tree; an unrelated untracked file and an unrelated unstaged-modified tracked edit **stay uncommitted** in the working tree.

**Grouped scope:** replace the per-task non-migration `git add --all` (`task.rs:1090`) with **`IndexHonoring`** — `git add` jigc's promoted-doc destinations + `.jigc/config`/`.jigc/.gitignore` (reuse the `existing_pathspecs`/`path_at_head` exit-128 guards from `stage_migration`, `task.rs:1224`) **into** the existing index, then `git commit` the **whole index** (**G6** — never `git commit -- <pathspec>`, which would drop the agent-written, non-promoted `owner-artifact`). Introduce the 3-way **`StagePolicy`** (`MigrationFixed` / `IndexHonoring` / `Sweep`) replacing `is_migration: bool` and map the three `try_execute_finalize_plan` callers: per-task → `IndexHonoring`|`MigrationFixed` (by `source_seam.exists()`), both milestone callers (`milestone.rs:536`, `task.rs:1000`) → **`Sweep` explicitly** (unchanged behavior). **G2 (per-task only):** redefine the per-task `has_diff` off the narrowed set (`git diff --cached` + promotions + config); dirty-tree+nothing-staged → **block** *"you staged nothing — `git add` your changes"*, genuinely-clean → the existing "produced no diff" — **CLI-side message selection**; leave `milestone.rs:488-490` whole-tree and **do not edit** the shared `empty_commit_finding` (`finalize.rs:756`) (design-review finding 2). **Fixture-wide staging migration** (design-review finding 3 — materially larger than "6 tests"): a shared test helper (`git add` the author's code before the finalize call) applied across the ~15–20 finalize-invoking fixtures that `fs::write` code and rely on the sweep (`finalize_to_git`, `flow13/16/20/28/29`, `e2e_audit`, `arch_doc_acceptance`, `doc_code_gate`, `cold_start_zero_commit`, `file_state_soundness`, …) — enumerate the real set, don't trust "20". Flip the **commit-contents** contract inversions (the half of the 6 that assert a stray *lands*: `finalize_manifest::normal_finalize_commits_stray_and_surfaces_it_as_untracked`, `store_sweep_acceptance::…freeform_notes_swept…`, `finalize_outcome_surface::{outcome_space_is_discriminable, landed_finalize_emits_findings}` where the commit-only `{task}.txt` must now be staged).

**Proves (red on the dogfood repro):** a finalize with a staged task edit + an unrelated untracked file + an unrelated unstaged-modified tracked edit commits **only** the staged edit + promoted docs; the untracked + unstaged files **remain uncommitted**; an agent-written `owner-artifact` (staged, not promoted) **still lands** (G6); a task that staged nothing on a dirty tree **blocks** with the git-add guidance; the migration path is **byte-identical** (`MigrationFixed` keeps `stage_migration`); both milestone callers still `Sweep`; gate green. design_refs: DECISIONS 2026-06-20 M30 planning (G1/G2/G6); design/finalize.md (revised Inc 4).

### Increment 2 — the left-out manifest surface (G3): index/worktree split + dry-run/landed symmetry

**Deliverable:** `finalize` (and `--dry-run`) names what was **included** vs **left out of this commit (unstaged/untracked) — git add them to include**, with a path that is staged-then-further-modified (`MM`) appearing in **both** sets.

**Grouped scope:** stop `git_status_entries` (`task.rs:1497`) collapsing the porcelain XY (its one consumer is `predict_manifest`, low blast radius); rewrite `predict_manifest`'s (`task.rs:774`) non-migration arm to split **X column → included**, **Y column → left-out**; add a post-commit `git status --porcelain` read to `classify_landed_manifest` (`task.rs:1775`) for the residual left-out set (it currently reads only the included `git show --name-status HEAD`). Model left-out as a **separate list** (not a `ManifestKind` variant — a kind can't express "partially included"); add the render line + the **`--format json`** field (**specify the field name + dry-run/landed symmetry up front** — design-review finding 4; the measurement envelope consumers parse it). Flip the **manifest** contract inversions (`finalize_manifest::{dry_run_prints_manifest_and_commits_nothing, json_manifest_on_dry_run_and_landed_run}` — the stray is no longer `swept (was untracked)` but `left-out`).

**Proves:** the dry-run and landed manifests both carry the included + left-out split symmetrically; an `MM` path is reported in both; the unrelated untracked + unstaged files from Inc 1 are **named as left out**; `--format json` carries the left-out set; gate green. design_refs: DECISIONS 2026-06-20 M30 planning (G3); design/finalize.md (B1 block revision, Inc 4).

### Increment 3 — the index-validated citation gate (G4): validate doc↔code against what gets committed

**Deliverable:** a doc citing a code symbol the agent wrote but **didn't stage** **blocks** at finalize — the finalize-scope `doc-code` probe validates the **index** (what commits), not the working tree.

**Grouped scope:** at finalize, after staging, **materialize the index** (`git checkout-index` into a temp tree) and run the finalize-scope `doc-code` probe with `working_tree_root` = that tree (`schedule_doc_code` → `EffectiveStateSnapshot::new`, `validate.rs:473`), so a cited symbol present on disk but absent from the index is caught (design-review finding 1 — the common modify-tracked-file case a path-granular `TrackedPredicate` misses). Covers `adr.cites-code` / `arch-doc.implemented-by` / `spec.maps-to-test`. Temp-tree lifecycle (create/clean) inside the finalize transaction; determinism preserved (the index is a pure function of the staged set). No false-block on a cited file carrying unrelated unstaged hunks (the index has the staged symbol).

**Proves:** a task that writes a cited symbol into a tracked file and stages it → finalize **passes**; the same symbol left **unstaged** → finalize **blocks** naming the dangling anchor (preserving "validated reality == committed reality"); a citation to an already-committed-at-HEAD symbol still passes; gate green. design_refs: DECISIONS 2026-06-20 M30 planning (G4); design/validation.md → the `doc-code` probe; design/finalize.md (Inc 4).

### Increment 4 — the agent-stage contract (G5) + docs + the measured acceptance (flow 32) + rebuild/re-pin

**Deliverable:** the pack + adapter instruct the agent to stage its code; the design docs tell the truth; and the dogfood repro is a **blocking-red→green measured on the installed binary**.

**Grouped scope:** **G5** — add a *"`git add` your task's code edits before finalize"* instruction to the `implement`/`implement-quick`/`finalize` pack steps; add **`git add`** to the claude-code adapter allowlist (`permit: ["jigc *"]` → also `git add`); refresh compose goldens **only if** the workflow body changed (`start_compose`/`config_fill`/`flow8` byte-identity goldens — verify, don't assume). **Docs** (the T3-qualify-don't-rewrite pattern): revise `design/finalize.md` — phase 5 Stage (`:16`/`:58-65`), the dirty-tree-policy section (`:89-101`, **keep the `#dirty-tree-policy` anchor stable** — flow 4 + two internal links target it), the B1 "surfaced, not prevented" block (`:103`), the empty-commit (`:161`) + stale `--dry-run` open-Q (`:173`); record the **agent-stages contract** + the **per-task `IndexHonoring` vs milestone `Sweep` divergence** as the M30/M31 seam (design-review findings 6, 7); add the index-as-code-manifest surface to `design/storage.md` (working-area + by-task-id join) + the term fix in `design/write-commands.md`; update `worked-examples.md` **flow 4** to the new contract + add **flow 32** (the WIP-left-out-and-surfaced acceptance); reframe migration flows 25/26's `#9a` note as the *general* rule. **Acceptance** `flow32_acceptance.rs` — the dogfood repro on the real binary (staged edit + unrelated untracked + unrelated unstaged tracked edit → commits only staged + promoted docs, names the left-out set; `JIGC_DOC_CODE_PROBE` removed, embedded pack). **Rebuild + re-pin** both bin dirs (record `jigc` + `doc-code` sha256 identical across `~/.local/bin` + `~/.cargo/bin`); record measured facts under `completions/artifacts/M30/`.

**Proves:** flow 32 — finalize on the *actually-shipped* binary commits only the declared change-set, names the left-out WIP, and blocks a dangling citation; the agent is told to stage and the adapter permits it; the design docs no longer document the sweep; the A1 production blocker is retired (per-task). design_refs: DECISIONS 2026-06-20 M30 planning (G5 + the doc fold-back); design/finalize.md; worked-examples flow 4 + flow 32.

## M31 · fan-out worktree code isolation — the correct fix for the squash/fan-out commit-scoping hazard M30 left on `Sweep`

**Created 2026-06-20** (the M30 split-off; direction blessed + WF1–WF4 spiked at M30 planning, no hard blocker). M30 narrowed the **per-task** finalize to its declared change-set but deliberately left the **milestone squash/fan-out** path on `Sweep` (`git add --all`) — because a naive narrowing there is a **data-loss regression**: sub-agent code is unstaged-by-design in the shared checkout and reaches the commit *only* via the sweep, so narrowing it drops every line of sub-agent code. The correct fix: each fan-out sub-agent runs in **its own git worktree** (own index/HEAD) and `git add`s its code there; finalize combines the N attributable staged code-sets — **disjoint-apply in task-id order, block + route the named colliding paths on a same-file collision, never `git merge`** — plus the unchanged CLI by-task-id **doc-join** overlay, into one deterministic commit. This unifies the staged-set contract (per-task = one index; fan-out = N worktree indices) and closes a latent same-file sub-agent clobber the shared-checkout model also carries.

**Baseline-verified by exercise** (4 `capability-auditor`s on the real binary + 4 `gap-detector`s spiking the exact new shape; survived the independent pre-decompose review). The M30 foundation is built-and-proven (`StagePolicy` 3-way, `IndexHonoring`, the index-validated `doc-code` gate, the left-out manifest). **Confirmed negatives:** no new doctype / schema / relation / grammar; no VISION/CLAUDE invariant edit; git helpers already worktree-parameterizable; no milestone-path `doc-code` gate to build. **Corrections to the parked plan:** `repo_root` is computed by **11 walk-up copies** (+ 5 `require_project_layer`), not one resolver; the combine mechanism exists **nowhere**; the `git add --all` sweep is `task.rs:1218` (line drift from the handover). The three human forks settled **robust over cheap** (DECISIONS.md → 2026-06-20 M31 planning): worktree binding **reopens the M8 spawn template** (rationale-honoring scoped relaxation); the doc-store seam **binds to jigc_home** (single canonical doc home, correct-by-construction); **squash:false is reworked to honest** per-sub-task commits (the `commit` doctype's message describes the tree it rides).

## Milestone 31 — worktree fan-out code isolation: decomposition

**Status: ✅ shipped + audited (2026-06-21).** All 5 increments built + validated clean; milestone audit (e2e 13/13 on the installed binary + code-review) found one HIGH (squash:false abort WIP-destruction), fixed `5532f68` + re-verified on the re-pinned binary; one pre-existing finding (the sub-task commit-doc provisioning over-reach) split to **M32**. Full record: [DECISIONS.md](../DECISIONS.md) → 2026-06-21 M31 completion.

**Planned + settled 2026-06-20** ([DECISIONS.md](../DECISIONS.md) → 2026-06-20 M31 planning; design fold-back into [storage.md](../design/storage.md) / [workflow-dialect.md](../design/workflow-dialect.md) / [finalize.md](../design/finalize.md)). **Five increments, risk-first + linear:** the **gate** (reopen the no-worktrees invariant, doc-only) first — *never silent*; then **WF3** (the invasive `repo_root`/`jigc_home` split, highest surface, behavior-preserving with zero worktrees); then **WF4** (provisioning + the M8-template reopen + spawn binding); then **the combine keystone** (the squash:true data-loss fix, highest novelty); then **squash:false honest rework** + ship. Every code gate carries the **red obligation** (a fan-out finalize commits each sub-agent's staged code and drops none; a same-file collision blocks naming the paths — proven before green) and the **two off-substrate gate-review obligations** (post-commit `git status` clean; the code combine byte-identical across sub-agent feed/completion orders). Verify baseline green at the build's base sha before starting; line numbers drift — re-confirm at each increment's plan.

### Increment 1 — gate: reopen the no-worktrees invariant (doc-only, separate commit)

**Deliverable:** the design docs authorize worktree code-isolation *before* any code is built against it — the 2026-06-04 "no worktree machinery" decision is **revised** (not lightly qualified) to add a third combine-mode (disjoint-apply, never `git merge`) while keeping its rationale (never text-merge structured managed docs) binding.

**Grouped scope:** **revise** `storage.md` — the "CLI and git" section (`:111/:141/:145/:146/:168`, real lines drift; re-confirm): correct "fan-out is *not* a worktree case" → fan-out **code** isolates by worktree (docs still by the by-task-id join); add the **third combine-mode** to the two-mode binary (`:143`) — fan-out code = worktrees + **deterministic disjoint-apply with block-on-collision, distinct from both the doc join and ordinary `git merge`**; quote the 2026-06-04 rationale as **still-binding** for docs; give the **doc-store→jigc_home invariant a home** ("code resolves against the worktree; the committed doc-store + `.jigc/` resolve against jigc_home/the main checkout"); note the worktree location in the Repository-layout block (ephemeral, gitignored, provisioned+torn-down per fan-out). Amend `workflow-dialect.md:266` "never run git" → "**never commits**" (sub-agents `git add` in their worktree) + the `:263` area note (code stages in the worktree index, docs in `.jigc/tasks/<sub>/`) + document the WF4 provisioning `Run:` step in the `milestone-execution` body (`:230-238`); update the drifted `:343` "no worktrees" pointer + `VISION.md:216`'s "milestone worktree orchestration" open-question pointer (closes *with* worktrees, for code). **Gate-vacuous → its own commit** (the M16-T5 / M30-T2 separate-doc-commit pattern).

**Proves:** the storage.md/workflow-dialect.md model states the worktree code-isolation contract with the 2026-06-04 rationale honored (docs never git-merged); no "fan-out is not a worktree case" / "two combine-modes" / "no worktree machinery" wording remains on the fan-out-truth surfaces; the doc-store→jigc_home invariant has a design-doc home; inbound anchor links still resolve; the full gate stays green. design_refs: DECISIONS 2026-06-20 M31 planning (the gated-first revise); storage.md; workflow-dialect.md.

### Increment 2 — WF3: the `repo_root` / `jigc_home` split + doc-store→jigc_home rebind (the invasive heart, highest surface)

**Deliverable:** `.jigc/` and the committed doc-store resolve against **jigc_home** (the main checkout) while code/index/HEAD resolve against `repo_root` (the worktree, when in one) — behavior-preserving **outside** worktrees (every existing test green, including the ~20 fake-`.git` fixtures).

**Grouped scope:** add a **separate `jigc_home()` resolver** (`dirname(git rev-parse --git-common-dir)`, shelling to git only when a real `.git` is present — `setup.rs:244` has the pattern); keep `discover_repo_root` as the worktree-finder (the layered design that keeps the fake-`.git` unit tests green). Thread `jigc_home` through the **~55 cli/src `.jigc` join-sites** (the 11 walk-up resolver copies + 5 `require_project_layer` copies feed it) and the **~31 committed-doc-store `repo_root.join(<location>)` engine sites** (`engine::store::canonical_path` + the `file_state`/`index`/`compose`/`state` readers — the human's jigc_home-bind choice, correct-by-construction). Fix `reenter_in_repo` (`start.rs:1419`) to resolve `.jigc`/task-dir via `jigc_home` (not the cwd-derived worktree `repo_root`); route the base-pin HEAD read deliberately (the worktree HEAD == the milestone pin under WF4). The engine already takes `jigc_root` as a parameter separate from `repo_root` — the CLI is the only fuser.

**Proves:** outside any worktree, `jigc_home()` returns the identical path the walk-up did (spiked) and the **whole existing suite is byte-identical green** — the fake-`.git` fixtures included (the layered resolver protects them); a manual worktree spike resolves `.jigc`/config/doc-store against the main checkout from inside a worktree (the previously-broken `reenter_in_repo` path now finds the project); the doc-store reads/writes target jigc_home regardless of worktree base. design_refs: DECISIONS 2026-06-20 M31 planning (WF3 + the doc-store-seam fork + the layered resolver); storage.md (Inc 1).

### Increment 3 — WF4: worktree provisioning verb + the M8-template reopen + spawn binding

**Deliverable:** a CLI verb provisions N detached worktrees at the milestone base pin before the fan-out, the `milestone-execution` workflow emits it as a `Run:` step, and each `Spawn:` line directs its sub-agent **into its own worktree**; teardown removes the worktrees on success and on abort.

**Grouped scope:** a new `milestone` sub-verb (clap variant + dispatch arm + `run_*` handler + a `commands.yaml` catalog entry) that adds **N detached worktrees at `read_base_pin().sha`** (not `main` HEAD) at a **deterministic, gitignored** location (a pure function of task-id, so the spawn line can render it at compose time), **idempotent** (prune/reuse a stale worktree left by a crashed run via `git worktree prune`/`--force`); a `Run:` step in `milestone-execution.yaml` **before** `implement-tasks`. **Reopen the M8 one-line spawn-template rule** (the human fork): `validate_spawn_template` (`adapter.rs:222`) relaxes to permit exactly `cd <deterministic-worktree> && jigc workflow … --task <id>` (one structured token, validated structurally — the determinism rationale preserved); `render_spawn` (`adapter.rs:187`) + `emit_fan_out_spawns` (`compose.rs:872`) gain the worktree-path token. **Teardown** beside `cleanup_subtask_areas` (`milestone.rs:598`, success) and the abort reset (`milestone.rs:566`); teardown-failure **surfaces a non-blocking warning naming the leaked worktree path + the `git worktree prune` remedy** (review A2 — pinned, not silent-log/block; a leaked worktree is heavier than gitignored scratch).

**Proves:** `jigc milestone <provision-verb> <id>` creates N detached worktrees at the base pin at the deterministic gitignored path, idempotent across a stale leftover; the composed `milestone-execution` emits the provisioning `Run:` step before the fan-out and each `Spawn:` line carries its sub-agent's worktree path; teardown removes the worktrees on a landed finalize and on an abort; the relaxed `validate_spawn_template` still rejects arbitrary multi-command shell; gate green. design_refs: DECISIONS 2026-06-20 M31 planning (WF4 + the M8-template reopen + worktree-base = pin); workflow-dialect.md (Inc 1).

### Increment 4 — the combine keystone (squash:true): N-index disjoint-apply + block-on-collision + narrowed has_diff (the data-loss fix, highest novelty)

**Deliverable:** a squash:true fan-out `finalize` combines the N worktree-staged code-sets into one commit — **disjoint-apply in task-id order, block + route the named paths on a same-file collision, never `git merge`** — committing every sub-agent's staged code and dropping none, with the main working tree clean afterward.

**Grouped scope:** a new `StagePolicy` variant carrying the worktree list (the shared `try_execute_finalize_plan` gains the N-worktree channel — `task.rs:1174`); collect each worktree's `git diff --cached --name-status` (rename-aware), compute the **block-set** = union of {staged path, rename old-path, delete path}; a non-empty cross-worktree intersection → **block + route the named paths** (the existing clash/cross-area-ref finding shape, never text-merge); else build the combined tree **off-line via a temp index** (review S2): `GIT_INDEX_FILE`=a temp index from the base tree, `git apply --cached` each worktree patch in **task-id order**, `git write-tree` → `git commit-tree` → fast-forward the ref + checkout — the live worktree/index is **never mutated until a clean commit lands**, so a failed/blocked apply needs **no destructive reset** (the `git apply --index`-into-live + `git reset --hard` rollback path would wipe unrelated main-checkout WIP — the M30 hazard on the failure path — rejected). **Preserve the milestone-finalize `base == main-HEAD` preflight** (review S1) — it enforces "main does not advance" (the sub-agent base-pin guard reads the worktree HEAD and cannot) and is the precondition for clean disjoint-apply. Narrow the milestone `has_diff` **CLI-side** to `docs-materialized OR Σ(worktree git diff --cached)` (`milestone.rs:488-490`); **do not edit** the shared `empty_commit_finding` (`finalize.rs:756`). Replace the squash:true `Sweep` selection (`task.rs:1133`). Add **worked-examples flow 33** (its prose home) + `flow33_acceptance.rs` driving the real binary.

**Proves (red on the data-loss repro):** a squash:true fan-out with two sub-agents touching **disjoint** files commits **both** sub-agents' staged code (drops none — RED before: `Sweep` under worktrees commits zero sub-agent code) + the merged docs, in one commit; two sub-agents touching the **same** file **block** naming the colliding path (incl. a rename of that path); `git status` is **clean** post-commit (no ` D` drift); the commit's **tree + message sequence are byte-identical across sub-agent feed/completion orders** (the code-combine determinism bar, hardening #7 extended — tree+message, not the timestamped commit SHA, per the `milestone.rs:982` bar); an **advanced main blocks** finalize (HEAD unchanged, nothing applied — review S1); a code-only fan-out no longer false-blocks as empty; unrelated main-checkout WIP **survives** a blocked/failed combine (the off-line temp-index build never touches it); gate green. design_refs: DECISIONS 2026-06-20 M31 planning (the combine + apply-mode + WF2 granularity + has_diff + the off-substrate gate-review); finalize.md; worked-examples flow 33.

### Increment 5 — restore the user's hooks on BOTH commit paths (squash:true WIP-safe + squash:false honest rework) + flow 9 fold-back + rebuild/re-pin + measured facts

**Deliverable:** the user's `pre-commit`/`commit-msg` hooks fire on **both** milestone-finalize commit paths (honoring `finalize.md`'s never-bypass-hooks contract) while WIP-safety holds: on **squash:true** the aggregate combine runs the hooks against the combined tree **without touching the main checkout**; under **squash:false** each per-sub-task commit carries **that sub-task's worktree-attributed code** and relays its hooks. The design docs tell **one** coherent hook story; the fan-out contract is proven on the **installed binary**.

**Grouped scope:** **(squash:true hook restoration — settled 2026-06-21, the halt resolution)** replace Inc-4's hook-less `combine_commit` `git commit-tree` with a **WIP-safe hook-running commit** — run the user's `pre-commit`/`commit-msg` hooks against the combined tree without mutating the main checkout (reuse M31's worktree machinery — e.g. `git commit -F` from a clean dedicated worktree at the combined tree, then fast-forward main; the exact shape is the planner's call, but it must honor **both** the hook contract **and** review S2's WIP-safety); flip the retargeted `milestone_finalize_relays_only_the_aggregate_hook_output` squash:true assertion back to **the hook fires** on the aggregate. **(squash:false honest rework)** rework `commit_per_subtask_messages` (`milestone.rs:632`) so each per-sub-task commit applies **that one worktree's** staged set (in id order, collision-blocked across the whole fan-out up front, reusing `combine.rs`'s block-set) + `git commit -F` per sub-task (**hooks run + relay** via the existing `git_commit`/`relay_hook_output`), reset-on-abort, replacing the tree-empty `Sweep`+`--allow-empty` form. **(review B1/B2)** "honest" = **code** per-sub-task; the **merged docs promote in the parent aggregate** commit (the by-task-id doc-join is a cross-sub-task merge with suffix resolution — `storage.md:161` — not cleanly partitionable, so docs are a milestone-level merge artifact, not a per-sub-task lie). **Fold-back (one hook story):** `finalize.md:104` (resolve the M30/M31 seam — the milestone arm is now worktree-combine, not `Sweep`) + the `fan-out` finalize transaction section (the N-worktree combine step + that **both** paths run the user's hooks WIP-safely — *not* a commit-tree carve-out) + the `squash:false` per-sub-task path + the `:74` relay note (rewire, not reword — both paths now run hooks); `worked-examples.md` **flow 9** ("a sub-agent never runs git" → "never commits / stages in its worktree", + the `tasks/<sub>/`-docs vs worktree-code split); the storage layout diagram. **Rebuild + re-pin** both bin dirs (`jigc` + `doc-code` sha256 identical across `~/.local/bin` + `~/.cargo/bin`); record measured facts under `completions/artifacts/M31/`.

**Proves:** a **squash:true** fan-out fires the user's `pre-commit`/`commit-msg` hooks on the aggregate commit (RED before — Inc-4's `commit-tree` ran none; the retargeted test flips to assert the hook fires) **and** unrelated main-checkout WIP survives a blocked/failed combine (WIP-safety preserved); a **squash:false** fan-out lays N per-sub-task commits each carrying its own worktree's code (a `git show --stat` per sub-task commit shows that sub-task's files, not an empty tree) and **relays each commit's hook output**; the **merged docs land in the parent aggregate** commit (not partitioned per-sub-task); the WF2 same-file block fires across the fan-out; `finalize.md`/`worked-examples` tell one hook story — no "sub-agents never run git", no `Sweep` milestone arm, no commit-tree hook carve-out; the fan-out commits-each-drops-none + same-file-block + hooks-fire contract holds on the **installed binary** (embedded pack, no env overrides); `completions/artifacts/M31/` records the binary sha256 + HEAD + the measured facts; gate green. design_refs: DECISIONS 2026-06-21 M31 Inc 5 fork settled (squash:true hooks restored, WIP-safe, folded into Inc 5) + 2026-06-20 M31 planning (squash:false honest rework + the doc fold-back); finalize.md; worked-examples flow 9.

## M32 · fan-out sub-task commit-doc provisioning — decouple provisioning from `selectable` (the M31-completion finding)

**Created + planned 2026-06-21** (the M31-completion split-off). The genuine `jigc workflow sub-task --task <id>` re-entry cannot provision its `commit:<sub>` doc — `provision_on_first_entry` (`crates/cli/src/start.rs:335`) gates on `!def.creates_task || !def.selectable` and `sub-task` is `creates-task: true, selectable: false`, so a real fan-out sub-agent hits **exit 1 ("no staged instance — provision it first")** at its only authoring entry point, and cannot author its commit through the CLI. The integration tests masked it: the only genuine-re-entry test (`workflow_reentry.rs:202`, case d) asserts **only** `status.success()` and never that the doc was provisioned; the provisioning-assert test (`:240`) substitutes the `single-task`/`selectable:true` fixture, escaping the broken branch. **Root cause = gate-divergence:** the `|| !def.selectable` clause is a **byte-identical transplant** of the router-catalog filter predicate (`selectable_workflows`, `:1326`) into a provisioning guard where only the `creates_task` half belonged — added incidentally by `7e5393a` (2026-06-05) under a commit message entirely about the router catalog, leaving the function's docstring still reading "Gated on `creates_task`." Meanwhile the **mint arm** (`compose_core:843`) gates on `creates_task` alone — so provisioning is governed by two predicates that silently desynced. The fix decouples provisioning from selectability via one shared predicate consumed by **both** sites.

**Baseline-verified by exercise** (3 `capability-auditor`s + 4 `gap-detector`s on the real binary; survived the independent pre-decompose review). **Audit verdict — 1 broken / 10 false-positive:** of the 11 `creates-task:true, selectable:false` workflows, only `sub-task` (the **sole** fan-out `run:` target, `steps/implement-tasks.yaml:4`) is broken; the 5 `migrate-*` ride a separate provisioner (`provision_migration_commit_doc`) and the 4 methodology + `record-change` are fresh-started via the mint arm — false-positive matches of the *flag* that never traverse the buggy *function*. **Human forks settled robust over cheap** ([DECISIONS.md](../DECISIONS.md) → 2026-06-21 M32 planning): a shared `should_provision_commit_doc(def) { def.creates_task }` (**not** the one-line flip — the defect *is* the divergence, a fragility the code has now), + **full prevention scope** (the `selectable` contract written to [workflow-dialect.md](../design/workflow-dialect.md), the sibling-census regression guards, the both-squash-mode genuine acceptance). **Confirmed negatives:** no schema/doctype/relation/grammar change (the genuinely-provisioned sub-task commit is byte-identical to single-task — `provision_commit_doc`/`fillable_form` take `(schema, slug)`, never the def); `path.exists()` (`:340`) protects the 6 mint-provisioned siblings (re-entry finds the filled doc present → no-op, no clobber); `selectable`'s router-catalog meaning + the M16 off-router invariant + `describe`'s unfiltered enumeration are untouched.

## Milestone 32 — fan-out sub-task commit-doc provisioning (decouple provisioning from `selectable`): decomposition

**Status: ✅ shipped + audited (2026-06-21).** Both increments built + validated clean (0 fix rounds); milestone audit (code-review + e2e through the real binary, overall pass) found one LOW/cosmetic finding (test comment `schema.scope`→`schema.ty`), fixed `e4dc280`; the §24 genuine-spawn artifact was run by the orchestrator (2 real concurrent sub-agents → finalize tree byte-identical to the sim, `579c315d…`, blackboard invariant witnessed — [completions/artifacts/M32/genuine-spawn.md](../completions/artifacts/M32/genuine-spawn.md)); installed binary re-pinned (`jigc` `dc5ae935…`). Full record: [DECISIONS.md](../DECISIONS.md) → 2026-06-21 M32 completion.

**Two increments, risk-first + linear:** the **gate fix + genuine re-entry acceptance** first (the headline — retires the masked defect, proven end-to-end through the real worktree fan-out in both squash modes); then the **sibling-census regression guards** (the blast-radius safety net — proves the fix's now-widened re-entry reach is safe for the 6 mint-provisioned siblings). The design-doc contract (`selectable` in [workflow-dialect.md](../design/workflow-dialect.md)) landed at **planning/Settle** (a design-workflow change), so the build carries only code + in-code comment corrections + tests. Verify baseline green at the build's base sha before starting; line numbers drift — re-confirm at each increment's plan.

### Increment 1 — the gate fix (shared predicate) + the genuine sub-task re-entry authoring acceptance (both squash modes, worktree-active)

**Deliverable:** a real fan-out sub-agent provisions **and** authors its `commit:<sub>` doc through the CLI on a genuine `jigc workflow sub-task --task <sub>` re-entry, and that authored commit flows through `join` + `finalize` in **both** squash modes — with the provisioning property reduced to **one source of truth**.

**Grouped scope:** extract `fn should_provision_commit_doc(def: &WorkflowDef) -> bool { def.creates_task }` and consume it at **both** call sites — the mint arm (`compose_core`, `start.rs:843`, replacing the inline `if def.creates_task` provisioning condition) and `provision_on_first_entry` (`start.rs:335`, replacing `!def.creates_task || !def.selectable` with `!should_provision_commit_doc(def)`, **keeping** the orthogonal first-entry `path.exists()` guard at `:340`). The catalog filter `selectable_workflows` (`:1326`) **keeps** its `|| !def.selectable` (router-membership — unchanged, correct). **Correct the two in-code claims the fix invalidates:** the `provision_on_first_entry` docstring (`start.rs:317-328` — reconcile to the now-true `creates_task`-only gate + a note that `selectable` is deliberately **not** a provisioning gate, anchored to the workflow-dialect.md contract) and the `workflow_reentry.rs:204-207` comment (distinguish "composes" from "provisions"). **Acceptance** (review-A1-sharpened, worktree-active — never a simplified no-worktree fixture that re-masks via a different shortcut): drive the **real binary** `milestone create` → `add-task` (default `sub-task`) → `provision` (M31 worktrees) → `execute` → genuine `jigc workflow sub-task --task <sub>` re-entry **from inside the worktree** → `set-field commit:<sub>#type` + `set-slot commit:<sub>#summary` → `finalize`, in **both** squash modes. squash:false stages **disjoint code per sub-task worktree** (reuse M31's `setup_squash_false` harness) and asserts the rendered per-sub-task commit message **carries the authored summary/body** (not merely `finalize` exit 0 — the provision→render→commit chain at `milestone.rs:963`); squash:true asserts the genuine commit group **materializes-then-skips** as transient and the aggregate still lands. Add the **provisioning assertion** case-(d) omitted: `jigc_home/.jigc/tasks/<sub>/docs/commit:<sub>.md` exists after re-entry — over the real `sub-task` workflow, not a `single-task` proxy.

**Proves (red on the genuine path):** on current code the re-entry `set-field` exits 1 ("no staged instance — provision it first") — **RED**; after the fix the sub-area provisions, authors, and `finalize`s in both squash modes; the squash:false per-sub-task message carries the authored prose; the provisioning assertion the masked `single-task` fixtures never made now fires over the real `sub-task` workflow (worktree write-target = jigc_home = the surface the join reads, `milestone.rs:941`); the docstring + test comment no longer conflate compose with provision; gate green. design_refs: DECISIONS 2026-06-21 M32 planning; workflow-dialect.md (the `selectable` contract); worked-examples flows 10/22/33 (the documented acceptance spine); write-commands.md (Sub-agent re-entry).

### Increment 2 — sibling-census regression guards: re-entered filled `migrate-*` commit survives byte-untouched + resume short-circuit

**Deliverable:** the fix's now-widened re-entry reach is proven **safe** for the 6 mint-provisioned siblings — a re-entered `migrate-*` (whose `commit:<id>` is auto-provisioned **filled** at mint for the no-author finalize) leaves that doc **byte-untouched**, and a `jigc start --task` resume of a `selectable:false` task short-circuits cleanly.

**Grouped scope:** a regression that mints a `migrate-*` task (`jigc migrate <src> --as <type>`), captures the **filled** `commit:<id>` bytes, drives a `jigc workflow migrate-<type> --task <id>` re-entry, and asserts the doc is **byte-identical** — the `path.exists()` guard (`start.rs:340`) makes the now-ungated `provision_commit_doc` a no-op, so the empty fillable form **never clobbers** the migration prose (Hardening #4 intact; `FALLBACK_TYPE == schema.ty`, so the guard checks the exact path the migrate provisioner wrote). A **resume guard**: mint a `selectable:false` task, resume via `jigc start --task <id>` (`provision=false`), assert the commit doc is unchanged. Name the **resume** sibling in the property census alongside {mint arm, re-entry first-touch, migrate dispatch}. These are **no-clobber regression guards** — they pass **before and after** the fix (the property the fix now depends on, locked against a future re-divergence), not RED→GREEN.

**Proves:** a re-entered filled `migrate-*` commit survives the post-fix gate byte-untouched (the empty fillable never overwrites the auto-authored migration prose); a `selectable:false` resume short-circuits cleanly; the property census names all four provisioning siblings governed by the one shared predicate; the full gate stays green. design_refs: DECISIONS 2026-06-21 M32 planning (the sibling-census verdict); auto-migration.md (Hardening #4 — the filled migration commit).

## M33 · schema/format stabilization — the freeze gate (productive-readiness G1)

**Created 2026-06-21** from the [format-stability readiness assessment](../READINESS-ASSESSMENT.md) (five-agent audit + Codex cross-model validation; [DECISIONS.md](../DECISIONS.md) → 2026-06-21 Readiness assessment). **The problem it closes:** a productive adopter inherits a migration on **every** doctype-schema or schema-format change, and the schema surface is **still moving** — `prd.requirements` was reshaped fixed-slot→repeatable on 2026-06-17 (corpus-breaking), and the schema-format engine still carries queued opens. Until the doctype set + schema-definition format reach a **declared, frozen v1**, the lock-in cost of going productive is unbounded *frequency*.

**Charter (planning will scope + decompose):** triage the deferred schema-engine opens ([document-type-schema.md](../design/document-type-schema.md) → opens: in-prose mentions / repeatable-item managed-ref edges / `prd —decomposes-into→ spec` / schema-fragment reuse / inverse-&-min-cardinality enforcement) — **build the ones a productive corpus will actually force, consciously defer the rest behind M34's migration path** (no premature generality: triage by real driver, not completeness). Then **declare the doctype set + schema-definition format v1-complete** and institute the freeze discipline: no schema-shape change ships without a versioned M34 migration. **Pairs with M34** (which bounds the *cost* of a change) and the **M17 value gate** (which justifies paying any cost — now **proven-in-regime**, [DECISIONS.md](../DECISIONS.md) → 2026-06-24 M33 + M34). **Not:** a public pack-authoring API (still future, per CLAUDE.md Non-goals). *Status: **planned with M34 as a paired unit 2026-06-24** — decomposition below. The triage of the deferred schema-engine opens resolved to **build all 5 into v1** (the freeze is a one-way door; a shape-bearing open deferred past it guarantees a future migration).*

## M34 · managed-corpus migration — the lock-in bound (productive-readiness G2)

**Created 2026-06-21** from the readiness assessment + Codex validation (verified gap: `schema_version` stamps exist only on result/probe/finalize JSON — `result.rs:17` — **not on managed Markdown docs**; `jigc upgrade` reconciles pack-definition deltas only; `jigc migrate` adopts *foreign* docs only). **The problem it closes:** there is **no automated path to migrate a managed-document corpus across a breaking schema / dialect / layout change** — and (per the M33/M34 baseline audit, 2026-06-24) the corpus is not even *detected* non-conformant when the **schema** changes under stable bytes: store-scope `jigc validate` detects byte-drift-from-baseline, **not** corpus-vs-current-schema non-conformance (a schema-broken doc passes `validate` clean, exit 0). So both halves are net-new — the **store-scope schema-conformance detector** *and* the **transform arm** — and the repair, absent them, is **manual** or a lossy per-file re-migrate. This is the machinery that **bounds the lock-in cost** the productive-go decision turns on. **Distinct from** the self-hosting "v1 in-place migration" deferral (adopting *jigc's own* working docs onto managed doctypes, post-spine, gated behind the M17 value-proof — and carrying the planning-gate-record encode obligation, [decisions-pending.md](decisions-pending.md)): that is jigc-self-adoption, kept independently keyed; M34 is the *general* v1→v2 corpus-upgrade transform any adopter's corpus rides.

**Charter (planning will scope + decompose):** a **per-doc schema-version stamp** (a managed doc records the schema version it was authored against — settled as a *schema-declared, engine-valued* field, the `status`/`date` model: in-schema so adding it is a real `added-field` migration the transform proves on); a **store-scope schema-conformance detector** (the net-new "detect half" — a 5th `validate` family that re-parses each committed doc against the current schema); an **`upgrade`-class transform** that migrates a v1 managed corpus to a v2 schema — **deterministic where the change is structural** (add optional field, widen cardinality, fixed-slot→repeatable-with-default: **CLI-owned, schema-diff-driven, no LLM**), **routed to the agent/human where it needs new prose** (never CLI-authored — same determinism boundary as `auto-migration.md`'s Framing A; *not* a reuse of `migrate`'s LLM re-author path, which adopts foreign→fresh docs and never re-opens a managed doc). **Composes with M33** (freeze reduces change *frequency*; M34 bounds change *cost* — together they make a productive corpus safe). **Honest boundary:** M34 makes format change *survivable*, not *free* — a prose-needing migration still costs an authoring pass. *Status: **planned with M33 as a paired unit 2026-06-24** ([DECISIONS.md](../DECISIONS.md) → 2026-06-24 M33 + M34); decomposition below.*

## Milestone 33 — schema/format freeze (declare + complete + enforce v1): decomposition

**Planned + settled 2026-06-24** ([DECISIONS.md](../DECISIONS.md) → 2026-06-24 M33 + M34; design [corpus-migration.md](../design/corpus-migration.md)). Planned **with M34 as a pair** but **decomposed as two sequential milestone builds, M33 first** — M33 completes + freezes the v1 shape and enforces it **dev-side** (a schema change can't silently ship); M34 then builds the **corpus-side** migration machinery onto that frozen base. The value gate is **proven-in-regime** (the 2026-06-23 long-horizon study — jigc strictly beats static for Sonnet × long-horizon × move-drift, blind-confirmed, prose-gap fixed — its verdict names M33/M34 the productive-go gate). **Four increments, risk-first + linear.** The freeze is a one-way door, so **all 5 deferred schema-engine opens are built into v1** (Inc 2 the shape-bearing three, Inc 3 the two additive). Every byte-mutating increment carries the **red obligation** (arbitrary-instance round-trip byte-identity, proven before green). M33 retires **productive-go G1**.

### Increment 1 — Byte-stability fuzz floor (the freeze prerequisite)

**Deliverable:** extend the `#1-risk` byte-stability proptest (`arb_doc()`, `write.rs`) from `commit`/`adr` to the four un-fuzzed persisted doctypes — `prd`, `spec`, `changelog`, `arch-doc` — generating arbitrary conformant instances; fix any byte-instabilities surfaced (the M26 empty-slot/field-group class is the prior art).
**Grouped scope:** the arbitrary-instance generators for the four doctypes (incl. `changelog`'s two-level nesting + multi-word section id + optional field — the riskiest shape); `no-op write byte-identical` + `single-field-edit changes only that field` over all six; remediation of any instability found.
**Proves:** arbitrary conformant instances of **all six** persisted doctypes round-trip byte-identical — the precondition to declare byte-frozen v1 and to safely let the opens (and M34's stamp) mutate these doctypes. design_refs: corpus-migration.md (the fuzz prerequisite); document-type-schema.md (byte-stability).

### Increment 2 — Complete the v1 doctype graph (the prd↔spec edge + completeness)

**Deliverable:** the schema-engine opens that complete the frozen doctype graph — the **`spec.derived-from → prd`** ref (the prd→spec edge, modeled **spec-side** per the M9-settled `project-setup.md` + `document-type-schema.md`: stored on the spec, the PRD's `has-specs` derived inverse never stored), **inverse/min-cardinality** enforcement ("a PRD must have ≥1 spec" via the inverse-card on `has-specs`), and **repeatable-item managed-ref edges** (a general edge-extractor completion — wire `index.rs`'s extraction to recurse repeatable bodies, today silently inert).
**Grouped scope:** the `spec` `derived-from` ref field (introduces a `---` header block to `spec`, which has none today) + its `ref-resolves` finalize coverage; the **forward `card` pinned at task decomposition** (`0..1` unless spec-authoring guarantees a PRD — must not author-require `derived-from` for a PRD-less spec, the halt's deadlock guard); the `inverse: has-specs, inverse-card: "1..*"` declaration + the store-scope `schema-completeness.inverse-cardinality` check counting each PRD's inbound `derived-from` edges (advisory, never a per-task finalize gate — `document-type-schema.md:128`); the byte-neutral edge-extractor branch for repeatable-item refs. The `spec.derived-from` add-field is **pre-freeze shape completion** (test fixtures updated in-task) and the reference schema-shape change M34's transform replays.
**Proves:** `spec.derived-from → prd` resolves at finalize (blocks when it dangles — the proven `supersedes`/`implements` pattern; passes when the target PRD exists); a PRD with zero inbound `derived-from` edges surfaces the min-cardinality finding at store scope; a repeatable-item ref enters the edge index. design_refs: doctype-map.md (the edge); project-setup.md + document-type-schema.md (the spec-side `derived-from` model); validation.md.

### Increment 3 — The additive v1 schema-format completions

**Deliverable:** the two additive opens that finish a clean frozen v1 (no instance-byte change, no migration penalty) — **in-prose mentions** (the `schema-conformance.mention-resolves` check, settled M33) and **schema-fragment `include`** (de-duplicate the `changelog` change-group block).
**Grouped scope:** the slot-prose **managed-mention** scanner (`#<type>:<slug>`, `<type>` a known doctype — a bare external `#issue-42` is *not* a mention) + the new **`schema-conformance.mention-resolves`** check id, **default severity advisory**, **store-scope only** (never a finalize gate — "lighter than `field`-refs"), resolving by the `committed_reachable` rule; the `include` directive in the schema-definition format + the changelog change-group de-dup, byte-identical render.
**Proves:** a managed mention `#<type>:<slug>` in slot prose pointing at a non-existent doc is **reported** at store scope (advisory), while an external `#issue-42` and a resolving mention both pass; the `changelog` schema de-duplicates via `include` with byte-identical output. design_refs: document-type-schema.md (In-prose mentions, settled M33) + validation.md (mention-resolves); corpus-migration.md (v1 completion).

### Increment 4 — Declare v1 + institute the freeze (the dev-side enforcement gate)

**Deliverable:** the versioned/hashed **doctype-set manifest** + the **pack-load enforcement assertion** + the v1 declaration + doc fold-back — the productive-readiness **G1** retirement.
**Grouped scope:** the enumerated `doctype → schema-version + schema-hash` manifest; the **engine pack-load assertion** recomputing each doctype's hash vs the manifest, **failing loudly** on an un-migrated change (the intrinsic-floor-assertion sibling — Review Finding 3, *not* the report-only `validate` sweep); the v1 declaration in [doctype-map.md](../implementation/doctype-map.md); the **freeze-discipline invariant** added to [CLAUDE.md](../CLAUDE.md) (now that it is enforced); the stale `document-type-schema.md` "placeholders pending implementation" line already corrected at planning; `worked-examples.md` freeze-enforcement flow + acceptance on the real binary; rebuild + re-pin both bin dirs; measured facts under `completions/artifacts/M33/`.
**Proves:** the doctype set + schema-definition format are declared **frozen v1** with a machine-checkable manifest; a schema-shape change that bumps no version is **blocked at pack-load**; productive-go **G1** is retired. (The *means* to satisfy a blocked change — the corpus migration — is M34.) design_refs: corpus-migration.md (the freeze); doctype-map.md (the v1 set); CLAUDE.md (the invariant); worked-examples.md.

## Milestone 34 — managed-corpus migration (detect → transform → stamp): decomposition

**Planned + settled 2026-06-24** ([DECISIONS.md](../DECISIONS.md) → 2026-06-24 M33 + M34; design [corpus-migration.md](../design/corpus-migration.md)). Builds the **corpus-side** machinery onto M33's frozen v1: detect a corpus stranded by a schema change, deterministically migrate it, and stamp every doc with the version it was authored against. The baseline audit (exercised on the real binary at `3d0190e`) **overturned the charter's "reconciliation already provides the detect half"** — store-scope `jigc validate` detects byte-drift only, never schema non-conformance under stable bytes (exit 0); so the detector, per-doc stamp, schema-diff, and transform are **all net-new**. **Three increments, risk-first + linear.** M34 retires **productive-go G2**.

### Increment 1 — Store-scope schema-conformance detector (the 5th `validate` family, the detect-half)

**Deliverable:** the **fifth content family** (after the four integrity families; M33 added the separate `inverse-cardinality` completeness + `mention-resolves` prose checks) that re-parses **every committed doc against the current resolved schema** and reports each `schema-conformance.{required-slot-present, required-field-present, field-value-conformant, ref-resolves}` break — closing the silent-drift hole (a schema-broken doc with stable bytes is invisible today, exit 0).
**Grouped scope:** lift the **pure** `conformance_for`/`schema_conformance` (today wired at task scope only) to store scope over the existing `file_state::detect_committed_store` doc-walk (not the anchor-scoped enumerator); wire into `validate_store_families` (alongside the families M33 added — `inverse-cardinality`, `mention-resolves`); reuse the `schema-conformance.*` check ids (no new severity surface — the M33 store-family pattern: `mention-resolves` already lifted `schema-conformance.*` to store scope, prose-scan only; this family adds the full per-doc re-parse); report-only exit 0.
**Proves:** a committed doc made non-conformant by a schema change under stable bytes is **detected** at store scope; `jigc validate` reports it; a conformant store stays exit 0. The detect-half, built not inherited. design_refs: validation.md (the fifth family); corpus-migration.md.

### Increment 2 — The deterministic transform engine (the riskiest net-new)

**Deliverable:** the schema-diff classifier + the CLI-owned structural transform driver — the migration engine, robust (no LLM in the structural path).
**Grouped scope:** a **schema-diff** over two `Schema` values classifying each change (`added-optional-field`, `widened-cardinality`, `fixed-slot→repeatable-with-default`, `prose-needing`); the transform driver emitting **byte-stable splices** over `write.rs` primitives, including the **net-new `fixed-slot→repeatable-with-default` promotion** (no primitive today); **Framing-A prose routing** for a new-required-slot (mint empty → route to agent → conformance-gate). **WIP-safety / atomicity** per-doc-gated (the property census + the M35-rename rollback-inventory discipline).
**Proves:** the hardest transform (fixed-slot→repeatable-with-default) round-trips **byte-stable, no LLM**; a prose-needing change routes to the agent and blocks until authored; acceptance e2e **replays reconstructed historical shape changes** deterministically on v0-shaped instances — **`fixed-slot→repeatable-with-default`** (the reconstructed M25 `prd.requirements` reshape, the riskiest net-new primitive) **and `widened-cardinality`** — the two branches with **no live case**, provable only synthetically; the schema-diff classifier is unit-tested over all four kinds. The **`add-field`** branch is **not** synthesized here — it earns its live e2e proof in Inc-3's stamp dogfood (the branch-split, [DECISIONS.md](../DECISIONS.md) → 2026-06-25; `spec.derived-from` is *not* a case — it shipped in M33). design_refs: corpus-migration.md (the transform); finalize.md (transactional boundary).

### Increment 3 — The in-schema schema-version stamp + the dogfood corpus migration (the loop end-to-end)

**Deliverable:** the per-doc schema-version stamp (schema-declared, engine-valued) + the corpus stamped to v1 **through Inc 2's add-field transform** (the real dogfood) + the version-aware detector routing — the full detect→block→migrate loop, and the **G2** retirement.
**Grouped scope:** the in-schema stamp field (engine-injected uniformly) + the **"current active schema version" deriver** (net-new — the on-create deriver is date-only today) + render/parse for the header-less doctypes (introduces a `---` block to `prd`/`changelog`; `spec` already carries a `meta` header from M33, so its stamp adds to the existing block); the **stamp-flips-last** rule (Review Finding 2 — only after prose-pending slots are authored + the gate passes); run the v0→v1 stamp migration via the transform — **this is the branch-split's live `add-field` e2e** (the one genuinely pending shape change); the Inc-1 detector gains **version-aware migrate-vs-corrupt routing** (`below-version ⇒ migrate`, `at-version + non-conformant ⇒ corrupt`); `worked-examples.md` flows (detect / migrate / dogfood) + acceptance on the real binary; rebuild + re-pin both bin dirs; measured facts under `completions/artifacts/M34/`. *(The dogfood runs over a committed dev-pack corpus in a fixture repo — this dev repo has no committed dev-pack-6 instances, only methodology doctypes, out of the frozen set.)*
**Proves:** every persisted doctype carries its authored-against version; the corpus is stamped v1 through the **real** transform (the dogfood is the live `add-field` e2e — the schema-diff classifier on a genuine `added-field` over the actual corpus); the detect→block→migrate loop runs end-to-end on the shipped binary for the **live add-field (stamp)** case. (Productive-go **G2** retires once the **structural** verb path lands — Inc 4 — so the loop migrates a real v1→v2 shape change, not only the stamp.) design_refs: corpus-migration.md (the stamp); storage.md + document-type-schema.md (the field); validation.md (version-aware routing).

### Increment 4 — Prior-schema sourcing + the verb's structural-migration path (G2)

**Planned + settled 2026-06-25** ([DECISIONS.md](../DECISIONS.md) → 2026-06-25 M34 audit triage; design [corpus-migration.md](../design/corpus-migration.md) → Prior-schema sourcing). The M34 completion audit found the shipped `jigc migrate-corpus` hardcodes `from = strip_stamp(current)`, so its diff can only ever yield the stamp add-field: the structural transform branches (built + unit-proven since Inc 2) are **unreachable through the verb for a real case**. This increment supplies the missing sourcing seam so a genuine v1→v2 structural change migrates end-to-end through the binary — retiring **G2** on a *real* corpus migration, not only the stamp.

**Deliverable:** the versioned **`schema-snapshots/<type>.v<N>.yaml`** store + reader, per-doc stamp-keyed `from`-selection in the verb, the stamp **value-bump** (the v1→v2 transition), and a reconstructed-case acceptance driven through the real binary.
**Grouped scope:** a new **`schema-snapshots` pack resource kind** + `load_prior_schema(pack, ty, version)` (parsed through `load_pack_schema` so field-types resolve + the stamp injects identically into `from`/`to`); rework `migrate_corpus.rs` to drop `strip_stamp(to)` and resolve `from` **per committed doc by stamp** (absent → `strip_stamp(current)`, the unchanged Inc-3 path; `k<current` → snapshot `vk`; missing snapshot → **blocked** with a route, never silent `already-current`); the **stamp value-bump** via `write::set_field` for a present-but-below stamp — closing the Inc-3 detector/verb divergence ([DECISIONS.md](../DECISIONS.md) → audit Finding 2: a doc routed `migrate` is migrated, never `already-current`); snapshot hashing **deferred** (a drift fails the transform loudly). The engine substrate (`schema_diff` all four kinds, `transform`, `promote_slot_to_repeatable`) is **already built — no engine change**.
