The control-plane half — fan-out/join step kinds, the `{{milestone.tasks}}` data-value root, the adapter spawn binding + `jigc workflow` re-entry + the write-time barrier, the `milestone-execution` workflow, and the genuine real-spawn end-to-end — is [M8](#m8--milestone-execution--spawn-binding--acceptance-control-plane).

## Milestone 8 — milestone execution, spawn binding + acceptance: decomposition

Cut 2026-06-04 via the [`/milestone-plan`](../.claude/commands/milestone-plan.md) command. Three `capability-auditor`s established the baseline (**all eight pieces genuinely net-new, nothing half-scaffolded**; the design docs more settled than expected; the decisive finding: **the M7 join's input preconditions are met only by fixture-staging today** — `copy_in` has zero production callers so `edited-from-base`/`same-doc-clash` is unreachable from the binary, and `add-task` mints areas with no `docs/`). Four `gap-detector`s + an independent **design-reviewer** + the **forward-looking harness review** (the conditional Settle phase for an off-substrate milestone) shaped it ([DECISIONS.md](../DECISIONS.md) 2026-06-04). The four pre-loaded ledger items all settled, plus two **new** forks the ledger didn't carry (the finalize-less sub-workflow; the spawn-emit marker convention). The human chose the **fuller M8** at every fork — per-sub-task authored commits + the `finalize.fan-out.squash` knob, the full edit-staging path (`copy_in` into production), the new `jigc workflow <W> --task` verb, the two-half acceptance. The forward review confirmed the M7 lesson again (**no new principle #8 — three new faces of #4/#5/#7** + the structural fact that the genuine spawn is unprovable by any automated gate → an orchestrator-driven completion artifact). Design homes: [workflow-dialect.md](../design/workflow-dialect.md) (step kinds, the `Spawn:` emit class, `{{milestone.tasks}}`, the `milestone-execution`/`sub-task` workflows), [write-commands.md](../design/write-commands.md) (the re-entry verb + the write-time barrier + copy-on-first-touch), [assistant-adapter.md](../design/assistant-adapter.md) (the spawn binding + install-time validation), [finalize.md](../design/finalize.md) (the squash knob), [validation.md](../design/validation.md) (the two new `workflow-refs` checks); acceptance = [worked-examples.md](../design/worked-examples.md) flow 10. Risk-first, linear: the compose-side spine first, the re-entry + the write→join seam (the #1 integration risk) next, the adapter, then the pack + the genuine two-half acceptance. **Status: planned, not yet built.**

**Pack content rides along.** The `sub-task` + `milestone-execution` workflows + their steps accrete in increment 5; the `finalize.fan-out.squash` + the two new `validation.workflow-refs.*` knob declarations in `knobs.yaml` accrete where first needed (increments 5 and 1).

### Increment 1 — `{{milestone.tasks}}` root + `fan-out`/`join` step kinds + the `Spawn:` emit class (the compose-side spine)

**Deliverable:** composing a workflow with a `fan-out` step over `{{milestone.tasks}}` (and a matching `join` step) emits **N deterministic `Spawn:` directives** (one per id-sorted sub-task, each carrying the `jigc workflow <W> --task <id>` re-entry command) — proven on fixtures (a test milestone + a fan-out workflow), not yet a real spawn or the pack workflow.

**Grouped scope:**
- The `milestone` data-value root: a `milestone` field on `ComposeContext` + a resolver arm mirroring `store`'s collection-leaf shape, fed the id-sorted `TaskList::enumerate()` output; `{{milestone.tasks}}` resolves to a collection of bare sub-task ids; `milestone.*` is no longer an `undeclared-root` error ([workflow-dialect.md](../design/workflow-dialect.md) → data-value roots).
- `fan-out`/`join` **step kinds**: `load_step_def` **parses and honors** step front-matter (today it strips-and-discards — a `fan-out:` marker composes green as inert prose); `StepDef` gains a kind (`fan-out {over, run}` / `join {}`); a plain step stays kindless ([workflow-dialect.md](../design/workflow-dialect.md) → On-disk definition format).
- The **`Spawn:` 5th emit class** in `emit_line`: a `fan-out` step resolves its `over:` collection and emits one `` Spawn: `<re-entry-cmd>` `` per id, in id order (the CLI payload; the adapter launch wrapper is increment 4) ([workflow-dialect.md](../design/workflow-dialect.md) → Emitted format).
- `workflow-refs`: the new **`spawn-marker-not-shadowed`** + **`fan-out-join-paired`** checks (intrinsic, floored) + their `knobs.yaml` keys; inventory 18 → 20 / 11 → 13 intrinsic ([validation.md](../design/validation.md) → Severity inventory).
- **Determinism (hardening #7 + the #5 face):** the N `Spawn:` directives are byte-identical and id-ordered across ≥2 divergent orders; a `fan-out` step composes **differently** than the same step with the marker absent (proving the marker is honored, not parsed-but-ignored).

**Proves:** the compose-side fan-out spine — deterministic spawn-directive emission from `{{milestone.tasks}}` — in isolation, engine-testable, before any real spawn or write.

### Increment 2 — `jigc workflow <W> --task <id>` re-entry + `add-task --workflow` + sub-area provisioning (the re-entry spine)

**Deliverable:** `jigc workflow <W> --task <id>` composes `W` for a milestone sub-task, **asserts `W` equals the sub-task's recorded mint workflow**, and **provisions the write-ready area on first entry**; `jigc milestone add-task`/`add-from-spec` gain `--workflow <id>` (default `sub-task`) and record it — proven through the binary on a milestone with sub-tasks.

**Grouped scope:**
- `add-task`/`add-from-spec`: the `--workflow` arg (default `sub-task`) recorded in the sub-task area, replacing the hardcoded `SUB_TASK_WORKFLOW = "single-task"` ([write-commands.md](../design/write-commands.md) → Minting a milestone).
- `jigc workflow` clap verb + dispatch: compose `W` for the named sub-task; the **W-equality guard** (mismatch with the recorded workflow → reject, fails loudly on a stale template); distinct from `jigc start --task` (top-level resume) ([write-commands.md](../design/write-commands.md) → Sub-agent re-entry).
- **Provision-on-first-entry**: the first re-entry provisions the sub-workflow's deterministic instances (the commit doc, etc.) into `tasks/<sub>/docs/` — mirroring `start`'s mint-time provisioning, deferred to first entry so unspawned areas aren't provisioned.

**Proves:** a fanned sub-agent can re-enter, get the *same deterministic composed workflow*, and land in a write-ready isolated area — the re-entry contract, in isolation, before the write path rides on it.

### Increment 3 — the write-time `--task` barrier + copy-on-first-touch (the write→join seam, the #1 integration risk)

**Deliverable:** `jigc doc <verb> --task <id>` scopes staging writes to the named sub-area (a staged-doc destination outside `tasks/<id>/` is rejected at write-time); editing a doc that existed at the milestone base **copies-in → `edited-from-base`**, a `create` stages `created`, provenance recorded **write-once, keyed on base-membership**; so the M7 join's `same-doc-clash` / collision-suffix are reachable from **real `--task` writes** — proven through the binary (real writes feed the existing join, not fixtures).

**Grouped scope:**
- The `--task <id>` selector on `jigc doc create`/`set-field`/`set-slot`/`add-item`; active-task resolution (explicit wins; else single; else reject); the **barrier** (reject a staged-doc destination outside the named sub-area; `bind`'s committed-store target is exempt — it stages nothing) ([write-commands.md](../design/write-commands.md) → The write-time barrier).
- **copy-on-first-touch**: `copy_in` wired into the production `--task` edit path (first write to a base-existing doc copies the committed body, then splices); `provision_commit_doc` records `created` provenance; provenance is write-once and sticky-`created` ([storage.md](../design/storage.md#the-by-task-id-join-m7)).
- **The seam verification (the integration-seam discipline):** assert a real `jigc workflow … --task <id>` + `jigc doc … --task <id>` sequence produces exactly the `docs/<addr>.md` + conforming `provenance.json` the M7 join consumes — replacing flow-9's hand-staging — and that a real `edited-from-base` × `created` overlap triggers the existing `same-doc-clash` block.
- **The #7-barrier face (hardening):** drive a write *through the barrier from the binary* and attempt a write *outside* the named sub-area, asserting it is refused — never pre-stage the area.

**Proves:** M8's #1 integration seam — real `--task`-scoped writes satisfy the M7 join's preconditions with correct provenance — closed through the binary, before the pack workflow drives it.

### Increment 4 — the adapter spawn binding + install-time template validation

**Deliverable:** the Claude Code profile carries a `spawn:` block (the launch template); `jigc setup` / `jigc adapter install` validates it (required placeholders + the required `jigc workflow … --task` invocation pattern + the **decidable** forbidden-pattern rule); a broken template **fails install** with a precise pointer — proven through the binary (setup with a good template + several rejected ones).

**Grouped scope:**
- `AdapterProfile` gains a `spawn` field (the model must stop rejecting it under `deny_unknown_fields`); the launch-template render (`jigc workflow {{workflow}} --task {{task_id}}` → the assistant's Task-tool invocation) ([assistant-adapter.md](../design/assistant-adapter.md) → Bind the spawn mechanism).
- Install-time schema validation: the full decidable rule (both placeholders present; the invocation pattern present; ≤1 newline in the whole rendered template, exactly one backticked span, no `<<author:`/ATX-heading/`Run:`/`Spawn:`/`> ` markers) — run at `setup`/`adapter install`/`regenerate`, a broken template is an install error.
- **The #4 face (hardening):** render the spawn template through the CLI for a real `(workflow, task_id)`, extract the rendered command verbatim, and **execute it as a process**, asserting it resolves to the real `jigc workflow … --task` verb (guards the L1 landmine — a template naming a nonexistent command).

**Proves:** the adapter owns a validated launch (the assistant-specific bit), and the launch line is an executed-bytes contract — the spawn binding, with the L1 landmine guarded at install.

### Increment 5 — the `sub-task` + `milestone-execution` pack workflows + per-sub-task commits + `finalize.fan-out.squash`

**Deliverable:** the pack ships the finalize-less **`sub-task`** workflow and the **`milestone-execution`** workflow (fan-out over `{{milestone.tasks}}` → join → milestone-finalize); **`jigc milestone execute <milestone-id>`** composes the fan-out over the milestone end-to-end (the new milestone-family verb — the invocation-surface fork settled 2026-06-05, [DECISIONS.md](../DECISIONS.md)); the **`finalize.fan-out.squash`** knob (default `true`) shapes the commit (one CLI-synthesized aggregate vs per-sub-task authored commits in id order) — proven through the binary on a milestone (driving increments 1–4, sub-agents simulated as N separate binary invocations).

**Grouped scope:**
- **The `jigc milestone execute <id>` verb + the `ComposeContext.milestone`-feeding dispatch (the CLI compose path — cut first).** A new verb in the `jigc milestone` family (clap + dispatch): resolve the milestone work-unit, read its id-sorted `TaskList::enumerate()` into `ComposeContext.milestone`, and compose the `creates-task: false` `milestone-execution` workflow over it with **no mint** (every production `ComposeContext` passes `milestone: Vec::new()` today — this is the net-new wiring that makes `{{milestone.tasks}}` resolve against a real milestone). Settles the flow-10 vs `start`-exclusivity contradiction without touching `start`'s `--workflow`/`--task` exclusivity ([write-commands.md](../design/write-commands.md) → Minting a milestone; [DECISIONS.md](../DECISIONS.md) 2026-06-05).
- Pack: the `sub-task` workflow (`creates-task: true`, `allows-create: [{type: adr, as: decision}]`, no `finalize` step — fan-out-free by construction) + its steps (`locate`/`implement`/`author-commit`); the `milestone-execution` workflow (`creates-task: false`) + the `implement-tasks` (fan-out) / `join-tasks` (join) / `milestone-finalize` steps; a `when` hint ([workflow-dialect.md](../design/workflow-dialect.md) → On-disk definition format).
- `finalize` fan-out: the **`finalize.fan-out.squash`** knob + both modes — `true` → one aggregate CLI-synthesized commit (the M7 form); `false` → one commit per sub-task in id order rendering each authored commit doc, plus the parent's — each **byte-identical / id-ordered across feed orders** ([finalize.md](../design/finalize.md#fan-out-finalize)); `knobs.yaml` declares the knob.

**Proves:** the end-to-end pack composition — a real milestone fanned out, recombined by the M7 join, and committed under both squash modes deterministically — everything but the genuine assistant launch.

### Increment 6 — the two-half acceptance (flow 10, the headline)

**Deliverable:** **Half A** (automated `cargo test`) — `milestone-execution`'s fan-out emits N `Spawn:` directives deterministically, the test invokes `jigc workflow <W> --task <id>` as **N separate binary processes** through the real write→join, asserting **byte-identical finalize** across ≥2 divergent orders, and renders+executes the spawn template (the L1 guard); **Half B** (recorded, orchestrator-driven) — a **genuine concurrent Claude-Code-Task-tool spawn** of ≥2 sub-agents into isolated areas, recombined by the join, with the committed **tree-hash asserted to match Half A's golden** and the transcript **witnessing the blackboard invariant** — a milestone-completion **audit artifact, not a CI gate**.

**Grouped scope:**
- The flow-10 Half-A e2e in a throwaway repo: the N-process sim through the real binary over the full `create`/`add-from-spec` → `milestone-execution` → join → finalize arc ([worked-examples.md](../design/worked-examples.md) → flow 10).
- The Half-B procedure: documented + run by the orchestrator at milestone completion (the genuine spawn + tree-hash match + blackboard witness), per [milestone-completion-workflow.md](milestone-completion-workflow.md) → the spawn-class artifact. **The milestone is not shippable until the Half-B artifact exists and matches** (a missing artifact is a blocking completion finding — the hollow-spawn trap).

**Proves:** M8's headline — **parallel sub-agent work stays reproducible under the blackboard model end-to-end**: the CLI emits spawn instructions deterministically, the adapter launches real concurrent sub-agents, the M7 join recombines them into one reproducible commit. Closes the milestone-execution primitive.

### Status

All six increments (1–6) complete as of 2026-06-05 — the milestone-execution control plane works end-to-end: `fan-out`/`join` step kinds (parsed-and-honored from step front-matter), the `Spawn:` 5th emit class, the `{{milestone.tasks}}` data-value root, the `jigc workflow <W> --task <id>` sub-agent re-entry (W-equality guard + provision-on-first-entry), the write-time `--task` barrier + copy-on-first-touch (`edited-from-base` reachable from the binary), the adapter `spawn:` binding + decidable install-time template validation, the `sub-task` + `milestone-execution` pack workflows entered via **`jigc milestone execute <id>`**, and per-sub-task authored commits gated by `finalize.fan-out.squash` (default `true`). Built via the [milestone-build](milestone-build) harness, hardened this cycle with the **M8 faces** of validation principles #4/#5/#7 + the spawn-class completion artifact. **One build halt** (inc-5 Plan) — a genuine fork the planner correctly refused to silently pick: the milestone-execution invocation surface was specified two incompatible ways across locked docs (`start --workflow … --task` vs `start`'s settled exclusivity); human-settled to the new `jigc milestone execute <id>` verb, docs reconciled, resumed ([DECISIONS.md](../DECISIONS.md) 2026-06-05). The pre-decompose design review had caught two blocking flaws *in the design* (the sub-task-workflow mismatch; a worktree-drift contradiction) + five advisories, all baked before the cut. The [milestone-completion audit](milestone-completion-workflow.md) returned **e2e overall-pass** + code-review **"deliverable genuinely holds"**, with 3 findings + 1 caveat, all human-triaged and remediated + re-verified: (FIXED `bb6a09c`) `squash:false` finalize was non-transactional — N sub-task commits landed before the aggregate with no rollback → now resets to the pre-finalize HEAD on aggregate failure; (FIXED `7e5393a`) the finalize-less `sub-task` workflow leaked into the router catalog (and the relaxation had been papered across the routing test **+ three production goldens**) → a `selectable: false` front-matter flag excludes it, all four assertions tightened; (FIXED `c5e7972`) sub-task working areas weren't cleaned on a landed finalize → now removed on the success path only (rollback path untouched); (BUILT `73dd243`) the install-time template **rejection** path was binary-unreachable → the `JIGC_ADAPTERS_DIR` adapter-source seam (mirroring M5's `JIGC_PACK_DIR`) makes it observable through the real binary. **The headline shippability gate — flow-10 Half B, the genuine concurrent Task-tool spawn — was driven by the orchestrator (main session, since a headless subagent cannot spawn the Task tool):** 3 real concurrent sub-agents, launched from the emitted `Spawn:` directives, each writing only through the `--task`-scoped CLI (blackboard invariant witnessed — no git, no sibling-area access), recombined by the M7 join (collision-suffix by task id) to a committed tree-hash **byte-identical to Half A's golden** (`18d6063…`) — proving the real launch reaches the same CLI payload + join the sim exercises, not a hollow sequential simulation.

## Milestone 9 — project setup (new & existing): decomposition

Cut 2026-06-06 via the [`/milestone-plan`](../.claude/commands/milestone-plan.md) command (scope → detect gaps → settle → review → decompose). Four `capability-auditor` subagents established a spiked baseline; four `gap-detector`s + an independent `design-reviewer` (both spiking the real binary) shaped the design ([DECISIONS.md](../DECISIONS.md) 2026-06-06). M9's risk was **scope, not a novel failure class** — the win condition was *bounding* the research-grade legacy-ingestion. Key settled calls: existing-project ingestion is **bounded to detect-and-route** (discover → classify against schemas → adopt-conformant / route-non-conformant / ignore-unmanaged), the research-grade **auto-migrate deferred** with a trigger; `prd` is driven here with **fixed prose slots** (single-word ids — pure pack data, dodging the stubbed `add-item` wall + a logged multi-word-heading parser defect); the new-project flow is its own plan-shaped **`project-setup`** workflow; "set up" stays "the install command ran." The design-review's two blockers — the ingest **adopt** action must be **net-new** (parse → conformance-gate → `index.absorb_doc` → record hash, *not* reconciliation's silent `baseline-adopt`) and **location-aware** (adopt only conformant-AND-at-location; register-only, never move/rewrite) — were baked before this cut. Design home of record: [project-setup.md](../design/project-setup.md); acceptance = [worked-examples.md](../design/worked-examples.md) → flows 11 (new) + 12 (existing). Linear, **risk-tiered**: the proven-substrate new-project on-ramp first (ships a working deliverable + the irreversibility proof), then the one net-new risk (the ingestion engine) in isolation, then the existing-project flow that wraps it. *(Deviates from strict milestone-level risk-first deliberately: the gap-spike already retired the ingestion *unknown* — the classifier is proven-buildable from `parse_sections` + `schema_conformance` — and the new-project flow both seeds the shared `prd` foundation and de-risks the command-vs-workflow framing on the proven path; the residual ingestion-engine risk is still retired in isolation, inc 2, before its flow in inc 3.)* **Status: planned, not yet built.**

### Increment 1 — `prd` + the new-project bootstrap flow (the proven on-ramp + the irreversibility proof)

**Deliverable:** `jigc setup` into a fresh repo → `jigc start --workflow project-setup "<idea>"` mints a task, the agent authors a `prd` via the create-gate, and `finalize` promotes it to `prds/` and commits a code-less `docs(prd):` commit — end-to-end; plus the binary-level no-clobber + idempotency acceptance for `jigc setup`.

**Grouped scope:**
- Pack: `schemas/prd.yaml` — `type: prd`, `location: prds/`, `id-from: title`, **fixed prose slots** `vision` / `requirements` / `context` (**single-word section ids only** — routes around the logged multi-word-heading reparse defect); **no** repeatable section, **no** ref edge ([project-setup.md](../design/project-setup.md) → Flow 1, the `prd` doctype; [document-type-schema.md](../design/document-type-schema.md)).
- Pack: the `project-setup` workflow (`creates-task: true`, `allows-create: [{type: prd, as: brief}]`, a `when` hint) + its steps (`develop-idea` → `author-prd` → `project-finalize`), mirroring the proven `plan` shape; the `as: brief` binding gates the create only (unread in M9) ([workflow-dialect.md](../design/workflow-dialect.md), [write-commands.md](../design/write-commands.md) → The create-gate).
- The bootstrap orientation copy fix (D1): the unset-project state routes to `jigc setup` (install) then names the real `project-setup` / `ingest-existing` workflows — no longer advertising an unbuilt "walks the pack choice" workflow ([bootstrap.md](../design/bootstrap.md) → orientation state 1).
- The **binary-level irreversibility acceptance** (the cheapest such proof, landed early): seed a non-trivial `CLAUDE.md` + a `.claude/settings.json` hook, run `jigc setup` **twice**, assert the human content is preserved **verbatim** (structure-aware merge, never clobber) and the second run is a **byte-identical no-op** — lifting today's unit-only coverage to the binary ([worked-examples.md](../design/worked-examples.md) → flow 11 / flow 12 assertion 3).
- e2e acceptance (throwaway repo): [worked-examples.md](../design/worked-examples.md) → **flow 11** — the new-project arc end-to-end (`prd` authored as pure pack data via create→set-slot→finalize; one `docs(prd)` commit promoting `prds/<slug>.md`).

**Proves:** the new-project on-ramp — `jigc` wires into a brand-new repo and develops an idea into its first managed `prd`, over the proven create-gate → code-less-finalize → promote substrate — and that re-running setup is safe (idempotent, non-destructive). Lowest-risk (pure pack data), shipped first, and seeds the shared `prd` schema inc 2/3 classify against.

### Increment 2 — the ingestion engine spine (the #1 net-new risk, in isolation)

**Deliverable:** an engine that, given a repo, discovers candidate markdown files, classifies each against the persisted schemas into a **location-aware** verdict (`adoptable` / `needs-reconcile` / `unmanaged`), and **adopts** an `adoptable` doc via a net-new schema-gated action — proven on fixtures (a temp repo with the four candidate shapes), not yet a CLI command/workflow.

**Grouped scope:**
- **Repo-wide discovery** — enumerate candidate `.md` beyond the declared `location:` dirs (root, `docs/`, the location dirs), **sorted** (the existing dir-read sort discipline → deterministic verdict order) ([project-setup.md](../design/project-setup.md) → Flow 2, the bounded slice; today discovery globs only `location:` dirs — `index.rs` / `file_state.rs`).
- **The location-aware N-candidate classifier** — per file, run each persisted schema through `engine::parse::parse_sections` + `engine::validate::schema_conformance`; reduce to: `adoptable` (conformant **and** under that schema's `location:`), `needs-reconcile` (under a `location:` dir but fails parse/conformance, **or** conformant but at the wrong location), `unmanaged` (outside every `location:` dir, parses-conformant against nothing). Binary parse-or-not, no fuzzy mapping ([validation.md](../design/validation.md), [parsing.md](parsing.md)).
- **The net-new adopt action** — parse → conformance-gate → `index.absorb_doc` (so an adopted `adr`'s `supersedes` edge is indexed) → record the `file-state` hash; **register-only, never moves/rewrites a file**. Explicitly **distinct from** reconciliation's silent `UNKNOWN → baseline-adopt` (which schema-checks nothing and indexes nothing) — **closing that safety hole** ([reconciliation.md](../design/reconciliation.md); [project-setup.md](../design/project-setup.md) → Flow 2).
- Tests (fixtures): a temp repo with a conformant-at-location adr, a non-conformant-in-`decisions/` near-miss, a conformant-wrong-location adr, and a freeform doc → assert the four verdicts; assert adopt populates the edge index + the `file-state` baseline and **moves no file**; assert a non-conformant doc is **never** adopted (the hole closed).

**Proves:** the one genuinely net-new surface — repo-wide discovery + location-aware classification + a schema-gated adopt that closes the silent-adopt hole — in isolation, retired before the command/workflow rides on it. The M9 #1 risk.

### Increment 3 — the existing-project ingestion flow (command + workflow + acceptance)

**Deliverable:** `jigc start --workflow ingest-existing "<intent>"` orients the agent to run `jigc ingest`, which drives inc-2's engine over the real repo and reports the triage verdicts (adopting conformant docs, routing the rest) — end-to-end through the binary.

**Grouped scope:**
- The `jigc ingest` CLI command (clap + dispatch): discover → classify → adopt-conformant → emit the **triage report** (`file × best-match type × verdict`, `needs-reconcile` rows carrying a routed [finding](../design/validation.md)); the exact verb surface + adopt-confirmation shape pinned here against the built command grammar (the M8 "spike the acceptance-flow commands" discipline) ([project-setup.md](../design/project-setup.md) → Flow 2).
- Pack: the `ingest-existing` workflow (`creates-task: false`, orient/route shape like `router`, a `when` hint) + its steps walking run-scan → review-verdicts → adopt/route; auto-appears in the router catalog (pure pack data).
- e2e acceptance (throwaway repo): [worked-examples.md](../design/worked-examples.md) → **flow 12** — the four-candidate repo classified through the binary; assert the conformant-at-location doc adopts (indexed, no file moved), the non-conformant and wrong-location docs route (not adopted), the freeform doc is left untouched; **nothing adopted without a schema check** (the hole closed end-to-end); no prose rewritten.

**Proves:** M9's existing-project headline — `jigc` ingests an existing repo's docs by **detect-and-route**, adopting what conforms and routing what doesn't, without auto-migration or clobber — the mandated acceptance path. Completes the milestone: jigc wires into a real project from zero, new **or** existing.

### Status

All three increments (1–3) complete as of 2026-06-06 — project setup works end-to-end for new **and** existing projects. **New-project:** `jigc setup` → the `project-setup` workflow develops an idea into a `prd` (pure pack data, fixed single-word prose slots) → code-less `docs(prd):` finalize promoting `prds/<slug>.md`; re-running `setup` is byte-identically idempotent and never clobbers a pre-existing `CLAUDE.md`/`settings.json` (the binary-level irreversibility proof). **Existing-project:** `jigc ingest` (composed via the `creates-task: false` `ingest-existing` workflow) does repo-wide **sorted** discovery + a **location-aware** N-candidate classifier (`adoptable` / `needs-reconcile` / `unmanaged`) + a **net-new schema-gated register-only adopt** (parse → conformance-gate → `index.absorb_doc` → file-state record) that **closes the silent `baseline-adopt` hole** — adopting only conformant-at-location docs, routing the rest, moving/rewriting nothing; deterministic/order-invariant. Built via the [milestone-build](milestone-build) harness across three risk-tiered increments with **0 build halts** and **0 in-increment fix rounds** — the spiked Settle bound held, so plan-time scope decisions converted to clean builds. The [milestone-completion audit](milestone-completion-workflow.md) returned **e2e overall-pass** + code-review **"M9 genuinely holds,"** with 3 findings (2 distinct, after dedup) — all human-triaged to fix, remediated + re-verified: (FIXED `5497c6f`) **MEDIUM** the canonical writer produced non-reparseable output when a **non-terminal** slot was filled with prose lacking a trailing newline (a pre-existing byte-stability round-trip defect, `parse::trim_span` anchored an empty slot's zero-width span at its trailing edge — the `prd` 3-slot flow was the most exposed, and a builder flow-11 test had **masked** it by always passing newline-terminated prose) → the span now anchors at the leading edge (populated slots + all byte-stability goldens untouched), and the flow-11 test is **de-masked**; (FIXED `243752c`) **LOW** `jigc ingest --help` + doc-comments advertised "read-only — adopts nothing" while the command adopts-and-persists → corrected to honestly describe the register-only adopt (agent-facing-surface honesty). The research-grade auto-migration core stays **consciously deferred** with a trigger ([decisions-pending.md](decisions-pending.md) → post-spine), as do the `prd` repeatable-requirements shape + the multi-word-heading parser fix. **M9 is the last spine milestone — the spine is complete.** The post-spine cluster (self-hosting, `describe`, the `doc↔code` pack-probe family, multi-pack) needs milestones created.

## Milestone 10 — doc↔code validation: decomposition

Cut 2026-06-06 via the [`/milestone-plan`](../.claude/commands/milestone-plan.md) command (scope → 3 `capability-auditor`s → 4 `gap-detector`s → settle → review → decompose). **The spiked baseline overturned the roadmap's reuse framing** ([DECISIONS.md](../DECISIONS.md) 2026-06-06 → M10 planning): the `Probe` trait shipped the *wrong shape* (`check(ctx)`, no `target`, a non-serializable live ctx — the "zero engine change to admit subprocess" claim was aspirational, never built), there is **no probe scheduler**, and `code-anchor` was a closed-enum engine variant with **no pack-extension hook** — so M10 is materially larger and more architectural than "the trait shipped, add the subprocess impl." M10's risk is **two new pack-extension axes at once + a locked contract that's easy to quietly waive** — a *prove-a-differentiator* milestone, narrow user-facing surface on a substantial new foundation. Four human-settled forks shape the cut: **subprocess-from-the-start** (the only path that satisfies "contract satisfied, not waived" — an in-process probe structurally can't exhibit the timeout/crash/malformed-output meta-findings); **a genuine pack-field-type extension axis** (the human chose the real axis — `code-anchor` becomes the first *pack-declared* type, adjudicator-bound to `doc-code`); **ride the editable channel** for the headline criterion (defer `add-item`); **`adr` header anchor (floor) + `spec` criterion anchor (headline)**. Anchor grammar `<repo-relative-path>#<symbol>`, resolved by **tree-sitter** (Rust grammar proven in M10; multi-language a fixture-add); **optional field, dangling-anchor blocks** (the proven `ref-resolves` mirror). The independent design-review's two blockers were baked before this cut: a `code-anchor` is a *field, not an edge*, so `doc-code`'s task-scope targets are the **task's effective-state docs carrying anchors** (created/edited ∪ bound) — the floor `adr` is **created in-task** so it is in scope; and the floor's `set-field` authoring needs the small `insert_front_matter_field`→verb wiring (distinct from the deferred `add-item`). Design homes of record: [validation.md](../design/validation.md) → The `doc-code` probe + Pack-probe determinism contract; [document-type-schema.md](../design/document-type-schema.md) → Pack-declared field types; [module-layout.md](module-layout.md) → Probe boundary; acceptance = [worked-examples.md](../design/worked-examples.md) → flow 13. Linear, **risk-first**: each new substrate retired in isolation before the next rides it — the field-type axis, then the reshaped seam, then the contract (proven with adversarial stubs **before** the real probe exists), then the real `doc-code` resolver, then the integrated acceptance. **Deferred (tracked, not gaps):** OS-level sandboxing + the `sandbox-violation` meta-finding (trusted-pack); staleness (a stretch — content-hash, never mtime); store/doc-scope `validate`; `add-item` + repeatable-item write adjudication; findings caching (recompute default). **Status: planned, not yet built.**

### Increment 1 — the pack-declared field-type extension axis + the two schema anchors (the first "first")

**Deliverable:** a pack declares a field type bound to an adjudicator probe; `code-anchor` becomes the **first pack-declared type** (out of the closed engine enum, into the dev pack, bound to `doc-code`); `adr` gains an optional `cites-code` header anchor and `spec`'s `criteria` block gains an optional `maps-to-test` anchor; `jigc doc set-field` can populate an **absent** optional header field. Proven on fixtures + the binary, not yet any probe.

**Grouped scope:**
- Engine: open the closed `FieldType` enum (`schema.rs`) to **engine-native variants + a pack-declared `(name, adjudicator-probe)` field type**; the meta-schema admits a pack-declared type and rejects an undeclared one with a typed error; the engine ships **no** pack field type (engine-empty invariant holds) ([document-type-schema.md](../design/document-type-schema.md) → Pack-declared field types).
- Engine: `check_value` gains **pack-adjudicator dispatch** — a pack-declared type runs its optional write-time *shape* check (for `code-anchor`: non-empty, single-line, parses as `path#symbol`); its real adjudication is the bound finalize-time probe (built later). Today's `String | Ref | CodeAnchor` shared arm is split.
- Engine + CLI: **wire the existing `write::insert_front_matter_field` primitive to the `set-field` verb** so an absent optional header field (`adr.cites-code`) is settable in-place (the planning-spike writer limitation; distinct from the deferred `add-item`).
- Pack: `code-anchor` declared in the dev pack (bound to `doc-code`); `adr.yaml` gains `cites-code` (optional `code-anchor` in the `status` header); `spec.yaml`'s `criteria` block gains `maps-to-test` (optional `code-anchor` leaf).
- Tests (fixtures + binary): a pack-declared `code-anchor` field parses/loads/round-trips; an undeclared pack type fails loudly at load; `set-field` populates an absent `adr.cites-code` with a `path#symbol` value; a `code-anchor`'s `#`/`/` pass the write-time shape check, control chars are rejected.

**Proves:** the **first pack-declared field type** is a genuine extension axis (engine admits a pack-supplied type + adjudicator binding, ships none itself), and both shipped doctypes carry their anchor — the field foundation, before any probe adjudicates it.

### Increment 2 — the reshaped `Probe` seam + serializable effective-state snapshot + target enumeration (the engine plumbing)

**Deliverable:** the `Probe` seam carries a **`target` + a serializable effective-state ctx**, without breaking the only existing impl (`override-default`); the engine materializes a **serializable snapshot** of the doc-code target surface — the `(target-address, anchor-value, check-id)` pairs enumerated over the task's effective-state docs (created/edited ∪ bound) + the working-tree root. Proven on fixtures with an **in-process test probe** over the serialized snapshot.

**Grouped scope:**
- Engine: reshape the `Probe` trait to `check(target, ctx)` with a **serializable** effective-state ctx (the M6 seam's `OverrideCtx` was a live, target-less handle); `override-default` is migrated onto the reshaped seam **with no behaviour change** (its M6 flow-8 tests stay green) ([module-layout.md](module-layout.md) → Probe boundary; [validation.md](../design/validation.md) → wire contract).
- Engine: the **target-surface enumeration** — walk the task's effective-state docs (working deltas ∪ bound read-roles), collect every `code-anchor` leaf as a `(target-address, anchor-value, check-id)` pair; a `code-anchor` is a *field, not an edge*, so this is direct enumeration, **never** the edge-index blast-radius ([validation.md](../design/validation.md) → Target surface).
- Engine: materialize the read-only **effective-state snapshot** (the enumerated pairs + the working-tree root) the wire contract carries by path-ref; serialization is unit-proven.
- Tests (fixtures): the reshaped seam admits an in-process test probe over a serialized snapshot; the enumeration finds anchors in a fixture task's created **and** bound docs (and skips committed docs *not* in effective state); `override-default` byte-identical through the reshaped seam (its goldens untouched).

**Proves:** the seam every probe rides now admits the doc-code shape (target + serializable ctx) and the engine can name *which* anchors a task must check — the reshape risk (not breaking the lone existing impl) retired in isolation, before the subprocess rides it.

### Increment 3 — the subprocess invoker + wire contract + the meta-findings (the contract, *satisfied not waived* — the #1 watch item)

**Deliverable:** the engine drives a **subprocess** probe over the snapshot via a CLI-owned invoker, ingests its JSON findings into the `ValidationReport`, and synthesizes the **`pack-probe-integrity.*` meta-findings** — proven with **adversarial test stubs** (well-behaved, timeout, crash, malformed-output), **before** the real `doc-code` program exists.

**Grouped scope:**
- CLI: the subprocess invoker (`std::process::Command` + a **wait-timeout**), keeping the engine **shell-free** (the engine never shells out — [finalize.md](../design/finalize.md)); the engine owns the request (snapshot path-ref) + response parse + Finding ingestion + meta-finding synthesis ([module-layout.md](module-layout.md) → Probe boundary).
- Engine: the **concrete wire contract** — request `{probe_id, target, effective_state:{snapshot_path}, config, schema_version}` → response `{findings, schema_version}` ([validation.md](../design/validation.md) → The wire contract); the six-rules enforcement points observable from the boundary (a bounded wall-clock budget; read-only invocation).
- Engine: the **three enforced meta-findings** — `pack-probe-integrity.{timeout,crash,malformed-output}`, each **intrinsic blocking** (a misbehaving probe can't be trusted) ([validation.md](../design/validation.md) → Failure semantics, What intrinsic means). `sandbox-violation` is **not** built (deferred with OS-sandboxing — no `knobs.yaml` key, not in the load-time floor-assertion set).
- Tests (fixtures): a well-behaved stub's findings ingest into the report; a stub that **sleeps past budget** → `timeout`; a stub that **exits non-zero / emits no JSON** → `crash`; a stub that **emits non-JSON** → `malformed-output`; each meta-finding is blocking and **cannot be demoted below `blocking`** (floor-rejected at resolution).

**Proves:** the determinism contract is **genuinely enforced, not a trust-the-pack stub** — the meta-findings fire against adversarial probes, proven in isolation before the real probe exists (closing the seductive-waiver failure the Codex review caught on the original roadmap). The milestone's #1 risk.

### Increment 4 — the `doc-code` probe executable (tree-sitter resolution: symbol-exists + criterion-maps-to-test)

**Deliverable:** the dev pack's `doc-code` program — a separate executable that, given the wire request + snapshot, resolves each `path#symbol` anchor against the working-tree code via **tree-sitter** and emits `doc-code.symbol-exists` / `doc-code.criterion-maps-to-test` findings (blocking on a dangling anchor). Proven on fixtures, riding the proven seam + invoker.

**Grouped scope:**
- The `doc-code` executable (a separate program, outside the engine workspace per the Probe boundary): parse the JSON request, read the snapshot, for each pair open `<path>` and resolve `<symbol>` via **tree-sitter** (Rust grammar); a bare `<path>` is a file-existence check ([validation.md](../design/validation.md) → The `doc-code` probe).
- The two checks: `symbol-exists` (the anchor resolves to a real file/symbol) and `criterion-maps-to-test` (the symbol additionally satisfies the **is-a-test predicate** — Rust `#[test]`); a **dangling** anchor (present, unresolvable) is a blocking finding mirroring `ref-resolves`; absence is not a finding.
- Determinism: static parse only — **no** `cargo`/build/network/wall-clock (contract rules 1/3/5/6); same code + same anchors → same findings.
- Tests (fixtures): a resolvable symbol/test → no finding; a deleted symbol → `symbol-exists` blocks; a `maps-to-test` pointing at a non-existent (or non-`#[test]`) fn → `criterion-maps-to-test` blocks; determinism over repeated runs.

**Proves:** the real resolver works against real code — the floor (symbol/file existence) + the headline (criterion→test) — deterministically, on the proven contract/seam substrate.

### Increment 5 — wire `doc-code` into the task gate + flow 13 acceptance (the headline)

**Deliverable:** the full loop — a work task that **binds** a spec (criterion carrying `maps-to-test`) and **creates** an adr (carrying `cites-code`) has `doc-code` run at `finalize`: it **passes** when both anchors resolve and **blocks** (no commit) when one dangles — end-to-end through the binary, with severity cascade-tunable.

**Grouped scope:**
- Engine: schedule `doc-code` in `validate_task` over the effective-state target surface (inc 2) → invoke (inc 3) → ingest; the inventory rows `doc-code.{symbol-exists,criterion-maps-to-test}` (tunable) + `pack-probe-integrity.{timeout,crash,malformed-output}` (intrinsic) land in `CHECK_INVENTORY` + `knobs.yaml` (the count reconciles to the [validation.md](../design/validation.md) inventory).
- Pack: the work workflow's create-gate already admits the `adr` (`allows-create: [{type: adr, as: decision}]`); the acceptance authors `adr.cites-code` via `set-field` (inc 1 wiring) and the `spec` criterion via the **editable channel** before mint (the spiked pin-to-base constraint).
- e2e acceptance (throwaway repo): [worked-examples.md](../design/worked-examples.md) → **flow 13** — the **passing walk** (both anchors resolve → one commit; the report **contains** both `doc-code` findings *resolving* — a zero-`doc-code`-finding report fails as hard as a missed block, the masking guard) and the **blocking walk** (a deleted test / renamed symbol → `finalize` blocks, non-zero exit, names the target + route, `git rev-list --count HEAD` unchanged); plus a **meta-finding firing** (a stub probe timing out blocks) and a **severity demotion** (`validation.doc-code.criterion-maps-to-test.severity: warning` → surfaces but doesn't block, while the intrinsic meta-findings stay floor-locked).

**Proves:** M10's headline — **documentation drift caught deterministically at the task boundary, proven by the blocking case** — over both pack-extension axes (the pack-declared `code-anchor` + the pack-provided `doc-code` subprocess probe), with the contract satisfied and severity cascade-tunable. The mandated acceptance path; completes the last deferred differentiator.

### Status

All five increments (1–5) complete as of 2026-06-06 — **doc↔code validation works end-to-end through the real binary**: a task that **binds** a `spec` (criterion carrying `maps-to-test`) and **creates** an `adr` (carrying `cites-code`) has the pack-provided `doc-code` subprocess probe run at `finalize` — **passing** when both anchors resolve and **blocking** (no commit) when one dangles. *(Full M10 completion record continues below.)*

## Milestone 11 — describe / introspection: decomposition

Cut 2026-06-07 via the [`/milestone-plan`](../.claude/commands/milestone-plan.md) command (scope → 3 `capability-auditor`s → 4 `gap-detector`s → settle → review → decompose). **The spiked baseline overturned the roadmap's "design mostly locked, planning is light" framing** ([DECISIONS.md](../DECISIONS.md) 2026-06-07 → M11 planning): the `when`-hint "authored-prose-on-definitions" reuse claim is **false on the cascade half** — `when` is read **whole-file from one layer, never cascade-merged**; definition files (workflow/doctype) are read **pack-only** (`read_workflow`/`all_schemas` bypass `file_owner`; only *step* files are layer-aware, and no `workflows/`/`schemas/` project shadow dir exists); doctype `Schema` is `deny_unknown_fields` (no authored-prose field today); and **no append/field-merge exists anywhere** in the cascade. So M11 is a *prove-a-differentiator / new-read-path-surface* milestone whose real net-new work is the **first human-authored prose carried on definitions** (`description:`/`usage:` — a third prose category, neither write-path slot nor read-path placeholder), its **cascade-read path**, and the **non-contractual output format** (no free-prose render mode exists; no test asserts a property *of* the format). Human-settled forks shaping the cut: **two fields `description`/`usage`** (load-bearing — the renderer weaves "X is …; reach for it when …") on **workflows + doctypes** (the floor; command-refs **project their existing `hint`**, pack-only; **data-value roots cut** — no definition substrate); **optional, skip-on-absent**; **cascade reflection by whole-file definition shadow** (new project `workflows/`/`schemas/` shadow dirs read via `file_owner` — a faithful extension of the proven step-shadow; **no field-merge**, honoring [overrides.md](../design/overrides.md)); **non-contractual enforced by an operational format predicate**; **`jigc describe` whole-menu only**. The independent design-review's two blockers were baked before this cut: the format predicate was re-pinned precisely (not-JSON + not-a-top-level-YAML-mapping/sequence + no key-shaped line + no per-definition extractable key + prose-density — so legitimate colon-bearing prose passes and a catalog fails), and the **command-ref `hint` is honestly pack-only** (cascade-reflection proven on workflows+doctypes only — the catalog read path is pack-only, the M4 unwired-resolver shape named openly). Design homes of record: [introspection.md](../design/introspection.md) (promoted from `ideas/describe.md`); cascade mechanism [overrides.md](../design/overrides.md) → Authored metadata on a definition; field carriers [workflow-dialect.md](../design/workflow-dialect.md) + [document-type-schema.md](../design/document-type-schema.md); acceptance = [worked-examples.md](../design/worked-examples.md) → flow 14. Linear, **risk-first**: the authored-prose substrate first (the `deny_unknown_fields` asymmetry handled), then the non-contractual read surface (the #1 watch-item — the novel output discipline, retired before the cascade rides it), then cascade reflection + the integrated acceptance. **Deferred (tracked, not gaps):** field-granular per-definition-metadata override + prose append-merge (logged with a trigger — [decisions-pending.md](decisions-pending.md)); left-behind pointers (touch the bootstrap/emit path); data-value-root introspection (needs a root-registry); the catalog cascade-delta path (would make `hint` cascade-reflecting). **Status: planned, not yet built.**

### Increment 1 — authored prose on definitions (the `description:`/`usage:` substrate)

**Deliverable:** workflow and doctype definitions carry optional `description:`/`usage:` fields that load on both types (the `deny_unknown_fields` asymmetry handled), and the **shipped dev pack authors them on all its workflows + doctypes**. Proven on loader/golden tests + the binary, not yet projected.

**Grouped scope:**
- Engine: add optional `description`/`usage` to `WorkflowFrontMatter`/`WorkflowDef` (`compose.rs`) — `WorkflowFrontMatter` is **not** `deny_unknown_fields`, so the loader must be *wired* to read them (a stray field is otherwise silently ignored — the "authored it but describe shows nothing" trap); and to the doctype `Schema` (`schema.rs`) as **top-level fields** (siblings of `type`/`location`/`id-from`) — `Schema` **is** `deny_unknown_fields`, so the struct field must land before any schema YAML carries the key, with `skip_serializing_if` so schemas that omit them leave goldens untouched ([document-type-schema.md](../design/document-type-schema.md) → Authored metadata fields; [workflow-dialect.md](../design/workflow-dialect.md) → On-disk definition format).
- Pack: author `description:`/`usage:` on **every** shipped workflow + doctype (skip-on-absent is the runtime contract, not a license to ship an under-narrated pack — [introspection.md](../design/introspection.md) → Deliverable scope), each held to facts-not-advice / usage-not-mechanism at review (`description` = what it *is*; `usage` = when/why to reach for it).
- Tests: the fields load + round-trip on both definition types; an omitted field is `None` (no error); the doctype goldens for schemas that gain prose update, the rest stay byte-identical.

**Proves:** the new authored-prose substrate — the first human-authored prose carried on definitions — parses/loads on both definition types with the `deny_unknown_fields` asymmetry handled, before any surface projects it.

### Increment 2 — `jigc describe` whole-menu projection + free-prose renderer + the format predicate (the non-contractual core, pack-only — the #1 watch-item)

**Deliverable:** `jigc describe` (no positional) emits a **discursive prose projection** of the **pack-default** resolved definitions — every workflow (the *unfiltered* set, not the selectable catalog) and doctype with their woven `description`/`usage`, plus the command-ref `hint`s — and the output is held to the **operational format predicate** (proven as a test). Reads pack-only; cascade reflection is increment 3.

**Grouped scope:**
- Engine: a projection assembler that enumerates the resolved definitions over the **unfiltered** `pack.list(Workflows)` + the full doctype set (not `selectable_workflows`, which filters `creates-task && selectable` — the wrong anchor), weaves the two fields into a structured sentence ("X is `<description>`. Reach for it when `<usage>`."), handles partial presence (one field → narrate that one; both absent → skip), and assembles command-ref `hint`s as prose; a new engine result type (no LLM call — assembled, never generated) ([introspection.md](../design/introspection.md) → The authored fields, Command surface).
- CLI: the `describe` subcommand (clap def → dispatch → `run_describe` → render, the `ingest` template) + a **free-prose renderer** (net-new — no existing render mode emits discursive prose; existing outputs are line-structured) ([module-layout.md](module-layout.md) → Renderers).
- Tests: the **format-property predicate** — output is not JSON, does not parse as a top-level YAML mapping/sequence (a bare scalar is fine), has no key-shaped line (`^\s*[\w-]+:\s`) and no bullet row, has no per-definition extractable key/delimiter (light unkeyed section grouping permitted), and meets a prose-density floor — asserted **both directions** (passes on legitimate colon-bearing prose, fails on a structured catalog); plus a byte-snapshot as necessary-but-insufficient; the projection contains the authored strings; absent-field definitions are skipped.

**Proves:** the non-contractual prose menu exists and is **provably hostile-to-parsing** — the milestone's #1 watch-item (the M10 "contract satisfied, not waived" analogue), retired before the cascade wiring rides it. A describe whose output is a clean machine-readable list has *not* built M11.

### Increment 3 — cascade reflection via whole-file definition shadow + flow-14 acceptance (the headline)

**Deliverable:** a project-layer **whole-file shadow** of a workflow/doctype definition visibly changes `jigc describe`'s output (its prose now wins), while unshadowed definitions still show pack prose — the full flow-14 acceptance end-to-end through the binary.

**Grouped scope:**
- CLI/engine: new project **`workflows/`/`schemas/` shadow dirs** (the project layer's `files` surface, today populated only from `steps/` — `project_step_ids`); route the definition reads (`read_workflow`/`all_schemas`/the describe enumerator) through `resolved.file_owner` so the highest-precedence layer's whole definition wins (replace at file granularity, **no field-merge**) — a faithful extension of `CascadeStepSource` ([overrides.md](../design/overrides.md) → Authored metadata on a definition resolves by whole-file shadow).
- e2e acceptance (throwaway repo): [worked-examples.md](../design/worked-examples.md) → **flow 14** — the **override walk** (shadow `single-task.yaml` with edited `usage:` → describe output **contains the project prose and not the pack prose** for that definition, others unchanged — a "describe mentions single-task" assertion is rejected as masking, it must assert the *override changed the output*; over a workflow/doctype, **never** a command-ref — `hint` is pack-only); the **non-contractual predicate** holding on the real output; and authoring over the **real shipped definitions**, not a renderer-shaped fixture pack (`project > pack-default` only — team layer unfed).

**Proves:** M11's headline — `jigc describe` **reflects the resolved cascade** (it can't drift — generated from the same definitions that drive composition), proven by a project override **visibly changing** the projection, not by "describe prints the list." The document model applied to jigc describing itself.

### Status

All three increments (1–3) complete as of 2026-06-07 — **`jigc describe` is a cascade-reflecting, non-contractual prose self-description surface**, built end-to-end through the real binary and independently audited clean. The engine assembler (`introspect.rs`) makes **no LLM call** — it weaves authored `description:`/`usage:` into discursive prose, skips both-absent definitions, projects command-ref `hint`s, and is deterministic (id-sorted); the CLI owns all formatting via a net-new free-prose renderer; `jigc describe` (whole-menu, no positional) enumerates the **unfiltered** workflow set (proven by `router`/`sub-task` appearing despite being excluded from `selectable_workflows`) + all doctypes. **The two load-bearing proofs both hold, end-to-end:** (1) **non-contractual enforced by format, proven *both directions*** — the agent output is not JSON, parses only as a bare YAML scalar, has no key-line/bullet/per-definition-extractable-field, meets a prose-density floor, *and* `--format json` of the same data IS a structured catalog (so the property is real, not vacuous); (2) **cascade-reflection by a *visible change*** — a project whole-file shadow of a workflow **and** a doctype makes project prose win and the pack prose for that one definition vanish, while unshadowed definitions keep pack prose, with **no field-merge** (a dropped field does not fall back to pack) and **skip-on-absent** (a field-less definition is silently not narrated); output is byte-identical across repeated runs and across divergent shadow-creation orders. Command-ref `hint` stays honestly **pack-only** (the catalog cascade-delta path is unbuilt — disclosed, not papered over). Built via the [milestone-build](milestone-build) harness across three risk-first increments with **0 design halts** (the four open-thread forks were settled at plan-time; build-health trend M9=0 · M10=0 · M11=0) — the only in-build remediation was increment 2's 1 fix round, where the build's own validation caught + de-masked a "doubled-lead" weave defect (pack `usage:` colliding with the weave template). The [milestone-completion audit](milestone-completion-workflow.md) (independent code-review + real-binary e2e, all 10 scenarios pass) returned **2 LOW findings, both human-triaged fix-now + remediated + re-verified** (`8220fd6`): the weave was de-fragilized against a period-less authored `description:` (byte-identical for the current period-ending prose), and the stale `jigc describe --help` ("pack-only in M11") corrected to describe cascade reflection (the agent-facing-honesty class, cf. the M9 `ingest --help` fix). **Deferred (tracked, [decisions-pending.md](decisions-pending.md)):** field-granular per-definition-metadata override + prose append-merge (logged with a trigger — not broken speculatively); left-behind pointers; data-value-root introspection; the catalog cascade-delta path that would make `hint` cascade-reflecting. **M11 proves the document-model-applied-to-jigc-itself differentiator — the introspection read-path surface is real.**

## Milestone 12 — self-hosting: decomposition

Cut 2026-06-07 via the [`/milestone-plan`](../.claude/commands/milestone-plan.md) command (scope → 4 `capability-auditor`s → 4 `gap-detector`s → settle → review → decompose). **The exercised baseline turned the milestone's "exploratory / dialect-discovered-by-encoding" framing from a hedge into a measured fact — in M12's favour:** the **dev-workflow proper is flat, single-pass linear** (the bounded fix-loop / hard halts / conditionals the roadmap feared live in the *increment-workflow wrapper*, not the dev-workflow), so a **reduced-linear encode runs end-to-end today with zero dialect/engine code** — spiked against the real binary on a fresh non-Rust repo (composes deterministically + `setup → start → finalize → one git commit`, git-only). Human-settled forks: **reduced-linear** encode of the **dev-workflow only** (judgment steps as prose; mechanizable degradations *named*, not built); methodology pack **subsumes** the dev surfaces (vendors `commit` + the full intrinsic-knob surface, with the `default-workflow` enum rewritten) and composes **alone** (the M14 circularity broken); success bar = **composition-fidelity + qualitative judgment check** (the quantitative build-health comparison is **deferred** — its tally lives in the external `milestone-build.js` harness, not the binary); dogfood on a `/tmp` copy of **`gherrink-galey`** (a TypeScript monorepo — unlike jigc, so jigc-shaped gates can't flatter it). The independent design-review (which re-spiked the acceptance flow against the binary) baked four flow-fidelity corrections before this cut: finalize *renders* an already-filled commit (the agent must `set-field type/scope` + `set-slot body/summary` first — four command-refs, not one), placeholders resolve **only as a lone line** (inline `{{…}}` emits literally — the M4 flow-3a class), and "vendor the knob surface" means *copy-and-edit the `default-workflow` enum*. Design home of record: [self-hosting.md](../design/self-hosting.md) (promoted from `ideas/self-hosting.md`); acceptance = [worked-examples.md](../design/worked-examples.md) → flow 15. **The acceptance bar cuts both ways** — hollowing a judgment slot *or* prose-washing a mechanizable structure without naming it both **fail** M12. **Expected outcome (an honest result, not a failure):** the encode names a dedicated **workflow-dialect-extension milestone** (five triggers: a test-first-ordering gate · a structural human-gate step · a portable gate-command knob · a bounded fix-loop + resumption · an owner-artifact gate). **Seams M12 must not cross:** the methodology's own working docs stay **plain markdown** (M13 graduates them to doctypes); dev + methodology **multi-pack co-composition** is M14. **Deferred (tracked, [decisions-pending.md](decisions-pending.md)):** the `(D)` resumption primitive (defers cleanly under reduced-linear — restart-from-scratch holds; named as trigger #4); the quantitative build-health measurement scaffolding; the Rust-only `doc-code` probe + `cargo install` relocation (gated off — the methodology pack ships no `code-anchor`). Linear, **risk-first**: the sole-foreign-pack compose + the honesty sort first (the milestone's core novelty), then the real-project dogfood (the integration proof). **Status: built + independently audited 2026-06-07 — see ### Status below.**

### Increment 1 — the methodology pack composes alone, deterministically + the graduation sort recorded (the core novelty)

**Deliverable:** a `methodology` pack (pure pack data — **zero `crates/*/src` changes**) that encodes the **reduced-linear dev-workflow** and **composes as the sole pack** via `JIGC_PACK_DIR` on a throwaway repo: `jigc start "<intent>"` emits the flat prose spine (`scope → implement → gate → finalize`) with `{{task.intent}}` resolved, **byte-identical on recompose** (`start --task`). The encode's honesty is part of the deliverable: each judgment step (scope-restatement, test-first discipline, gate) is **agent-authored prose**, and every *prose-washed mechanizable* (test-first ordering, the gate) is **named** as a dialect-extension trigger in [self-hosting.md](../design/self-hosting.md) — nothing judgment is mechanized, nothing mechanizable is silently dropped.

**Grouped scope:**
- Pack authoring: `config/defaults.yaml` (`pack-id: methodology`, `default-workflow: dev-task`, `creates-task: true`); `config/knobs.yaml` **copied from the dev pack and edited** — the `default-workflow` enum rewritten to `of: [dev-task]`/`default: dev-task` (the enum constrains the cascade value — a verbatim copy fails `no workflow 'router'`), the full intrinsic-check severity surface kept floored at blocking; `config/commands.yaml` with the command-refs the steps use; `schemas/commit.yaml` **vendored** (finalize hardcodes `COMMIT_TYPE`); `workflows/dev-task.yaml` (flat `{{include: step:…}}` body); `steps/{scope,implement,gate,finalize}.yaml` — single-word ids, **every `{{…}}` placeholder the sole content of its line**, the gate step **portable prose** (no hardcoded `cargo`), the finalize step wiring a command-ref **each** for `set-field type` + `set-field scope` + `set-slot summary` + `set-slot body` ([self-hosting.md](../design/self-hosting.md) → Acceptance flow, Verified friction).
- Tests (throwaway repo, real binary): `jigc setup` (honors `JIGC_PACK_DIR`) then `jigc start "<intent>"` composes `dev-task` and resolves `{{task.intent}}`; recompose via `start --task <id>` is **byte-identical** to the original successful capture; orient (`jigc start`, no intent) shows the methodology pack + its workflow.
- Honesty artifact: confirm the three-way sort table + named-trigger list in [self-hosting.md](../design/self-hosting.md) match the **authored** steps (the falsifiable check — bar #2's two FAIL exemplars).

**Proves:** a **sole `JIGC_PACK_DIR` pack that is not the embedded dev pack** composes deterministically (the subsume / compose-alone proof, the M14 circularity broken) **and** the encode is honest (no hollowing, no un-named prose-wash). The riskiest, most-novel half of the milestone, on a controlled repo before the real-project dogfood.

### Increment 2 — the dogfood: a real task end-to-end on `gherrink-galey` (flow 15 acceptance, the integration proof)

**Deliverable:** on a **`/tmp` copy** of `gherrink-galey` (a TypeScript pnpm monorepo — the original **never touched**), the full flow-15 walk runs: `jigc setup` → commit the setup artifacts → `jigc start "<a real planned-but-unbuilt task>"` → the agent does the TS work (writes a failing vitest test, implements, runs `pnpm test`/`biome` by hand — the prose gate) → fills the commit doctype (`set-field type/scope` + `set-slot summary/body`) → `jigc task finalize` lands **exactly one code-only git commit**, git-only (no `cargo`).

**Grouped scope:**
- e2e acceptance (throwaway `/tmp` copy of the real project): [worked-examples.md](../design/worked-examples.md) → **flow 15** — the dogfood walk + its six-part bar: composition fidelity (a real task ran through to one commit on a non-Rust project); the judgment slots genuinely exercised on a **live** run (not just by inspection); the pack composes **alone** + is pure pack data (the diff touches no `crates/*/src`); the M13 seam holds (working docs stay plain markdown, no managed methodology doctype); the `doc-code` probe is gated off (no `code-anchor`); a real unlike-jigc project (never modified — the run is on the copy).
- Build-health record: capture halts / fix-rounds / audit-findings **observationally** (qualitative — the success bar; the quantitative comparison against a named milestone stays deferred), noting whether the dogfood surfaced any portability gap the toy-repo spike did not.

**Proves:** **composition fidelity on a real, unlike-jigc project** + **portability** (non-Rust, git-only) — jigc ran (the reduced-linear slice of) its own development methodology on a fresh foreign codebase. The integration proof, last because a real foreign project is exactly where a toy-repo spike's assumptions are stressed.

### Status

Both increments complete as of 2026-06-07 — **jigc ran (the reduced-linear slice of) its own development methodology on a fresh, unlike-jigc project**, built via the [milestone-build](milestone-build) harness across two risk-first increments with **0 design halts / 0 in-increment fix rounds** (build-health trend M9=0 · M10=0 · M11=0 · M12=0 — the hardest milestone to keep at 0, held because the four forks were settled and the acceptance flow spiked + re-spiked at plan-time). The deliverable is **pure pack data — zero `crates/*/src` changes** (verified `git diff 20bcc27..HEAD`): a `methodology` pack (`packs/methodology/`) that vendors the dev surfaces (`commit` schema + the full intrinsic-knob surface, the `default-workflow` enum rewritten to `[dev-task]`) and **composes as the sole `JIGC_PACK_DIR` pack** — not the embedded dev pack. The headline ran **live on a `/tmp` copy of the TypeScript `gherrink-galey`**: `setup → start → genuine agent TS work (a failing vitest test observed before impl, the project's own `pnpm`/`biome` gate run by hand, git-only) → the four fill verbs → `finalize` landing exactly one code-only commit`; the original galey HEAD (`4147a36`) + tree are byte-identical before/after (the run was on the copy, never the original). **The honesty knife-edge held, verified by inspection, not assertion:** `scope.yaml` is judgment prose (restate + stop-and-check — not hollowed), `implement.yaml` ships test-first as prose **that names its own degradation in-step** ("nothing here enforces that the test was observed failing"), `gate.yaml` is portable (no hardcoded `cargo`), and a `methodology_honesty_artifact` test makes the both-ways FAIL-exemplar check a falsifiable regression gate. **The expected exploratory outcome stands honestly:** the encode **named** a dedicated workflow-dialect-extension milestone — five triggers (test-first-ordering gate · structural human-gate step · portable gate-command knob · bounded fix-loop + resumption · owner-artifact gate), each with the failure class it prevents — *named, not built*. The [milestone-completion audit](milestone-completion-workflow.md) (independent code-review + real-binary e2e, all 8 scenarios pass) returned **2 LOW findings, both human-triaged fix-now + remediated + re-verified**: (FIXED `10fb269`) the vendored `commit` schema carried a dead-by-construction `implements→spec` ref (no `spec` doctype ships in the sole pack) → dropped; (FIXED `41bc6b6`) the honesty-artifact test guarded `gate`/`implement` but not `scope` → a `scope`-hollowing guard added (the falsifiable check now covers all three judgment-bearing steps). **Honest scope (recorded, not a defect):** M12 adds **no new engine behaviour or workflow structure over M1** — its genuinely-new proofs are sole-foreign-pack composition + portability (non-Rust, git-only) + the graduation sort performed for real; "composition fidelity" largely re-exercises M1's machinery on a foreign pack + a foreign project. **Deferred (tracked, [decisions-pending.md](decisions-pending.md)):** the quantitative build-health comparison (no runnable home — the tally lives in the external harness); the five dialect-extension triggers (the spun-off milestone); the first-finalize `.jigc/.gitignore` housekeeping amendment (pack-agnostic, identical with the embedded pack — intended, not an M12 change). **M12 mints the second (methodology) pack M14 needs — the M14 circularity is broken (the dogfood ran the methodology pack alone).**

## Milestone 13 — doctype & surface completion (arch-doc): decomposition

Cut 2026-06-07 via the [`/milestone-plan`](../.claude/commands/milestone-plan.md) command (scope → baseline audit → detect gaps → settle → review → decompose). Four `capability-auditor` subagents established the baseline by **exercising** the real binary; four `gap-detector` subagents + a design spike + an independent `design-reviewer` reshaped the design ([architecture-documentation.md](../design/architecture-documentation.md), the new design of record; [DECISIONS.md](../DECISIONS.md) 2026-06-07). **The central planning judgment: M13 = half #1 (arch-doc) only** — half #2 (graduating the methodology's own working docs to managed doctypes) **defers** behind the dialect-extension milestone, because M12's dogfood earned **zero** methodology doctypes and the workflows that would author those docs (planning/completion) are not encoded; schematizing them now would invert the earn-from-the-driver discipline. **The human chose the repeatable-component arch-doc shape** over the minimal fixed-prose one — a faithful living-architecture doc (per-component entries each tied to the code that implements them), which pulls a bounded but **real engine surface** into M13 (not the "pure pack data" the minimal shape would have been). The independent review pinned **two blocking forks** before decompose (both baked into the design): the `doc-code` predicate selector's absent-default (would silently break shipped `spec.criteria`), and the item-leaf setter addressing contract (the existing setters target the *wrong item*). Risk-first, linear: the cross-cutting predicate change first, the item-leaf write surface in isolation next, then the CLI authoring verbs, then the pack + driver, then the mandated acceptance + doc fold-back. **Status: ✅ shipped + audited (2026-06-07) — see the completion record at the end of the decomposition.**

**Pack content rides along.** The `arch-doc` schema, the `architecture-documentation` workflow + `author-arch-doc` step, the `create-arch-doc` command-ref row, and the `check:` addition to `field-types.yaml` accrete where each increment first needs them (the schema + workflow in increment 4).

### Increment 1 — The per-field-type `check:` predicate selector (the cross-cutting engine change, retired first)

**Deliverable:** the `doc-code` predicate (`symbol-exists` vs `criterion-maps-to-test`) is chosen from the **resolved field-type's `check:`** (overridable per schema field), not by section position — proven by a repeatable-section `code-anchor` resolving as `symbol-exists` **and** the M10 flow-13 acceptance staying green (`spec.criteria/maps-to-test` still `criterion-maps-to-test`, still blocks a deleted test).

**Grouped scope:**
- Widen `PackFieldType` and the schema `Field` each by an optional `check`; `pack/config/field-types.yaml` carries `{ name: code-anchor, adjudicator: doc-code, check: symbol-exists }` ([document-type-schema.md](../design/document-type-schema.md) → Pack-declared field types).
- `target_surface.rs`: both `collect_simple` and `collect_repeatable` read `check_id` from `field.check ?? field_type.check`; **delete** the two positional constants ([validation.md](../design/validation.md) → The `doc-code` probe, [architecture-documentation.md](../design/architecture-documentation.md) → predicate selector).
- Edit `crates/cli/pack/schemas/spec.yaml` so `maps-to-test` carries explicit `check: criterion-maps-to-test`; **re-run flow 13 as a green-bar** (the regression the review flagged), plus a new test over a repeatable `symbol-exists` anchor.

**Proves:** the predicate is pack-declared and position-independent, with `spec`'s shipped behaviour preserved — the cross-cutting risk retired in isolation before arch-doc depends on it.

### Increment 2 — Item-scoped engine setters (`set_item_slot` / `set_item_field`)

**Deliverable:** engine functions set a repeatable item's slot/field by `(section_id, item_id, leaf)`, scoped to that item's byte region — proven by a **multi-item golden** where item B's leaf is set and item A's identical-keyed leaf is provably untouched. Fixtures only, not yet the CLI.

**Grouped scope:**
- `set_item_slot(schema, source, section_id, item_id, prose)` / `set_item_field(…, field_key, value)` over the existing item-scoped `locate_item_block` region, restricting the field/slot scan to that region (thread `item_id`→region into the locator — `field_value_in_lines` already takes a region bound); reuse the span-generic `splice` ([architecture-documentation.md](../design/architecture-documentation.md) → build surface 2, [write-commands.md](../design/write-commands.md)).
- The shipped `set_field`/`set_slot`/`set_field_validated` are **not** reused for item leaves (their global scan / section-only slot lookup is the wrong-item bug) — the new setters are separate.

**Proves:** the wrong-item write bug is retired in isolation; an item-leaf write targets the addressed item, not the first matching key.

### Increment 3 — CLI `add-item` verb + item-leaf addressing

**Deliverable:** `jigc doc add-item <addr>#<section> --title "…"` mints a repeatable item, and `set-slot`/`set-field` resolve a `#section/item/leaf` fragment and route to the increment-2 setters — proven end-to-end on a fixture doctype with a repeatable section (mint an item, fill its slot + field, byte-stable).

**Grouped scope:**
- `DocCommand::AddItem` + `run_add_item` (clone the `run_create`/`run_set_slot` shape: resolve active task, copy-in, call the proven engine `add_item`, map `GenerateError`→`Finding`) ([write-commands.md](../design/write-commands.md)).
- Extend `field_target`/`slot_target` (`doc.rs`) to handle `Fragment::UnitItem`/`UnitItemLeaf`, routing to the item-scoped setters.

**Proves:** repeatable items are authorable through the binary — the capability `spec.criteria` deferred, the surface arch-doc's `components` need.

### Increment 4 — The `arch-doc` doctype + the architecture-documentation workflow (the pack + the `cites` edge)

**Deliverable:** `jigc start --workflow architecture-documentation "<intent>"` mints a task; the agent creates an arch-doc, authors `overview` + per-component `description`/`implemented-by` + the doc-level `cites`, and the **n→n `cites → adr` edge walks at finalize** (passes when the cited adr exists, blocks when it dangles); the arch-doc promotes to `architecture/`. The router lists the new workflow.

**Grouped scope:**
- Pack: `schemas/arch-doc.yaml` (header `meta` with `cites → adr` 0..* + the repeatable `components` block carrying `implemented-by` `code-anchor`), `workflows/architecture-documentation.yaml` (`creates-task: true`, `allows-create: [{type: arch-doc, as: arch-doc}]`, a `when` hint), `steps/author-arch-doc.yaml`, the `create-arch-doc` row in `config/commands.yaml`, and authored `description:`/`usage:` (the M11 describe surface) ([architecture-documentation.md](../design/architecture-documentation.md), [workflow-dialect.md](../design/workflow-dialect.md)).
- Finalize promotion to the fourth `location:` (`architecture/`) rides the generic `plan_promotions` loop (zero new finalize code); the doc-level `cites` edge verified pass+block over the proven edge-index path.

**Proves:** arch-doc earns its schema from its real driver; the `cites → adr` edge; persisted promotion to a fourth `location:` — VISION's starting set complete in the pack.

### Increment 5 — `arch-doc↔code` activation + the flow-16 acceptance + doc fold-back (the headline)

**Deliverable:** the mandated acceptance ([worked-examples.md](../design/worked-examples.md) → flow 16) end-to-end — a task documents a part of jigc's own (Rust) architecture with **two components carrying two different, independently-resolving** `implemented-by` anchors; `finalize` **blocks** naming component A's specific item address when A's symbol is deleted (the `symbol-exists` selector on a repeatable anchor, the per-item disambiguation proof) and blocks on a dangling `cites`; fixing both lands one `docs(arch-doc):` commit. Plus the doc fold-back.

**Grouped scope:**
- The first **real `finalize` snapshot-spawn over a repeatable-item `doc-code` anchor** (the seam the spike left unexercised); the create→first-`add-item` `## Components` schema-ordered materialization (a named check) ([architecture-documentation.md](../design/architecture-documentation.md) → flow 16, honest caveats).
- Doc fold-back: [worked-examples.md](../design/worked-examples.md) flow 16 written; [validation.md](../design/validation.md) gains arch-doc as the third `doc-code` target doctype + the `check:` selector; [doctype-map.md](doctype-map.md) arch-doc row → shipped + `cites` edge landed; [VISION.md](../VISION.md) → Document model starting-set noted complete.

**Proves:** the richest doc↔code case activates — architecture prose validated against the code it describes, blocking on a vanished per-component symbol — M13's mandated acceptance path.

### Status

All five increments complete as of 2026-06-07 — **`arch-doc` is built, VISION's named doctype starting set (`commit · arch-doc · prd · adr · spec`) is complete, and the self-hosting fold-back's half #1 is shipped.** A task runs the `architecture-documentation` workflow, authors a persisted arch-doc (doc-level `overview` + n→n `cites → adr` header ref + a repeatable `components` section, each component a `description` slot + an `implemented-by` `symbol-exists` code-anchor), and `finalize` walks **both** new gates: the `cites → adr` edge (`ref-resolves`, pass/block) and `arch-doc↔code` (`doc-code`, the richest case — a *per-component* symbol-exists anchor), promoting to a fourth `location:` (`architecture/`). The flow-16 acceptance is genuinely adversarial: it deletes component A's symbol and asserts the block names **A's specific item address** (B stays silent — the per-item disambiguation proof), asserts the dangling-`cites` block, asserts no commit, then fixes both for exactly one `docs(arch-doc):` commit. Built via the [milestone-build](milestone-build) harness; **all five increments validated independently CLEAN with 0 in-increment fix rounds.**

**Scope honesty — the milestone grew real engine surface the plan under-sized.** The human chose the **repeatable-component** arch-doc shape (over the minimal fixed-prose option) at planning — a more faithful living-architecture doc, but one that pulled an item-authoring engine surface into M13 that the plan framed as "CLI wiring over proven setters." This surfaced as **two build halts**, each a genuine gap the harness caught and the human gated: (1) **absent-item-field generation** (`40f321d`) — `add-item` mints empty, but the increment-2 item setter only spliced *present* fields, so filling a fresh item's field had no path; resolved by mirroring the header `set_field`↔`insert_front_matter_field` two-layer path; (2) **mint-empty byte-stability** (`3afc98a`) — `set_item_slot`/`add_item` produced non-canonical bytes at the mint-empty seam (the #1 retired invariant, leaking), resolved by routing through `render_item`. Increment 2's setters had been validated *in isolation* but never against the mint-empty composition seam the `add-item` verb introduces — the lesson: **validate a new write primitive against its real authoring path, not just its happy unit shape.** A bonus: the long-deferred **`spec.criteria` authoring gap (open since M3) is retired** — repeatable items are now authorable through the binary (`add-item` + item-leaf `set-slot`/`set-field`).
