**One genuinely-new engine concept:** the per-field-type **`check:` predicate selector** — a `code-anchor`'s `doc-code` predicate is now pack-declared (`{name: code-anchor, adjudicator: doc-code, check: symbol-exists}`, field-level override), not chosen by section position. This was forced because arch-doc's *repeatable* `components` want `symbol-exists` while `spec.criteria` wants `criterion-maps-to-test` — position no longer discriminates. A pure positional flip would have silently broken `spec.criteria` (the absent-default trap the pre-decompose review pinned); the selector keeps the engine empty (predicate is pack data) and `spec.criteria` green.

The [milestone-completion audit](milestone-completion-workflow.md) (independent code-review + real-binary e2e) confirmed M13 holds end-to-end and returned **2 findings, both human-triaged fix-now + remediated + re-verified** (final gate green from clean: fmt · clippy `-D warnings` · 379 engine + all CLI integration tests): (FIXED `66b1f99`) **HIGH** — a **leading-Simple-slot `set-slot` byte-stability defect** (the #1 invariant, on arch-doc's own `overview` authoring path + the pre-existing shipped `spec.goal`): `set_slot` spliced a bare empty-slot span, yielding a non-canonical staged file; resolved by re-rendering via `render_section` (the same defect class as `3afc98a`). The audit's sharpest catch was a **masking smell** — the build had logged this defect as a deferral and *scoped its inc-5 byte-stability test to the `## Components` subtree to avoid catching it*; the fix **un-masks** that test (broadened to a whole-doc `render(parse)==staged`) and graduates the deferral. (FIXED `9f18f46`) **LOW** — `add_item` accepted an unslugable `--title` (`""`/`"###"`), minting an empty `{#}` anchor → now rejected with a routed `write.unslugable-title` finding. **Deferred (tracked):** the item-leaf write **value-adjudication parity gap** (item-leaf writes carry no value-type/heading-ceiling gate, unlike `set_field_validated`) stays pinned with a trigger; `remove-item`/`reorder` verbs and the multi-word-section-id fix stay deferred (arch-doc's single-word ids route around the latter). **M13 = half #1 only** — half #2 (methodology working-docs → managed doctypes) defers behind the dialect-extension milestone, logged with its re-trigger.

**Build-health trend: M9=0 · M10=0 · M11=0 · M12=0 · M13=2 · M14=0 design halts** (M13's two halts traced to the repeatable-shape engine surface the plan under-sized — the item-authoring composition seam unspiked; M14 returned to 0 halts / 0 in-increment fix rounds, the two-axis collision model + subsume reckoning settled at plan-time and twice-reviewed before the cut). **M14 closes the post-spine arc (M10–M14).** Owed next: the **workflow-dialect-extension** milestone (5 triggers) + its dependent **M13 half-#2** (methodology working-docs → managed doctypes), plus the standing condition-keyed deferrals — all tracked in [decisions-pending.md](decisions-pending.md).

## Milestone 10 — doc↔code validation: completion record

All five increments (1–5) complete as of 2026-06-06 — **doc↔code validation works end-to-end through the real binary**: a task that **binds** a `spec` (criterion carrying `maps-to-test`) and **creates** an `adr` (carrying `cites-code`) has the pack-provided `doc-code` subprocess probe run at `finalize` — **passing** when both anchors resolve (one commit, adr promoted) and **blocking** (exit 1, no commit, names the dangling target + route) when one doesn't. Both pack-extension **firsts** are real: `code-anchor` is the **first pack-declared field type** (`FieldType` opened to engine-native + `Pack(name, adjudicator)`; declared in `pack/config/field-types.yaml`, bound to `doc-code`; engine ships none), and `doc-code` is the **first pack-provided probe** — a tree-sitter subprocess resolving `path#symbol` against working-tree code, reached over the reshaped serializable `Probe` seam. **The determinism contract is satisfied, not waived:** the std-only subprocess invoker is proven against **four real adversarial stub processes** (well-behaved / sleeper-killed-not-leaked / crasher / garbage), the `pack-probe-integrity.{timeout,crash,malformed-output}` meta-findings are intrinsic + floor-locked, the engine stays shell-free, and `sandbox-violation` is correctly absent (deferred with OS-sandboxing under the trusted-pack model). Built via the [milestone-build](milestone-build) harness across five risk-first increments with **0 design halts / 0 in-increment fix rounds** — the subprocess-vs-in-process and pack-field-type forks were converted at **plan-time** (the win condition), so the build had no architectural fork to resolve (the only interruption was a transient API-overload mid-run, cleanly resumed from the journal). The [milestone-completion audit](milestone-completion-workflow.md) (independent code-review + real-binary e2e) confirmed the engine machinery genuinely holds and returned **3 findings, all human-triaged fix-now, all remediated + re-verified**: (FIXED `55d96c7`) **HIGH** — the `doc-code` probe was excluded from the workspace and built only by fixture tests (all selecting it via `JIGC_DOC_CODE_PROBE`), so on a real install the production path resolved a nonexistent binary → every anchor-carrying finalize a permanent floor-locked `crash` meta-finding; a `build.rs` now produces the probe alongside `jigc` and a new e2e exercises the **production default path** (no env override); (FIXED `1d21bf6`) **MEDIUM** — the tree-sitter resolver walked only top-level items, so the **dominant** Rust unit-test layout (`#[test]` in `#[cfg(test)] mod tests`) + impl methods were invisible (false-block), and the flow-13 fixture was shaped to the rare top-level layout that **masked** it → the resolver now descends into `mod`/`impl` at any nesting and the fixture is **de-masked** to the dominant layout (so the acceptance proves the real case); (FIXED `a946119`) **LOW** — the empty-stdout→`malformed-output` strictness lived only in engine code → documented in the wire contract. The two substantive findings were both the audit doing its job: a *works-in-tests-broken-on-a-real-machine* gap (HIGH) and a *masking-fixture-hides-a-real-gap* gap (MEDIUM, the M9 lesson recurring). **Residual deferred** (tracked, [decisions-pending.md](decisions-pending.md)): the fix makes the **build-tree** production path work; `cargo install jigc` won't relocate the build-script-produced probe sibling — closing that needs a `jigc setup` embed-and-extract step, deferred until `cargo install` is a supported distribution channel. Staleness, OS-level sandboxing + `sandbox-violation`, store/doc-scope validate, and `add-item` stay consciously deferred. **M10 ships the last deferred differentiator — VISION principle #6's "validate against reality" is now real.**

## Milestone 14 — multi-pack composition: decomposition

Cut 2026-06-08 via the [`/milestone-plan`](../.claude/commands/milestone-plan.md) command (scope → 3 `capability-auditor`s → 4 `gap-detector`s → settle → review → decompose) — **the last post-spine-arc milestone**. The exercised baseline confirmed single-pack is baked into the **data shape** (`PackDefaultLayer.pack_id` scalar), the **factory** (`make_pack()` → one `Box<dyn PackSource>`), the **read boundary** (`PackSource` keyed `(kind,id)`, no pack dimension), **provenance** (one `Pack:` segment), and **reconciliation** — *no N-pack scaffold exists*, so M14's mechanism is net-new (not a flag-flip). Human-settled forks: **KEEP-SUBSUME** (thinning the methodology pack is *forced out* — it breaks 7/8 `methodology_pack_*` regression tests + compose-alone, and finalize's hardcoded `COMMIT_TYPE` means a `creates-task` pack *must* vendor `commit`), so M14 is **compose + collision-adjudicate**; **precedence-override, whole-definition** collision resolution for top-level ids (doctype/knob — the M11 whole-file-shadow model, **no new merge primitive**); **pack-local body-reference resolution** as a *separate correctness rule* for the three body-reference kinds (`{{include: step:X}}`, `{{cli.X}}`, schema field-types) — each resolves against the pack that **defines** the definition, never a merged namespace (the M3-class silent-corruption class); **`.jigc/config/packs:` ordered list** (pre-cascade input, base pack lowest; pack-precedence a sub-order *within* pack-default — the team layer stays inert); scope = compose + collisions + provenance-of-winner + the single-pack **byte-identity floor**, DEFER reconciliation-over-a-composed-base + cross-pack probe/field-type *collision* + enum-union + finer precedence. Design home of record: new [multi-pack.md](../design/multi-pack.md); acceptance = [worked-examples.md](../design/worked-examples.md) → **flow 17**. **Two review passes before this cut, both baked:** the same-model `design-reviewer` caught the **command-catalog** body-reference gap (the catalogs aren't mutual supersets — a whole-file shadow would strip the loser's `{{cli.X}}`), and a human-approved cross-model `/review-codex` pass added the **field-type** body-reference axis (a dev doctype's `code-anchor` must resolve from dev's pack though methodology — the precedence winner — ships none), the in-binary cold-start floor formulation, and `--explain` provenance by **path + content-hash** (a `.jigc/config/packs:` dir's id/version is not its identity). **Two collision axes, kept independent:** *top-level* ids resolve by precedence; *body-references* resolve pack-locally. Linear, **risk-first**: the composite substrate + the byte-identity floor first (the headline regression), then the pack-local correctness axis (the M3-class trap), then the e2e acceptance + provenance completeness.

### Increment 1 — the composite pack-source + the single-pack byte-identity floor (the substrate spine + the headline regression)

**Deliverable:** an ordered **composite `PackSource`** assembled from the `.jigc/config/packs:` list (highest-precedence first) over the base pack (`JIGC_PACK_DIR`/`EmbeddedPack`, lowest), behind the **existing** `&dyn PackSource` so the ~22 call sites don't ripple. `read(kind,id)` returns the highest-precedence pack owning the id; `list(kind)` returns the union **deduped-by-id then sorted** (the existing single-pack `list()` contract). `.jigc/config/packs.yaml` is parsed at **pack-assembly time** as a pre-cascade input (not a cascade knob). The orientation `Pack:` header lists the composed pack-set (degrading to today's single segment for one pack). Top-level precedence-override falls out of composite `read` (a colliding `knobs.yaml`/`commit`/workflow id resolves to the winner's whole file).

**Grouped scope:**
- `crates/cli/src/pack.rs`: generalize `make_pack()`/`make_pack_from` to assemble the pack-set (base + listed dirs); the `.jigc/config/packs.yaml` reader; the composite `PackSource` (`read` = precedence-winner, `list` = dedup-then-sort union).
- The multi-pack `Pack:` orientation header (`orient.rs`) — one pack → byte-identical to today; N packs → highest-first segments.
- Tests (real binary + goldens): **the floor** — `Composite([base])` composes byte-identically to the single-pack path (the existing no-delta `start_compose` goldens drive through the composite; the M12 `methodology_pack_*` compose-alone tests stay green untouched); a **two-pack set loads** — both packs' non-colliding resources resolve via `list`/`read`, and a colliding top-level id (`default-workflow` knob → `dev-task`; `commit` schema) resolves to the precedence winner.

**Proves:** the composite substrate exists and the **single-pack byte-identity floor holds** (the headline regression — if the composite perturbs the one-pack path, everything downstream is poisoned), and top-level precedence-override resolves a colliding doctype/knob deterministically. Risk-first: the foundation, proven before any correctness logic rides on it. **Does not yet pack-localize body-references** — so a *loser*-pack workflow including a colliding step is still mis-resolved here (exercised + fixed in increment 2); increment 1's tests assert loading + the floor + top-level winner only, never correct cross-pack workflow composition. Builds against [multi-pack.md](../design/multi-pack.md) → The pack-set; Collision resolution; Where it sits.

### Increment 2 — pack-local body-reference resolution (the correctness axis — the M3-class trap)

**Deliverable:** **pack-of-origin scoping** so a definition's body-references resolve against the pack that defines it: a workflow's `{{include: step:X}}` against *its* pack's steps, its `{{cli.X}}` against *its* pack's `commands.yaml`, and a schema's field-type names against *its* pack's `field-types.yaml`. The `workflow-refs` / `command-ref-resolves` / field-type checks fire **per-definition against its own pack**. `--explain` names which pack won each top-level collision.

**Grouped scope:**
- Thread pack-of-origin through the composite source + `CascadeStepSource::step` / `CascadeDefs::read_workflow` (`start.rs`), `load_catalog`/`load_command_catalog`, and `pack_field_types` (`pack.rs`) — a pack-scoped read for body-references, distinct from the precedence read for top-level ids.
- `--explain` collision-winner line (the resolved winner per adjudicated top-level collision).
- Tests (real binary): dev's `single-task` composes **dev's** `step:implement` + `{{cli.create-adr}}` even though methodology (the precedence winner) ships a divergent `implement` and lacks `create-adr` (the loser-pack-workflow correctness — the M3-class corruption averted); methodology's `dev-task` composes **methodology's** test-first `implement` + `set-commit-type/scope/body`; a dev doctype (`spec`/`arch-doc`) loads with its `code-anchor` field-type resolving from **dev's** `field-types.yaml`. The scope boundary (project/team references go through the cascade) is stated, not exercised.

**Proves:** the **correctness axis** — both packs' workflows and doctypes resolve their *own* body-references regardless of which pack wins a top-level id, so co-composition never silently corrupts the loser's definitions. Risk-first second: the trickiest net-new logic (pack-of-origin tracking, no precedent), built once the substrate + floor are proven. Builds against [multi-pack.md](../design/multi-pack.md) → Pack-local body-reference resolution.

### Increment 3 — the flow-17 acceptance + provenance completeness (the integration proof)

**Deliverable:** the end-to-end **dev + methodology co-composition** acceptance ([worked-examples.md](../design/worked-examples.md) → flow 17) driven through the real binary, plus `--explain` surfacing each composed pack's **resolving directory path + blake3 content-hash**.

**Grouped scope:**
- `--explain`/provenance: per-composed-pack path + content-hash (blake3 over pack bytes), alongside the collision-winner lines from increment 2.
- e2e acceptance (throwaway repo, real binary): flow 17's walk + its six-part bar — the single-pack floor (golden); both top-level collisions (`default-workflow`→`dev-task`, `commit`) named in `--explain`; pack-local body-references (dev's `single-task` keeps `create-adr`; methodology's `dev-task` keeps its test-first `implement`); both packs' distinct surfaces co-composing **and dev's doctypes loading + validating** (create a dev `arch-doc`/`adr`, set its `code-anchor`, prove the field-type resolved from dev); provenance naming every pack by path + hash; byte-identical recompose under composition.

**Proves:** all six flow-17 assertions hold against the built binary — the architectural invariant *engine-neutral, packs supply content* **generalizes to scale** (two real packs co-compose deterministically, collisions resolved on their two axes, the engine still knowing neither). The integration proof, last because the real two-pack composition is exactly where the prior increments' assumptions are stressed. Builds against [worked-examples.md](../design/worked-examples.md) → flow 17; [multi-pack.md](../design/multi-pack.md) → Provenance.

### Status

All three increments (1–3) complete as of 2026-06-08 — **two real domain packs (the embedded dev pack + the M12 methodology pack) co-compose in one project, the engine knowing neither.** The deliverable: a `.jigc/config/packs.yaml`-selected **composite `PackSource`** (listed dirs over the `JIGC_PACK_DIR`/embedded base, base lowest) behind the **zero-arg `make_pack()`** so the ~23 call sites don't ripple; **top-level precedence-override** (a colliding `default-workflow` knob / `commit` doctype resolves to the precedence winner's whole definition — falls out of composite `read`); **pack-local body-reference resolution** on all three reference kinds via a new defaulted `PackSource::origin_pack` seam (a workflow's `{{include: step:X}}` + `{{cli.X}}` resolve in the workflow's own pack; a schema's field-types in the schema's own pack) — the M3-class silent-corruption averted; and **multi-pack provenance** (the `Pack:` header names every composed pack highest-first, `--explain` adds each pack's resolving **path + blake3 content-hash** + the per-collision winner). The **single-pack byte-identity floor** holds (composite-of-`[base]` byte-for-byte equals the single-pack path; all four M12 `methodology_pack_*` compose-alone regressions green untouched). Built via the [milestone-build](milestone-build) harness across three risk-first increments with **0 design halts / 0 in-increment fix rounds** (the two-axis collision model + the subsume reckoning were settled at plan-time, and the design survived **two** independent review passes — same-model + cross-model `/review-codex` — whose catches, the command-catalog and field-type body-reference axes, were baked *before* the cut). The [milestone-completion audit](milestone-completion-workflow.md) (independent code-review + real-binary e2e, all six flow-17 assertions pass) returned **2 findings — one coupled defect (a CRITICAL + the HIGH test masking it), human-triaged fix-now + remediated + re-verified** (`f651b14`): the pack-local rule was applied to the **fresh-compose** front doors but **not the resume/re-entry spine** `compose_task_workflow` (shared by `jigc start --task` and `workflow W --task`), so resuming a *loser*-pack workflow silently swapped in the precedence-winner pack's step bodies + catalog (mint ≠ resume — the exact corruption class the milestone exists to prevent) — and flow-17 assertion 6a **masked** it by comparing resume-vs-resume (never resume-vs-fresh) with a comment misnaming the resumed workflow; the fix mirrors the proven fresh-compose origin-scoping (`origin_pack` → `load_catalog(origin)` + `scope_to_workflow`) onto the resume spine and **de-masks 6a** to compare resume-vs-fresh on a loser-pack workflow (the M9/M13 masking-fixture lesson recurring — the audit doing its job). **Deferred (tracked, [decisions-pending.md](decisions-pending.md)):** reconciliation/upgrade over a *composed* base (subsumes the fired `override-default` content-target check); cross-pack probe/field-type *collision* (methodology ships neither); closed-enum **union** + finer pack-precedence; the `--explain` collision-winner's highest-owns-it assumption (correct for the two-pack bound, latent for N>2). The pre-existing item-leaf set-field value-laxity the e2e surfaced is the already-tracked item-leaf adjudication-parity gap, not an M14 defect. **M14 closes the post-spine arc (M10–M14) — multi-pack composition proves the engine/pack boundary generalizes at scale.**

## Milestone 15 — workflow-dialect extension (the checkpoint step kind): decomposition

Cut 2026-06-09 via the [milestone-planning workflow](milestone-planning-workflow.md) (scope → baseline audit → four `gap-detector`s → settle → independent review → decompose). The slice (human-confirmed): **encode the increment-workflow only** — the harder workflow that forces the sharpest primitives (planning + completion deferred). The baseline audit + four gap-detectors converged and an independent design review (2 blocking + 4 advisory, all baked back) confirmed: **M15's entire engine delta is one new primitive — the `checkpoint` step kind** ([DECISIONS.md](../DECISIONS.md) 2026-06-09); everything else is pack authoring + prose + orchestration that already exists. The `checkpoint` is the third step kind after `fan-out`/`join`: it **always composes** and emits a reserved sixth emit class `Checkpoint: <reason>` the agent/orchestrator **honors by halting** — *halting, not branching* ([workflow-dialect.md](../design/workflow-dialect.md#the-checkpoint-step-kind-m15); [VISION.md](../VISION.md) → Non-goals), convention-honored, riding the existing stateless restart-from-scratch resumption (no runtime, no progress cursor). It unifies triggers #2 (human-gate) + the structural half of #4 (the fix-loop's halt-pending-fix); the loop's bound + re-run stay orchestration-level. #1's A==A lint, #3's gate-command knob, #5's owner-artifact gate are **deferred** ([self-hosting.md](../design/self-hosting.md) → What M15 built). Risk-first, linear: the novel engine primitive in isolation first, the pack encode + acceptance on top. **Status: shipped 2026-06-09 — completion record below.**

### Increment 1 — the `checkpoint` step kind (the novel engine surface)

**Deliverable:** a step carrying `checkpoint:\n  reason: <slug>` front-matter composes — through the real binary — to a `Checkpoint: <slug>` directive emitted **at the head of the step's text** (before the body prose), followed by the body; composed output **differs** with vs. without the marker; the marker is honored on the **live fill-aware compose path**. Exercised via fixtures (a test pack with a checkpoint step), not yet the methodology pack.

**Grouped scope:**
- `StepKind::Checkpoint { reason }` variant + `StepFrontMatter` gains a `checkpoint:` field; `parse_step_kind` rewritten from the closed `(fan-out, join)` 2-tuple to a **three-marker mutual-exclusivity** check (a step is exactly one kind; both/all markers present → `step-kind-malformed`) ([workflow-dialect.md](../design/workflow-dialect.md#the-checkpoint-step-kind-m15), [parsing.md](parsing.md) → Front-matter).
- The emit path: `compose_with_store` gains the `Checkpoint:` arm — the directive is **prepended** to the step's emitted text (unlike `Spawn:`'s append), the bare `reason` slug as the sixth emit class ([workflow-dialect.md](../design/workflow-dialect.md#emitted-format)).
- `checkpoint-marker-not-shadowed` conformance check: fires against the **de-included step body before directive emission** (the `step.body` surface `find_run_shadow`/`find_spawn_shadow` read — never the post-emit text, or the step's own directive self-trips), added at **both** `workflow_refs_with_deltas` **and** `workflow_refs_with_fills` (the fill variant is the live path). The new `workflow-refs.checkpoint-marker-not-shadowed` code synced across the three check-inventory lists (`CHECK_INVENTORY` ↔ `knobs.yaml` ↔ `INTRINSIC_CHECK_KEYS`, floored blocking) ([validation.md](../design/validation.md) → Severity inventory).
- The forward-review obligations as Red steps: composed output **differs** with vs. without the marker (the M8 face-#5 parsed-but-ignored guard); **resume-vs-fresh** byte-identical with the same halt verdict (no progress state introduced — the M14 A==A-oracle mask discipline, never resume-vs-resume).

**Proves:** the one novel engine primitive — a structural halt directive that composes deterministically, honors the no-runtime invariant (halts, never branches), and is shadow-protected — retired in isolation before the pack encode rides on it. The #1 (and only) engine risk.

### Increment 2 — the methodology `increment` workflow encode + the acceptance (flow 18)

**Deliverable:** the full M15 proof — `jigc start --workflow increment "<intent>"` composes the methodology pack's `increment` workflow into the multi-phase spine (plan → execute → validate → fix) with the three halts emitted as `Checkpoint:` directives, end-to-end through the real binary = [worked-examples.md](../design/worked-examples.md) flow 18.

**Grouped scope:**
- Methodology pack: a new `increment` workflow (`creates-task: false`, a `when` hint) + its **task-ref-free** steps — `plan`/`execute`/`validate` (Reason prose) and `plan-gate`/`execute-gate`/`fix-gate` (checkpoint steps with `reason` slugs `new-fork-at-plan`/`blocked-task`/`fix-rounds-exhausted`) ([workflow-dialect.md](../design/workflow-dialect.md#the-checkpoint-step-kind-m15), [increment-workflow.md](increment-workflow.md)).
- The pack-config edge: `increment` is **not** added to the `default-workflow` `of:` enum (not router-selectable — the M8 catalog-leak class); the methodology `defaults.yaml`/`knobs.yaml` `default-workflow` surface stays untouched and the `methodology_honesty_artifact` tripwire stays green (the existing prose-degraded `dev-task` steps are not modified) ([self-hosting.md](../design/self-hosting.md) → What M15 built).
- e2e acceptance (throwaway repo, real binary): flow 18's walk + its seven-part bar — the `Checkpoint:` directives emitted from the real binary's bytes; composed output differs with vs. without each marker (live fill path); `checkpoint-marker-not-shadowed` fires; the outer loop is task-ref-free (a `task.*` ref blocks); halting-not-branching + resume-vs-fresh determinism; the structural deliverable (halts-as-directives, the advance over M12); the honest orchestration-level bound observed, not over-claimed ([worked-examples.md](../design/worked-examples.md) → flow 18).

**Proves:** M15's headline — jigc composes its *own* multi-phase, human-gated, fix-looping development workflow (the increment-workflow), the deepest self-hosting proof beyond M12's reduced-linear slice, with the halts as machine-recognizable structure and the multi-agent orchestration honestly bounded out. Builds against [worked-examples.md](../design/worked-examples.md) → flow 18; [self-hosting.md](../design/self-hosting.md).

### Status

Both increments (1–2) complete as of 2026-06-09 — **jigc composes its own harder development workflow.** The deliverable: the **`checkpoint` step kind** (the third after `fan-out`/`join`), built halt-only within the no-runtime invariant — `parse_step_kind` rewritten to a three-marker mutual-exclusivity check, the sixth emit class `Checkpoint: <reason>` **prepended** at the step head (the inverse of `Spawn:`'s append), `checkpoint-marker-not-shadowed` wired at **both** `workflow_refs` gate sites against the pre-emit de-included body (a real checkpoint step never self-trips), the new intrinsic key floored blocking across `INTRINSIC_CHECK_KEYS` + both packs' `knobs.yaml`; and the methodology pack's **`increment` workflow** + its six task-ref-free steps (plan/execute/validate prose + plan-gate/execute-gate/fix-gate checkpoints), composing through the real binary into the plan→execute→validate→fix spine with three machine-recognizable halt directives. Tasks were cut per-increment via the [increment workflow](increment-workflow.md); the build ran **0 design halts / 0 in-increment fix rounds** (the no-runtime/no-conditional reckoning + the convention-honored checkpoint were settled at plan-time, the design survived an independent adversarial review whose two blocking catches — directive-prepend-not-append and the pre-emit shadow-check surface — were baked in *before* the cut). The [milestone-completion audit](milestone-completion-workflow.md) (independent code-review + real-binary e2e, all seven flow-18 assertions pass) returned **1 low finding — documentation-only drift, auto-fixed + re-verified** (`13bc6d6`): `design/validation.md`'s severity inventory (the declared single source of truth) still read 25/16 while the shipped+tested code added the 26th/17th intrinsic check — reconciled to 26/17 and the reconciliation test's brittle line-cite de-fragilized. The **honest bound holds**: jigc composes the **single-agent composable spine** with the halts as structure; the multi-agent orchestration (per-phase agents, the independent read-only validator, the bounded ≤3-round fix re-run) stays **orchestration-level** (the build harness), unchanged by M15 and not over-claimed — the spawn-class e2e confirmed the `increment` workflow ships no fan-out, so there is no hollow-spawn surface. **Deferred (tracked, [decisions-pending.md](decisions-pending.md)):** #1's test-first **A==A-oracle lint** (milestone-keyed — fires once a methodology doctype carries a test-source `code-anchor`; test-first *ordering* stays honest agent-policed prose); #3's **portable gate-command knob** (prose is more correct on a foreign project; surfacing a knob into composed output needs a `cascade`/`knob` data-value root that doesn't exist); #5's **owner-artifact gate** (deferred with the completion-workflow encode). **M16 does not fold** — encoding the increment-workflow earns zero managed doctype (it only reads working docs at fixed paths). M15 is **prereq #1 of the adoption arc** — the dialect surface the dogfood (M17) needs, built ahead on the human's direction.

## Milestone 16 — close the self-hosting loop (planning/completion encode + 4 doctypes): decomposition

Cut 2026-06-09 via the [milestone-planning workflow](milestone-planning-workflow.md) (scope → baseline audit ×4 → four `gap-detector`s → settle → independent review + cross-model `/review-codex` → decompose). The baseline + gap pass **overturned the roadmap's "earn-from-driver, mirroring M13" framing as too light** — the M13 `arch-doc` analogy breaks three ways (M13's workflow was `creates-task: true`; create-only; one repeatable level, ref in the header). Design-of-record: [methodology-docs.md](../design/methodology-docs.md) ([DECISIONS.md](../DECISIONS.md) 2026-06-09 → M16 planning). The **authoring crux** (a `creates-task: false` workflow can't author a managed doctype) **dissolved** via the `sub-task` precedent — planning/completion are **`creates-task: true, selectable: false`** (mint a task, author through the *proven* create-gate + finalize-promote spine, stay off the router; spiked against the binary). **Full-4** doctypes in the methodology pack, proven on **fresh instances** (jigc's own files migrate at v1): three **running singletons** (`roadmap`/`deferral-ledger`/`decisions-log` — a net-new `singleton: true` schema flag fixes the slug, idempotent-create maintains) + the per-milestone create-fresh `completion-record`. Two adversarial reviews (same-model: 3 blocking + 3 important; cross-model: 1 blocking + 4 more) were **all baked back before the cut** — notably the `#5` gate's owned-location promotion (was vacuous), the per-finding ref **downgraded to a `string`** (the methodology pack composes alone, so a managed ref to dev-pack `adr`/`spec` is unresolvable → **B4 shrinks to conformance-only**, the `index.rs` ref-edge lift deferred), and the decisions-log authored by **both** halves. Risk-first, linear: the three shared/isolated engine deltas first (each proven on fixtures, each carrying its *fire-on-malformed* red obligation against the "vacuously-green managed-doc gate" failure class), then the two earn-from-driver pack halves on top. **Status: planned, not yet built.**

### Increment 1 — repeatable-section conformance (the shared validation substrate)

**Deliverable:** `schema_conformance` adjudicates **repeatable** sections — per item, `required-slot-present` / `required-field-present` / `field-value-conformant` over the block's leaves (the `id-from` heading field exempted), findings addressed at `#section/item/leaf`. Proven on a fixture repeatable doctype, not yet any methodology pack content.

**Grouped scope:**
- Lift the `SectionBody::Repeatable` skip at `validate.rs` (the unconditional `continue` past non-`Simple` bodies) to iterate items and run the three synthetic checks over each block leaf, exempting the `id-from` source field (mirror the `parse.rs` heading-field exclusion) ([validation.md](../design/validation.md) → schema-conformance scope, [document-type-schema.md](../design/document-type-schema.md) → the MVP limit being lifted; [methodology-docs.md](../design/methodology-docs.md) → engine work 1).
- **Ref-edges are OUT** (Codex finding 3): no M16 doctype carries a per-entry managed ref, so `index.rs`'s repeatable-edge skip + the per-item edge-identity question stay deferred — conformance-only.
- Red obligation: a hand-malformed repeatable entry (empty required slot; malformed required field value) **produces a blocking finding** — the loop *fires*, not merely passes good input (the vacuously-green guard).

**Proves:** the differentiator's substrate — jigc validates that every entry of a repeatable-bodied managed doc is well-formed — retired in isolation before the four repeatable-heavy doctypes ride on it. Updates [validation.md](../design/validation.md) + [document-type-schema.md](../design/document-type-schema.md).

### Increment 1b — multi-slot-per-repeatable-item (inserted at build-time; a deferral lifted by human choice)

**Deliverable:** a repeatable item template with **>1 slot leaf** persists every slot — each rendered under a `#### <Leaf-Title>` sub-heading (schema order); a **single**-slot item keeps the bare-prose form byte-identical (backward-compat). The roadmap milestone entry's `proves` + `decomposition` slots both round-trip. Shipped `dcf85a3`.

**Grouped scope:**
- `ParsedItem.slots: Vec<(leaf, Span)>` + `ItemTemplate.slot_ids`; `parse_items` splits a multi-slot item body by its `####` sub-headings (`#####`+ stays opaque), single-slot keeps the whole-body span ([parsing.md](parsing.md)).
- `ItemContent.slots` + `render_item` (the `####` form for multi-slot, bare for single); `add_item` mints the **full ordered empty skeleton** (the M13 cold-start seam); `set_item_slot` splices the named leaf, siblings untouched; the `{{store.…}}` compose read-view + the inc-1 per-leaf conformance follow.
- Red-first: the two-slot round-trip (`render(parse(x)) == x`, both slots preserved); the cold-start fill-one-leaf-at-a-time byte-stability; **backward-compat byte-identity** (the entire existing suite green with zero snapshot churn on single-slot items — the hard constraint); per-leaf conformance fires.

**Proves:** the deferred "Multi-slot sections + their rendering" limit ([decisions-pending.md](decisions-pending.md)) lifted for the **repeatable-item** case (Simple-section case stays deferred — no driver), so the roadmap entry's genuine two-part structure is representable rather than collapsed. Surfaced at inc-4 T4 by the executor's cold-start spike — the [milestone-completion](milestone-completion-workflow.md) human gate firing on a real fork ([DECISIONS.md](../DECISIONS.md) → M16 build halt).

### Increment 2 — `singleton` doctypes + idempotent create (the running-doc substrate)

**Deliverable:** a `singleton: true` schema doctype mints at a **fixed slug = the type id** (no `id-from`), and `create` on an **already-committed** singleton slug **copies-in** the committed doc instead of clobbering it; proven on a fixture singleton doctype — cold-create round-trips byte-stable, warm re-create copies-in + `add-item` appends + re-promotes byte-stable, the `single-task` ADR/superseding flow regression-passes unchanged, and a baseline-recorded-then-OOB-drifted warm edit conflict-blocks at finalize-preflight.

**Grouped scope:**
- The `singleton: true` schema flag → fixed-slug minting (slug = type id; the running docs need a *constant* slug for idempotent-create to target the same committed file — review finding B-2) ([methodology-docs.md](../design/methodology-docs.md) → the doctype table; [document-type-schema.md](../design/document-type-schema.md)).
- Idempotent `create`: on a committed-store collision for a `singleton` doctype, route through the `read_or_copy_in`/`copy_in` path (was the clobber-on-blank landmine, `state.rs`) instead of minting blank; **gate the committed-copy-in to `singleton` doctypes** so non-singleton `create` keeps mint-or-reject (the shared-primitive regression — review finding I-2) ([methodology-docs.md](../design/methodology-docs.md) → engine work 2).
- Each running doctype gets its **own** `location:` subdir (never a shared root — review finding B-1); copy-in reads the committed doc directly, drift caught at finalize-preflight (DRIFTED+TOUCHED conflict-block), not at copy-in (review finding I-1).
- Red obligation + cold/warm spike (M13 cold-start discipline): cold round-trips; warm preserves prior content + re-promotes byte-stable (`first_touch_canonicalize` a no-op on already-canonical); the warm-drift spike **records the baseline first** (an unrecorded drift adopts vacuously); the `single-task` ADR flow regression holds.

**Proves:** the running-singleton maintenance substrate — create-or-update as a deterministic state-dependent write command, the clobber landmine gone — retired before the planning/completion workflows maintain real docs on it. The #1 engine risk (a shared-primitive change).

### Increment 3 — the #5 owner-artifact presence gate (the completion-half engine primitive)

**Deliverable:** a finalize-time intrinsic check that reads the `owner-artifact` **owned-location path** field on a `completion-record` and asserts the artifact is **durably staged/committed under the owned home**; proven on a fixture — it **blocks** when the path is absent, unsafe (absolute / `..` / symlink-escape / out-of-repo), or untracked, and **passes** when the artifact is staged under `completions/artifacts/<milestone>/`.

**Grouped scope:**
- The check: validate `owner-artifact` is a repo-relative path under the owned artifact home, finalize **promotes/stages it in the same transaction** as the `completion-record` (the transactional-at-finalize invariant), then assert durable presence — reading the field string + file presence, **never the artifact's bytes** (presence, not content; the determinism boundary) ([methodology-docs.md](../design/methodology-docs.md) → engine work 3 + the honesty bound).
- Severity-list sync: the new intrinsic key (floored blocking) added to `INTRINSIC_CHECK_KEYS` (17→18), **both** packs' `knobs.yaml` (26→27), `CHECK_INVENTORY` (a task-validate/finalize check, unlike the compose-time marker checks), and the [validation.md](../design/validation.md) Severity inventory — the M15 three-list-sync discipline + the four-list reconciliation.
- Red obligation: the gate **fires** (blocks) on absent/unsafe/untracked and **passes** on a durably-staged owned-location artifact — not a vacuous always-pass (Codex blocking-1). The honest bound recorded: presence-at-owned-path, **not** genuineness (the M8 orchestrator-responsibility analogue).

**Proves:** the third determinism-bucket primitive — an owner-assigned-artifact presence gate that mechanizes the *presence* without crossing into *judging* the artifact — retired before the completion workflow rides it. Builds against [methodology-docs.md](../design/methodology-docs.md), [milestone-completion-workflow.md](milestone-completion-workflow.md).

### Increment 4 — planning-half: the planning workflow + roadmap / deferral-ledger / decisions-log doctypes (flow 19)

**Deliverable:** `jigc start --workflow planning "<milestone>"` mints a task (off-router), composes the planning spine with the **Settle checkpoint**, the agent authors a `roadmap` milestone entry (increments-as-prose in the `decomposition` slot) + `deferral-ledger` entries + a `decisions-log` entry via idempotent-create + `add-item`/`set-slot`, and `finalize` promotes the three running singletons — proven end-to-end across **two** milestone runs (cold-create then warm-append) = [worked-examples.md](../design/worked-examples.md) flow 19.

**Grouped scope:**
- Methodology pack schemas: `roadmap` (singleton, one repeatable `milestones` section — entry = title + `proves` slot + `decomposition` slot, one level), `deferral-ledger` (singleton, repeatable `entries` — title + `kind` enum + `trigger` string + `date` + `body` slot), `decisions-log` (singleton, repeatable `entries` — title + `date` + `why` slot); each its own `location:` subdir, single-word section ids ([methodology-docs.md](../design/methodology-docs.md) → the four doctypes).
- The `planning` workflow (`creates-task: true`, `selectable: false`, `allows-create:` the three doctypes) + its steps: the phase walk (scope / detect-gaps / settle / review / decompose as Reason prose) with the **Settle checkpoint** (`reason: settle`); steps that author the docs via idempotent-create + item authoring. **Off-router:** absent from the methodology `default-workflow.of` enum, no `when` (the M8 catalog-leak class) — the `methodology_increment_off_router` test pattern extended.
- e2e acceptance (throwaway repo, real binary): flow 19's two-run walk — cold-create the singletons (run 1), warm-append on a second milestone (run 2, idempotent-create copies-in + re-promotes byte-stable); a half-authored roadmap entry **blocks** at validate (increment 1 fires); `planning` absent from the router catalog; the build-honesty watch holds (Settle/gap-detect stay agent-judgment prose, never a gap-count lint — the `methodology_honesty_artifact` bar).

**Proves:** the planning half of the loop — jigc composes its own milestone-planning spine and authors-and-maintains three of its running working-docs as managed doctypes, earn-from-driver. Builds against [methodology-docs.md](../design/methodology-docs.md), [milestone-planning-workflow.md](milestone-planning-workflow.md), [worked-examples.md](../design/worked-examples.md) → flow 19.

### Increment 5 — completion-half: the completion workflow + completion-record + the #5 gate wired (flow 20)

**Deliverable:** the full M16 close — `jigc start --workflow completion "<milestone>"` mints a task (off-router), composes the completion spine (audit → triage → fix → re-verify, the halts as checkpoints), create-fresh authors the per-milestone `completion-record` (verdict + findings + the `owner-artifact` owned-location path) + appends `decisions-log` triage entries, and `finalize` promotes the owner-artifact in-transaction and runs the **#5 presence gate** = [worked-examples.md](../design/worked-examples.md) flow 20.

**Grouped scope:**
- Methodology pack schema: `completion-record` (per-milestone, `id-from: title`, `location: completions/`; `meta` header = `verdict` enum + `owner-artifact` owned-location path field; repeatable `findings` — title + `severity` + `disposition` enum + `evidence` **string**, not a managed ref) ([methodology-docs.md](../design/methodology-docs.md) → the four doctypes + Relations).
- The `completion` workflow (`creates-task: true`, `selectable: false`, `allows-create: [completion-record]` + decisions-log append) + its steps: the audit/triage/fix/re-verify phase walk with checkpoints (the human-gated halts), the completion-record authoring, the decisions-log append, and the **#5 owner-artifact gate** at finalize (increment 3). **Off-router** (same discipline as increment 4).
- e2e acceptance (throwaway repo, real binary): flow 20's walk — the completion spine composes with the halts as `Checkpoint:` directives; the #5 gate **blocks** when the owner-artifact is absent/unsafe/untracked and **passes** when durably staged under the owned home (increment 3 fires, not vacuous); decisions-log appended by this half too (the both-halves driver); the single-agent-spine bound observed — the audit verdict stays orchestration-level, not over-claimed.

**Proves:** M16's headline — jigc composes its full milestone-completion workflow (with the genuine-audit owner-artifact gated for presence) and manages its own completion working-docs, closing the self-hosting *machinery* loop (fresh instances; jigc's own historical files migrate at v1). Builds against [methodology-docs.md](../design/methodology-docs.md), [milestone-completion-workflow.md](milestone-completion-workflow.md), [worked-examples.md](../design/worked-examples.md) → flow 20.

### Status

All increments (1, **1b**, 2–5) complete as of 2026-06-09 — **the self-hosting *machinery* loop is closed: jigc composes its own planning + completion workflows and manages four of its methodology's working-docs as managed doctypes** (proven on fresh instances; jigc's own historical files migrate at v1). The deliverable: the **authoring spine** = `creates-task: true, selectable: false` (the `sub-task` precedent — mint a task, author via the proven create-gate + finalize-promote, off-router); four doctypes (`roadmap`/`deferral-ledger`/`decisions-log` running singletons via a net-new `singleton: true` fixed-slug flag + the per-milestone `completion-record`); the engine deltas — **repeatable-section conformance** (conformance-only, the validate.rs skip lifted), **idempotent-create** (copy-in on a committed singleton → create-or-update + the clobber fix, gated to singleton doctypes), **multi-slot-per-repeatable-item** (the `#### <Leaf-Title>` sub-label form, a deferral lifted mid-build), and the **`owner-artifact.present` #5 gate** (a finalize-time presence check on a safe owned-location durably-staged path — presence never content; the four-list severity sync → 27/18); the **`planning`/`completion` workflows** + their phase-walk steps (Settle/audit/triage halts as `Checkpoint:` directives) authoring the doctypes; and flows **19** (planning two-run cold/warm) + **20** (completion encode, the #5 gate block/pass) e2e through the real binary. The build ran via the [milestone-build harness](milestone-build-workflow.md) — **1 design halt** (inc-4 T4 surfaced the two-slot/one-slot-per-item contradiction; the human chose to lift the deferred multi-slot limit, built as inserted Increment 1b, then T4 resumed) and **0 in-increment fix rounds**. The [milestone-completion audit](milestone-completion-workflow.md) (independent code-review + real-binary e2e: 11/11 e2e scenarios pass, both flows reproduced) returned **3 findings, all verified-real + auto-fixed + re-verified**: **1 HIGH** (`ddd8eb5`) — multi-slot slot prose containing a `#### ` line shadowed the slot delimiter (silent round-trip corruption) → a `conformance.item-slot-delimiter-shadowed` block (the marker-shadow discipline), single-slot byte-identity held; **2 LOW** — item-level `set: on-create` date never materialized (`a8056e4`, surfacing that doc-level on-create is *also* inert — pre-existing, tracked in [decisions-pending.md](decisions-pending.md)), and a gitignored owner-artifact wrongly passing the #5 gate (`f3f9855`, the `--exclude-standard` gap). The **honest bound holds**: jigc composes the single-agent spine; planning's fanned recon/review and completion's parallel audits + verdict stay orchestration-level (the build harness), not over-claimed. **The dialect-extension trigger ledger is fully resolved** (M15 checkpoint #2/#4-structural; M16 #5 presence-gate; #1 A==A lint + #3 gate-command knob stay deferred with named triggers — [self-hosting.md](../design/self-hosting.md)). **M16 is adoption-arc prereq #2** — M17 (dogfood + measurement on real projects) is now unblocked.

## Milestone 17 — dogfood + measurement: decomposition

The terminus milestone splits structurally ([measurement.md](../design/measurement.md); [DECISIONS.md](../DECISIONS.md) 2026-06-12): **Increments 1–5 are harness-fittable TDD work** (the measurement substrate + the six pre-fix run-breakers + the recording surface) and build via the [milestone-build harness](milestone-build-workflow.md); **the three dogfood runs are NOT increments** — they are genuine live-agent/human sessions recorded as owner-artifacts (the M12 Half-B class, [self-hosting.md](../design/self-hosting.md) → the two-half pattern), run from the main session per the protocol phases below, after the increments land. Forcing a run into a TDD increment is the hollow-dogfood trap. Need-driven fix-what-hurt increments insert *between* runs (binary pinning: fixes never land mid-measured-run). Ordering is risk-first: the engine-semantics changes (re-pin, file-state) come before the pack/apparatus work that depends on their behavior; the recording surface comes last of the increments because it consumes everything before it.

## Increment 1 — Emission + exit-code soundness (the tally's discrimination substrate)

**Deliverable:** a harness-side tally can discriminate every jigc invocation outcome: successful `task finalize` emits the findings envelope (advisories included — absorb evidence no longer swallowed), validation-blocked exits with **exit 3** (operational error stays 1; the operational-error path honors `--format json`), in both renderers.

**Grouped scope:**
- Finalize-success findings emission, symmetric with `validate` ([measurement.md](../design/measurement.md) → The capture substrate, item 2; the silent-success gap verified at planning).
- Exit-code split: 3 = validation-blocked (≥1 blocking finding), 1 = operational error; the error path emits the JSON envelope under `--format json` ([measurement.md](../design/measurement.md) → capture substrate item 2, scope pinned at review M3).
- Renderer/e2e proof: blocked vs. landed vs. operational-error finalize distinguishable by exit code + output alone — the drift-caught counting contract.

**Proves:** drift-caught and the absorb term are observable from invocation output — the zero-binary-change capture claim's two verified holes closed.

## Increment 2 — File-state soundness (one edit, one absorb; artifacts don't poison)

**Deliverable:** an absorbed OOB edit fires its `reconciliation.absorb` exactly once across subsequent tasks (the durable baseline advances at landed finalize; `validate`/`start`/reads stay pure readers), and a promoted owner-artifact no longer false-blocks later finalizes.

**Grouped scope:**
- Absorb baseline-advance persisted by landed finalize only ([reconciliation.md](../design/reconciliation.md) → the persistence amendment; the verified re-fire defect).
- The false-rename fix: *missing means absent from disk, not absent from the walk* — disk-presence check before classifying a baselined path missing ([reconciliation.md](../design/reconciliation.md) → the rename amendment; the spike-found permanent-block defect, reproduced in isolation at planning).
- Red obligations: the re-fire case (two tasks after one OOB edit → one emission) and the post-promotion finalize (artifact promoted → next finalize lands clean; genuinely deleted artifact still blocks).

**Proves:** the adapter-adherence absorb channel is sound (no over-count) and repeated per-run record promotion is possible at all.

## Increment 3 — Finalize re-pin + owned-location requiredness (the serial-task + omission gates)

**Deliverable:** a task whose base moved on disjoint history finalizes (auto-re-pin per the pinned overlap rule); a completion-class doc that omits its declared `owned-location` field blocks at conformance.

**Grouped scope:**
- `finalize.base-mismatch` re-pin: re-pin iff *(paths changed between recorded base and HEAD)* ∩ *(dirty working-tree paths ∪ promote destinations)* = ∅, then re-run preflight against the new base; overlap keeps the block with a conflict route naming the overlapping paths ([finalize.md](../design/finalize.md) → the phase-1 amendment).
- Owned-location requiredness: a **declared** owned-location field joins `required-field-present`; the #5 gate unchanged (conformance owns absence, the gate owns the named file); the `validate.rs` exemption comment flips ([methodology-docs.md](../design/methodology-docs.md) → engine work item 3 amendment).
- Red obligations: disjoint-history finalize lands; overlapping-history finalize blocks with the route; a fresh completion-record minted without `owner-artifact:` blocks at finalize.

**Proves:** the completion workflow's own designed shape (mint at audit-start, finalize after fix commits) works, and "each run recorded as an owner-artifact" is no longer silently voidable.

## Increment 4 — Pack + doc pre-fixes (the composed surface tells the truth)

**Deliverable:** the composed planning workflow carries its finalize tail; composed authoring commands carry the minted task id; QUICKSTART documents the router reality.

**Grouped scope:**
- `packs/methodology/workflows/planning.yaml` gains the finalize step include (+ commit-doc authoring guidance) — the stranded-docs gap.
- Composed write-command task-disambiguation: the authoring steps' literal command lines carry the minted task id via compose-time placeholder substitution (the finalize tail already renders it — extend the same mechanism; prove against the >1-active-task case).
- QUICKSTART.md: the router-menu reality replaces the stale one-call-mint claim (+ the install section names the probe sibling) — case-3's verbatim fixture.

**Proves:** an agent following composed prose verbatim lands the planning loop and never hits the more-than-one-active-task wall the workflows themselves create.

## Increment 5 — The recording surface (dogfood-record + record-dogfood + the hook apparatus)

**Deliverable:** `jigc start --workflow record-dogfood "<run>"` composes off-router, authors a `dogfood-record` (the post-review field set: organic facts · `seeded-*` instrument checks · verdict · owner-artifact) through the binary, and finalize promotes record + artifact; the versioned hook artifact + tally script land at `implementation/dogfood/`; flows 21/22 authored into [worked-examples.md](../design/worked-examples.md).

**Grouped scope:**
- `packs/methodology/schemas/dogfood-record.yaml` (`location: dogfood/`, the planning-spike YAML as seed, post-review fields per [measurement.md](../design/measurement.md) → The dogfood-record doctype) + `workflows/record-dogfood.yaml` + authoring step + command-ref.
- The hook set (PostToolUse Bash / Write|Edit) + append-only log format + the tally script (logical-mutation grouping, dedup rule, exit-3 keying) at `implementation/dogfood/` — versioned measurement apparatus, conscious placement.
- Flow-21-shaped e2e through the real binary on a throwaway repo: seeded OOB edit absorbed once (exercises inc 1+2), seeded bad finalize exits 3, record blocks on a missing fact field / omitted owner-artifact (exercises inc 3), promotes on complete; off-router + honesty guards extended (`record-dogfood` off the catalog; the judgment slot stays prose — no scoring lint).
- Flows 21 + 22 authored into worked-examples.md (doc fold-back, separate commit — the M16 T5 pattern).

**Proves:** the full measured-run loop is drivable end-to-end through the real binary before any real dogfood spends a session on it.

## The dogfood runs — protocol phases, not increments (main-session / human work)

Run per [measurement.md](../design/measurement.md) after increments 1–5 land, each on a pinned binary (sha in the record), each producing a `dogfood-record` + the raw-capture owner-artifact exported to `completions/artifacts/M17/<case>/`:

- **P1 · The pilot** — `gherrink-galey`: pre-registration → the three-arm comparison (jigc · control · static-methodology) + the full-methodology dogfood, seeded failures planted mid-run. Gated by the **pilot gate** (substrate validated; verdict names ≥1 non-seeded observation) before P2/P3 proceed.
- **P2 · Existing-with-docs** — `gherrink-lacon`: ingest on the real corpus, task work referencing adopted docs, doc↔code genuinely adjudicating (Rust).
- **P3 · Greenfield, human-driven** — intent named at the pilot gate; the human at the wheel; capture identical.
- **Fix-what-hurt** — need-driven increments inserted between runs (each its own dev-workflow loop; never mid-run), recorded as "fixed because it hurt" with the run that surfaced it.

Then the [milestone-completion workflow](milestone-completion-workflow.md) audits the whole — the spine's last completion record states the thesis verdict with the future-work deferrals explicitly post-spine.
